# FPDS — the whole design system in one file

Generated by `npm run fpds:build` on 2026-10-27. Do not edit; edit the source files.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/README.md ===== -->

# FPDS — the FleetPanda Design System

FPDS is the design system distilled from what the **Unified View** (3PL, Sales, Service
Work) and the **Customer Portal** already do. Its one goal: **anyone who installs it — in
Lovable, Cursor, Claude or anywhere else — gets output that looks and behaves exactly
like those screens**, and is told clearly when what they want has no design yet.

Owner: Pavan Shetty · pavanshetty@fleetpanda.com

## The layers

| # | Layer | File | What it holds |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | Protocol | `00-protocol.md` | How to build anything: five checks (L1–L5) and the gap message |
| 1 | Foundations | `01-foundations.md` | Tokens — color, type, space, radius, stroke, elevation, motion, screen sizes, accessibility levels |
| 2 | Atoms | `02-atoms.md` | Single primitives — button, input, pill, status chip… |
| 3 | Molecules | `03-molecules.md` | Small parts with one job — search field, filter chip, date field, stat tile… |
| 4 | Units | `units/*.md` | Named regions of a screen, with names close to what you see — View row, Toolbar, Filter panel, Chip shelf, View bar, Data table, Board, Map, Record sheet, Lines table… |
| 5 | Templates | `05-templates.md` | Whole pages and sheets — List page, Record sheet, Directory record, Dashboard, Settings, App frame |
| 6 | Reference pages | `06-pages.md` | The real screens to copy |
| — | UX rules | `07-ux-rules.md` | Every decision, with its reason |
| — | Interaction rules | `08-interaction-rules.md` | How things respond — hover, staging, autosave, motion… |
| — | Not designed yet | `09-later.md` | UX copy, empty pages, highlight units, illustrations |
| — | Checks | `evals/README.md` | Five levels of evals, golden prompts |
| — | Misses log | `evals/misses.md` | Every miss the owner had to point out, its pattern and its guard — read before reporting |

Every atom, molecule, unit and template is a **spec card** with the same twelve sections
(`_spec-card-template.md`): Purpose · Use when / Don't use when · Anatomy · Look · States ·
Interactions · Outcomes · Data contract · Rules · Do / Don't · Checks · Reference.
`register.json` lists them all, generated from the cards.

## Install it

**Docs** — give the tool `docs/fpds/FPDS.md` (everything in one file) and its rules file:

| Tool | File |
|---|---|
| Cursor | `install/fpds.mdc` → `.cursor/rules/fpds.mdc` |
| Lovable | `install/lovable-knowledge.md` → Project settings › Knowledge |
| Claude / Claude Code | `install/CLAUDE.md` → project `CLAUDE.md` |
| Any other agent | `install/AGENTS.md` → project `AGENTS.md` |

**Code** — use the real code, never re-create it. **The org-level package is being set
up (2026-10-25); until it is published, copy each unit from the code path on its card
and the theme from `src/index.css`.** Once published, install from the registry (shadcn
format):

```bash
npx shadcn@latest add https://pavanshetty-rgb.github.io/FP-DS-2026/r/fpds-theme.json
npx shadcn@latest add https://pavanshetty-rgb.github.io/FP-DS-2026/r/fpds-data-table.json
```

Each card's id line names its registry item (`registry: fpds-…`).

## Working on FPDS (this repo)

```bash
npm run fpds:build     # cards → register.json, public/r registry, FPDS.md, install/
npm run eval           # all checks, incl. fpds-cards / -register / -units / -rules / -gaps
npm run fpds:resolve -- "describe a screen"   # what the protocol answers
```

The design reference page (`FP-DS-2026-design-reference.html`) is built from these same
files.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/00-protocol.md ===== -->

# FPDS — the build protocol

Every builder, person or AI (Lovable, Cursor, Claude, anything else), follows these
steps in order for every screen or change. The protocol is what makes the output come
out the same as the Unified View and the Customer Portal; skipping a step is how
screens drift.

## The five checks before anything is drawn

**L1 · Classify the request.** Write down, in one line each:
- the **page type** — list · board · map · record sheet (create / edit / view) ·
  dashboard · settings · directory record · portal page · something else;
- the **module** — 3PL · Sales · Service Work · Pricing · Lease · Customer Portal · new;
- the **audience** — tenant (every module except the Customer Portal) or customer
  (Customer Portal only — UX rules, Audience);
- the **record** it is about (order, customer, asset, part, invoice…), and whether that
  record already appears anywhere (`06-pages.md`).

**L2 · Match a template.** Look the page type up in `05-templates.md` and
`register.json` (`layer: "template"`, `contexts`). A list of anything is the
**List page**; a create/edit of anything is the **Record sheet** — even if the record is
new to the product. A template matches when its *Use when* describes the job, not when
the data is the same.

**L3 · Match every region to a unit.** Walk the template's regions (view row, toolbar,
chip shelf, view bar, table/board/map, sheet sections, rail…) and name the unit for
each from `units/`. Read each unit's *Use when / Don't use when* — a unit that says
"don't use when" for this job does not match.

**L4 · Try to compose.** For anything still unmatched, try to build it from existing
units, molecules and atoms **with their documented options only**: new columns, new
facets, new card fields, new statuses mapped to the existing kinds, new sections in a
record sheet, new rows in a lines table, a new dashboard tile — all of these are
*configuration*, not new design, and are allowed. Composition is not allowed to add a
new look (a color, a size, a shape, a layout) or a new behavior (a gesture, a state,
a motion) that no card describes.

**L5 · Check the rules.** Read the composed result against `07-ux-rules.md` and
`08-interaction-rules.md`. If it would break a rule, it is not a match.

Only a region that fails **all five** checks is a gap. Everything else gets built.

## When something is a gap

Build every region that passed. For the region that did not, do **not** invent a
design. Put this message where the region would be (and in the reply), word for word,
with the four fields filled in:

> **This unit needs a tweak in design/new design. Please contact the owner to get a
> design for this gap, pavanshetty@fleetpanda.com.**
>
> - **Use case:** <what the user is trying to do, in their words>
> - **Missing component:** <the region / unit / behavior that has no design>
> - **Closest existing:** <the nearest unit or template, and why it does not fit>
> - **Checks run:** L1 <page type, module, audience> · L2 <template tried> ·
>   L3 <units tried> · L4 <composition tried> · L5 <rule it would break>

A partial gap is still a gap: a list page whose table needs a cell type that does not
exist gets the whole list built, with the message in place of that one cell.

Two things are **never** gaps — they are always answered from the docs:
- a new record, module or field on an existing page type (L2/L4 cover it);
- a screen size, theme or accessibility level (Foundations cover it).

## Areas not designed yet

Until they are written, these are answered with the gap message (field *Missing
component* names the area): **UX copy** beyond the rules in `07-ux-rules.md`, **empty
pages and empty states** beyond the Empty-state molecule, **highlight units** beyond the
Alert banner and status/awaiting pills, and **illustrations** (see `09-later.md`).

## After building

Run the checks in `evals/README.md` that apply (`fpds-*` always; the repo's `npm run
eval` when working in this repo). A failing check is fixed, not explained.

Then look before you report (UX-164): **every stage** the screen appears in (draft,
assigned, completed…), **a blank and a filled row**, **light and dark**, and the
**edges** — last rows, card rims, repeated values. Read the patterns in
`evals/misses.md`; if the owner has to point something out, log it there.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/01-foundations.md ===== -->

# FPDS · 01 Foundations

`layer: foundation` · `code: src/index.css · src/components/theme/a11y.ts · prototypes/unified-view/statusKind.ts · src/components/ui/term-icons.tsx`

The values every atom, molecule and unit is built from. **Code is the source of
truth**: every value below is read from `src/index.css` (and `a11y.ts` for the
Accessibility panel). If this page and the CSS disagree, the CSS wins and this
page is wrong. Older docs (`docs/ds/ui/1-fundamentals.md`) still list some
pre-2026-10-21 dark values; the dark column here is the current one.

**How to use a token.** Always by its Tailwind utility (`bg-card`,
`text-muted-foreground`, `border-fp-outline-1`, `bg-fp-board-lane`). Never a hex,
never `rgb()`, never an arbitrary `[#…]` value. Opacity modifiers on a token are
fine (`bg-status-success/10`, `border-foreground/25`).

**Themes.** Light is `:root`; Dark is `<html class="dark">`. The Accessibility
panel then mixes each token toward two further points per theme — **WCAG**
(`.wcag:not(.dark)`, `.dark.wcag`) and **Maximum** (`.a11y-max:not(.dark)`,
`.dark.a11y-max`). Light / Dark is chosen in that panel (no header theme icon).

---

## 1. Color — two layers

**Layer 1 — primitives** (`--fp-ink-*`, `--fp-surface-*`, `--fp-card-*`,
`--fp-outline-*`, `--fp-stroke-*`, `--fp-emphasis*`, `--fp-selected*`,
`--fp-danger`, `--fp-shell-*`, `--fp-lane-*`). The only place a color literal is
written. **Layer 2 — semantics** (`--background`, `--card`, `--selected`,
`--border`, …) alias primitives and carry no literals of their own. Build with
Layer 2 wherever a semantic exists; reach for a primitive utility
(`bg-fp-card-1`, `border-fp-outline-2`) only where the DS itself does.

### 1.1 Surfaces and layers

| Utility | Token | Light | Dark | Means / use |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bg-background` · `bg-rail` · `bg-fp-surface-base` | `--background` → `--fp-surface-base` | `#efefeb` | `#07080a` | The app ground. Warm gray under cool paper in light — the temperature does the separating. The rail is the ground and disappears into it |
| `bg-fp-shell-ground` | `--fp-shell-ground` | `#f2f2f2` | `#07080a` | The ground the whole app sits on, top bar included (the top bar has no fill of its own) |
| `bg-fp-shell-panel` | `--fp-shell-panel` | `#fbfbfb` | `#23282f` | The navigation's box |
| `bg-fp-shell-selected` | `--fp-shell-selected` | `#f1f0ec` | `#2e343e` | The selected page in the nav |
| `bg-fp-surface-1b` | `--fp-surface-1b` | `#ffffff` | `#1d222a` | **The content layer**, and the fill inside every field (fields are white wells) |
| `bg-popover` | `--popover` → surface-1b | `#ffffff` | `#1d222a` | Anything that floats: menus, popovers, dialogs, sheets, toasts |
| `bg-card` · `bg-fp-surface-1a` | `--card` → `--fp-surface-1a` | `#f4f5f8` | `#272c35` | Card / inner panel — one step under the content layer |
| `bg-drawer` | `--drawer` | `#fafbf8` | `#272c35` | Tier-2 drawer (two-layer nav only) |
| `bg-sidebar` | `--sidebar` | `#fafbf8` | `#181c22` | Sidebar surface. Deliberately a quiet edge in light |
| `bg-fp-card-1` | `--fp-card-1` | `#e9ecf1` | `#2e343e` | Raised inner card, inset block, pressed tint |
| `bg-fp-card-2` (via `--muted` in dark) | `--fp-card-2` | `#f4f5f8` | `#23282f` | The quieter card step |
| `--fp-card-3` | `--fp-card-3` | `#fafafa` | `#21262d` | The faintest step |
| `bg-fp-board-lane` | `--fp-board-lane` | `#e9ecf1` (card-1) | `#07080a` (ground) | **Board lane ground — one token, every board.** Light darkened one step on 2026-10-22 for washed-out monitors; in dark the lane is a well, so cards sit lighter on it |
| `bg-fp-lane` | `--fp-lane` | `#f4f5f8` | `#2e343e` | Band behind a **grouped table's** header row. Not a board lane |
| `bg-fp-table-header` | `--fp-table-header` | `#fafafa` | `#23282f` | Every table header row; also the hanging add and the selected pagination page |
| `bg-fp-row-hover` | `--fp-row-hover` | `#f4f5f8` | `#252a32` | Table row hover. Differs from the header on purpose |
| `bg-fp-row-selected` | `--fp-row-selected` → `--selected` | `#fee8d9` | `#2e343e` | A ticked row |
| `.fp-sorted-cell` | mix of row hover 55% | — | — | Cells of the sorted column (half the row hover) |
| `bg-chip` | `--chip` | `#efefeb` | `#2e343e` | Chip fill, active view icon, sorted header (`th[aria-sort]`) |
| `bg-muted` | `--muted` | `#f4f4f5` | `#23282f` | Neutral pill tint, removable chips, the on state of segment controls |
| `bg-secondary` | `--secondary` | `#f4f4f5` | `#2e343e` | Secondary button fill |
| `bg-fp-field-fill` / `-hover` | `--fp-field-fill` | `#c4c4c9` @30% / 50% | `#474f5b` @30% / 50% | Dark-theme fill of fields and outline buttons (`dark:bg-fp-field-fill`) |
| `bg-fp-switch-track` | `--fp-switch-track` | `#c4c4c9` | `#474f5b` @80% | A switch's off track |

**Dark frame** (Accessibility, light theme, Contrast ≥ 80): `--fp-frame-ground #232327`, `--fp-frame-selected #2b2b31`, `--fp-frame-ink #ffffff`, `--fp-frame-ink-muted #d0d4d9`, `--fp-frame-rim #55555e`. Anything new that sits straight on the ground carries `data-fp-on-frame`.

### 1.6 Status — kinds, tones, pills

A module declares **kinds**; `KIND_TONE` in `statusKind.ts` is the only place a kind meets a `StatusChip` tone. The pill draws the kind.

| Kind | Means | Tone | Pill classes | Light text | Dark text |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `caution` | Waiting on somebody — pending, awaiting, Unassigned, Paused | `processing` | `bg-fp-banner-accent-caution/10 text-fp-banner-accent-caution` | `#b45309` | `#fcd34d` |
| `info` | Simply true, asks nothing — Assigned, Scheduled, Active | `info` | `bg-fp-banner-accent-info/10 text-fp-banner-accent-info` | `#1d4ed8` | `#a5b4fc` |
| `success` | Done — Completed, Delivered, Synced | `success` | `bg-status-success/10 text-status-success` | `#15803d` | `#22c55e` |
| `critical` | A warning — Blocked, Declined, Over limit | `failed` | `bg-status-failed/10 text-status-failed` | `#dc2626` | `#f87171` |
| `neutral` | Neither — Draft, Canceled, Ended, Inactive | `neutral` | `bg-muted text-muted-foreground` | `#596475` | `#a0a7b0` |

Active / inactive records: `ACTIVE_KIND(active)` → `info` / `neutral`.

Other status tokens: `--status-processing` `#b45309` / `#f59e0b`; `--status-pink` `#db2777` / `#ff8080`; `--verified` `#2563eb` / `#3b82f6` (white text on it); `--status-success-foreground` `#ffffff` / `#052e16` (ink on a green FILL); `--destructive` `#ef4444` / `#f87171` (white text on it in both).

### 1.8 Accessibility levels (WCAG and Maximum)

The panel's **Stroke contrast** and **Contrast** sliders each run (text follows Contrast — no Text color slider since 2026-10-24) Default (0) → WCAG (50, unlabelled middle) → Maximum (100). `a11y.ts` mixes each token between the two points either side. WCAG = text AAA 7:1 on every surface, controls / graphics / every rim and divider AA 3:1+, surfaces a full step apart. Maximum = rims 5:1+, text still 7:1 on the darker surfaces; in light, **whites stay white and grays go dark**.

| Token | WCAG light | WCAG dark | Max light | Max dark |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `--fp-ink-1` / `-2` | = theme | = theme | `#000000` | `#ffffff` |
| `--fp-ink-3` | `#3a424e` | `#cfd3d8` | `#08090a` | `#e6e8eb` |
| `--fp-surface-base` | `#e4e4dd` | `#040506` | `#9a9a9a` | `#000000` |
| `--fp-shell-ground` | `#e6e6e1` | = base | `#9a9a9a` | = base |
| `--fp-shell-selected` | `#dedcd6` | = card-1 | `#9a9aa0` | = card-1 |
| `--fp-surface-1a` | `#eef0f4` | `#30363f` | `#c0c4ca` | `#3b434e` |
| `--fp-card-1` | `#e2e6ed` | `#373e48` | `#adb2b9` | `#424b57` |
| `--fp-card-2` | `#eef0f4` | `#2b3038` | `#c0c4ca` | `#333b45` |
| `--fp-card-3` | `#f3f4f7` | `#282d34` | `#cfd2d6` | `#2f363f` |
| `--fp-emphasis` (`--accent`) | `#dfe2e8` | `#373e48` | `#b2b7bd` | `#424b57` |
| `--fp-emphasis-hover` | `#e6e9ef` | `#404853` | `#bbbfc5` | `#4d5764` |
| `--fp-table-header` | `#e9ecf1` | = card-2 | `#b7bcc2` | = card-2 |
| `--fp-board-lane` | `#e9ecf1` | = base | `#adb2b9` | = base |
| `--fp-row-hover` | `#e6e9ef` | `#2d333b` | `#cbcfd4` | `#363e48` |
| `--fp-outline-1` · `--fp-stroke-hairline` | `#7c7c86` | `#7d899c` | `#2a2a30` | `#a3aebe` |
| `--fp-stroke-field` · `--fp-outline-2` | `#5f5f69` | `#9aa5b6` | `#121216` | `#b9c3d2` |
| `--fp-selected-border` | `#d35400` | `#636f80` | `#7a3100` | `#8b96a8` |
| `--fp-selected-strong` | `#873400` | = theme | `#301200` | = theme |
| `--ring` | `#853500` | = brand | `#2f1300` | = brand |
| `--fp-brand-ink` | `#853500` | `#ffc7a2` | `#2f1300` | `#ffe2cf` |
| `--status-success` | `#0e5328` | `#7be8a3` | `#020b05` | `#bdf4d1` |
| `--status-processing` | `#763606` | `#f9cc7d` | `#100701` | `#fce5bd` |
| `--fp-danger` (`--status-failed`) | `#8c1515` | `#fcc5c5` | `#160303` | `#fee1e1` |
| `--verified` | `#103ea6` | `#bcd4fc` | `#061841` | `#dde9fe` |
| `--accent-violet` | `#4f0de6` | `#dacbfc` | `#1e0557` | `#ece5fd` |
| `--fp-banner-accent-info` | `#173ca8` | `#b4c0fd` | `#091741` | `#c8d1fe` |
| `--fp-banner-accent-caution` | `#763606` | = theme | `#100701` | = theme |
| `--fp-trend-up` / `-down` | `#1f5c3e` / `#81344b` | `#88d9b1` / `#eeb1c2` | `#0a1e15` / `#2d121a` | `#a8e3c6` / `#f3c8d4` |

Lane accents move too (WCAG light: yellow `#a67f06`, green `#1a9848`, cyan `#0592aa`, pink `#c026d3`, orange `#b97708`, red `#e11d48`; Maximum light: gray `#515e71`, blue `#0a55d0`, yellow `#755a04`, green `#126c33`, cyan `#036778`, purple `#3f42ee`, pink `#9c1fac`, orange `#825406`, red `#b7183b`). **Text on a color fill** (`ON_FILL`) is chosen, never blended: white or dark, whichever reads better (WCAG dark sets these to `#0f1216`). **The brand fill never moves** — only `--fp-brand-ink` and `--ring` do.

### Rules — color

1. **Tokens only; a new color joins the token layer** with a light value, a dark value, WCAG light + dark values, a pair in `scripts/eval/wcag-contrast.mjs`, and a place in `a11y.ts` (STROKE, BACKGROUND, INK or ON_FILL). — *A color outside the layer cannot follow the theme or the Accessibility panel, and earlier AI work hallucinated values read off screenshots.* (constitution; DS ui §10n)
2. **The default CTA is `bg-primary`, never `bg-brand`.** — *Orange is the product's mark; as a CTA it would compete with meaning on every screen.* (constitution §2)
3. **Brand says "you chose this"; violet highlights; gray `--accent` says "the pointer is here".** The three never trade jobs. — *Hover and chosen shared one gray once and a hovered segment looked selected. Purple had been used ad hoc in two hues; Pavan wanted one accent.* (DS ui §10c, 2026-10-16)
4. **Dark selects in gray, light in brand tint.** Use `bg-selected` and let the theme decide. — *An orange dark enough to sit on near-black has gone brown, which the product does not own.*
5. **Navigation state is neutral** (`bg-sidebar-accent`, `bg-fp-shell-selected`), never brand. — *The page you are on is a report, not a choice; the loudest color was saying the least.*
6. **A status is coloured by its KIND, declared as kinds, never tones.** — *"Assigned" came out green, amber and blue in three modules when authors picked swatches.* (DS ui §10b, 2026-09-23)
7. **Red only for a warning.** Types, categories, plain lanes and Canceled are never red. — *A lane of red badges is the loudest thing on the board saying the least.* (2026-10-16)
8. **Being active is `info`, not `success`.** — *Nothing has been achieved by a customer existing; green is kept for done.*
9. **A card's rim is `border-fp-outline-1`; a divider is `border-border`; a field is `border-input`; hover rims go to `border-fp-outline-2`.** The hover rim must stay darker than the resting rim. — *A "stronger" rim that measured weaker made every Kanban card get lighter on hover.*
10. **Never fill with a stroke token.** Fields in dark use `dark:bg-fp-field-fill`; a switch's off track `bg-fp-switch-track`. — *Stroke contrast must move strokes only.* (2026-10-21)
11. **Never raise a stroke to make something read** — fix spacing or the surface step. — *A darker line is the wrong answer to a layout problem.*
12. **Board lanes use `bg-fp-board-lane`, grouped-table bands `bg-fp-lane`.** Two tokens even when equal. — *Retuning one must never drag the other.*
13. **Categorical hues are the same in both themes.** — *A series that changes color with the theme changes identity.*
14. **Orange TEXT uses `text-brand` (→ `--fp-brand-ink`); the brand fill is never moved by an accessibility setting.** — *Text color once recoloured the logo.* (2026-10-21)

---

## 2. Type

**Font:** Geist (variable, 100–900 loaded), `font-sans`. Weights used: 400 regular, 500 `font-medium`, 600 `font-semibold`, 700 only in the file-type tag. **All numbers that compare** (`tabular-nums`): counts, money, dates in tables, percentages.

| Utility | Size / line | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `text-[7px]` | 7px | File-type tag letters only |
| `text-[9px]` · `text-[10px]` · `text-[11px]` | 9 · 10 · 11px | Token initial badge · filter hover-card heading (uppercase, `tracking-[0.12em]`) · time-scale ticks |
| `text-xs` | 12 / 16 | Pills and chips, sub-lines, captions, tooltip, table nested headers, view-bar tool names |
| `text-sm` | 14 / 20 | **The UI default**: buttons, menu items, table cells, labels, field text on desktop (`md:text-sm`) |
| `text-base` | 16 / 24 | Field text below `md`; card / breakdown titles; group headings in stepped forms |
| `text-lg` | 18 / 28 | Dialog title, empty-state title |
| `text-3xl` | 30 / 36 | Stat tile figure (`font-semibold tabular-nums`) |
| `text-heading-sm` · `-md` · `-lg` · `-xl` | 24/1.3 · 36/1.2 · 48/1.1 · 60/1.05 | Display headings (semibold) |

**Text size (Accessibility):** `.a11y-text` scales `--text-xs/sm/base/lg` by `--fp-text-scale` (1–1.3) and adds weight `(scale − 1) × 250`; the fixed 7/9/10/11/13px sizes scale too; text in table cells, board lanes, sheets and the nav wraps instead of truncating (buttons stay one line); boxes keep their width.

### Rules — type

1. **Sizes from the scale only.** — *Scale discipline is a constitution rule.*
2. **Pills, chips and sub-lines are `text-xs` regular; cells and controls `text-sm`.** — *One recipe makes a status read the same in a cell, a sheet header and on a card.*
3. **Numbers are `tabular-nums`.** — *Digits line up and the eye reads magnitude.*
4. **Fields capitalise the first letter by default** (`autoCapitalize="sentences"`), except email, URL, password, number, tel. — *The value stored is the value seen.*
5. **Text grows; boxes don't.** No width follows Text size (`--fp-scale`, `--fp-nav-k`, `--fp-lane-k` stay 1); text wraps and heights grow. Bigger boxes = Display size. — *"Only the text size should increase without the card size increasing."* (2026-10-23)

---

## 3. Spacing

Tailwind's 4px step (`--spacing: 0.25rem`). FP-named spacing works on any spacing utility:

| Token | Value | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `container-padding-x` | 24px | Page side padding |
| `section-padding-y` | 96px | Marketing-scale section padding (showcase) |
| `section-title-gap-sm` / `-md` / `-lg` / `-xl` | 16 / 20 / 20 / 24px | Title → content gap |

Fixed spacings the screens rely on:

| Where | Value |
|---|---|
| Between fields in one row | **24px** (`gap-6`); 16px only in a dense table-like grid |
| Content inset inside a flush box | **16px** |
| Between groups in a stepped form | 40px |
| Toolbar left cluster / right cluster | `gap-3` / `gap-1` |
| Chips on a shelf | `gap-2`; inside a pill `px-3` |
| Sheet gap from window edge | 56px (112px for a sheet over a sheet) |

### Rules — spacing

1. **A row of fields fills its row** — `1fr` / `flex-1` shares, 24px apart, last field ending at the container padding. No fixed px container around fields unless commented `FIXED WIDTH` (dates, times, the 320px list search). — *A fixed 340px column left a third of a grown sheet empty.* (DS ui §10e, 2026-10-16)
2. **Flush means the box meets the edge; content keeps a 16px inset.** — *A chip touching the edge reads as cut off.* (2026-09-25)
3. **Tables fill the full content width**; never a `max-w` cap. — *"Why was it built without using the full space?"* (2026-10-07)

---

## 4. Radius

`--radius: 0.625rem` (10px). Ramp:

| Utility | px | Use |
|---|---|---|
| `rounded-xs` | 2 | Dialog close focus box |
| `rounded-sm` | 6 | Menu / select items, kbd, small icon buttons in chips |
| `rounded-md` | 8 | **Buttons, inputs, selects, popovers, menus, tooltips, toggles, content layer** |
| `rounded-lg` | 10 | **Cards, dialogs, alert dialogs, tab list, metric tile, attention outline** |
| `rounded-xl` | 14 | Dashboard cards and their ground |
| `rounded-2xl` · `3xl` · `4xl` | 18 · 22 · 26 | On the ramp; rarely used |
| `rounded-full` | — | Pills, chips, avatars, switches, radios, discs, progress, slider |
| `rounded-[4px]` | 4 | Checkbox only (DS code) |

### Rules — radius

1. **Controls `rounded-md`, cards `rounded-lg`, pills `rounded-full`.** — *Scale discipline.*
2. **A hanging add is square on top, rounded below** (`rounded-t-none rounded-b-md`). — *Its top edge is the card's bottom edge; a radius there notches.*

---

## 5. Stroke widths

| Width | Where |
|---|---|
| 1px | Every border: fields, cards, dividers, popovers, dialogs |
| 2px ring | Focus: `focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50` + `border-ring` |
| 2px outline, offset 2 | `.fp-attention` (offset 1 for `.fp-attention-info`) |
| 2px (`h-0.5`) | Stage-track line, nested-row green rail (`status-success/70`), tab underline, module-switch progress bar |
| 2px border | Current-stage pill in `StageTrack` track / compact |
| 5px border | `StageTrack` rings (current = violet, upcoming = `fp-outline-2`) |
| 1.5px / 2px | `OutlineShine` ring default / on banner CTAs |
| Icon line 2 / 1.75 | Lucide default / `MetricIcon` |

---

## 6. Elevation

| Utility | Value | Use |
|---|---|---|
| none | — | **The shell** (rail, nav, top bar, content layer) and **every control** (inputs, buttons, toggles, tabs, checkboxes, switches, slider handles, pagination) |
| `shadow-xs` | Tailwind | Dashboard cards |
| `shadow-md` | Tailwind | Menus, popovers, select lists, hover cards |
| `shadow-lg` | Tailwind | Dialogs, alert dialogs, sheets |
| `shadow-fp-surface` | `0 4px 4px 0 rgb(0 0 0 / 0.15)` | Something that genuinely floats over content (a hovered card) |
| `shadow-fp-overlay` | `0 8px 10px -6px` + `0 20px 25px -5px`, black 10% | The tier above content — the bulk-action bar. Pair with `border-fp-outline-1 dark:border-fp-outline-2` |
| Scrim | `bg-black/50` | Behind dialogs and sheets |

### Rules — elevation

1. **Nothing in the shell casts a shadow; layers separate by value.** — *A shadow on top of a real value step is a second answer to a question already answered.*
2. **Controls cast no shadow — their border is the edge.** (2026-10-14)
3. **Elevation steps once per level**: `fp-surface-1b → card → fp-card-1`.
4. **On dark, elevation is a lighter rim, not a darker shadow.** — *Black on a dark surface has almost no range.*

---

## 7. Motion

| Motion | Class / where | Duration · easing | Reduced motion |
|---|---|---|---|
| Skeleton shimmer — a light sweeping left → right | `.fp-shimmer` (Skeleton) | 1.4s ease-in-out, infinite | Off |
| Urgent blink — tank ≤ 10% | `.fp-urgent-blink` | 1.4s, opacity 1 → .35, infinite | Steady |
| Attention ring — "it is THIS one" | `.fp-attention` | 600ms, **once** | Ring held steady 1.2s |
| Attention, info blue — chips just became clickable | `.fp-attention-info` | 600ms, once | Held 1.2s |
| Attention fill — a grid column | `.fp-attention-fill` | 600ms, once (ring 16%) | Held 1.2s |
| Attention fill, subtle — landing on a form section | `.fp-attention-fill-subtle` | 800ms, once (foreground 5%) | Off |
| Outline shine — a band sweeping the outline | `OutlineShine` | 1.9s lap every 6s, first after 1.2s, `cubic-bezier(0.45,0,0.55,1)`, band skewed −24° | Still |
| Pill sweep — the shine through a pill's face | `OutlineShine fill` (AlertBanner pills, collapsed only) | same | Still |
| Module-switch progress | `.fp-nav-progress` | 3000ms linear | — |
| Nav fold → move → unfold | ModuleSidebar | 200 → 260 → 240ms, `cubic-bezier(0.2,0,0,1)` | None |
| Bulk-action bar in | `.bulk-bar-in` | 200ms ease-out, translateY 1rem | — |
| Overlays in / out | `animate-in fade-in-0 zoom-in-95` + side slide | Dialog 200ms; sheet open 500 / close 300ms | — |
| Stage change | `StageTrack` | line 500ms ease-out; pill fades + zooms in after 300ms; held 900ms | `motion-reduce:` none |
| Name slide-out (view-bar tools, hanging add) | width / opacity | 200ms | — |
| Copy confirm | `CopyValue` | tick shows 1.4s | — |
| Check pop | `.module-check-pop` | 320ms `cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1)` | — |

### Rules — motion

1. **Every looping or attention motion has a reduced-motion form** that keeps the point (a held ring) or stops. — *Somebody who asked for less motion still has to be told which section they were sent to.*
2. **Attention blinks once, never twice.** — *Two flashes is a thing asking to be watched; one points and gets out of the way.* (2026-09-22)
3. **A skeleton shimmers; it never pulses.** (2026-10-10)
4. **In an AlertBanner every pill sweeps (while collapsed) and every CTA shines; screens never add it.** (DS ui §10l, 2026-10-20)
5. **Never animate on first paint** of a record (StageTrack). — *Opening a record is not a stage changing.*

---

## 8. Screen sizes and zoom

| Token / setting | Value | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| Display size (Accessibility) | ×0.5 · ×0.75 · **×1** · ×1.25 · ×1.5 (100% in the middle) | One CSS zoom on `<html>`; sets `--fp-zoom` |
| `--fp-vw` · `--fp-vh` | `100vw / zoom` · `100dvh / zoom` | **Anything sized to the window** |
| `html.a11y-zoom body` | `min-width: 1280px` | The app's narrowest layout; a zoomed page scrolls sideways (visible 12px bar) instead of clipping |
| `--fp-text-scale` | 1 – 1.3 | Text size |
| `--fp-scale` | 1+ | Fixed widths inside sheets and their table columns |
| `--fp-nav-k` · `--fp-nav-width` | 1+ · `15rem × k` (240px) | The one-panel nav; collapsed rail 56px |
| `--fp-lane-k` · `--fp-board-lane-width` · `--fp-board-column-width` | 1+ · `400px × k` · `320px × k` | Board lanes |

### Rules — screen

1. **Never `100vw`, `100vh` or `h-screen`;** use `var(--fp-vw)` / `var(--fp-vh)`. — *A zoomed page multiplies viewport units too.*
2. **A new kind of fixed-width unit is added to `UNITS` in `a11y.ts`** so it is fitted when text grows.
3. **No scrollbars by default** (`scrollbar-width: none` on `*`) — the clipped edge says there is more. — *A gutter ate half a lane's padding on one side.*

---

## 9. Icons

- **Lucide only** (`lucide-react`). Size via `size-*`: **16px (`size-4`) default** in buttons, menus, select; `size-3` in xs buttons and chip ✕; `size-3.5` in copy / clear; `size-6` in empty media; `size-7` in `MetricIcon`. Line 2 (Lucide default).
- Icons inside a control inherit color; a bare icon in a field or menu row is `text-muted-foreground` unless it names its own color.
- **Fixed glyphs:** search `Search`; column search `TextSearch`; clear `X`; add `Plus`; sort `ArrowUp` / `ArrowDown`, unsorted `ChevronsUpDown`; display `Table2` · `KanbanSquare` · `Map`; update view `RefreshCw`; select / combobox `ChevronDown`; date `Calendar`; warning `TriangleAlert`; battery `Battery*`; Accessibility `AccessibilityIcon` (`src/components/theme`).
- **Term icons** (`src/components/ui/term-icons.tsx`, `TERM_ICONS`): deliveries `TruckIcon`, products `DropIcon` (~60% wavy fluid), gallons `DrumIcon` (fuel drum ~70% wavy fluid), ship-tos `PinIcon`. Style: Lucide 24 grid, round caps / joins, `currentColor`, soft fill of its own color at 22%, liquids fill to a **wave**.

### Rules — icons

1. **Lucide only; never Tabler, Hugeicons, Phosphor, Remix, emoji or one-off SVG.** A missing glyph is an inline SVG in `currentColor`. (constitution §3)
2. **One icon per term, every module.** Wherever the app names deliveries, products, gallons or ship-tos it draws that icon and no other. (DS ui §10k, 2026-10-20)
3. **A new term icon follows the term-icon style** and is added to `TERM_ICONS`.

---

## Never

- ❌ A raw hex, `rgb()`, `hsl()` or arbitrary color (`bg-[#…]`, `text-[#…]`) in a component. Eval `tokens`.
- ❌ `bg-brand` as the default CTA, or orange on hover, selected rows, links or nav state.
- ❌ A second violet, or using `--accent` (gray hover) to mean the accent.
- ❌ A status map written in tones or hues (`processing`, `amber`) instead of kinds. Eval `status-kinds`.
- ❌ Red for a type, category, plain lane or Canceled.
- ❌ A radius off the ramp (`rounded-[5px]`, `rounded-[12px]`); a type size off the scale.
- ❌ A shadow on the shell or on a control; `shadow-md` invented as a token (there is no `--shadow-md` token).
- ❌ A fill made from a stroke token (`bg-input/30`, `bg-border` as a surface).
- ❌ `100vw` / `100vh` / `h-screen`; a fixed px container around fields without `FIXED WIDTH`. Eval `fields-fill`.
- ❌ A visible scrollbar.
- ❌ An icon from any set other than Lucide; a different icon for a term in `TERM_ICONS`.
- ❌ A new color token without WCAG light/dark values and an `a11y.ts` slot. Eval `wcag-contrast`.
- ❌ An animation without a reduced-motion form; an attention blink that repeats.
- ❌ Values read off a screenshot or Figma when the code has them.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/02-atoms.md ===== -->

# FPDS · 02 Atoms

Single primitives from `src/components/ui/`. Each card uses the spec-card
sections (`_spec-card-template.md`) at `###` level. Values are token names from
[`01-foundations.md`](01-foundations.md); a px size appears only where the code
fixes one.

**Shared by every focusable atom:** focus is `focus-visible:border-ring
focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50` (brand-orange ring); disabled is
`opacity-50` + no pointer events; invalid (`aria-invalid`) is `border-destructive`
+ `ring-destructive/20` (`/40` dark). Controls cast no shadow.

---

# Actions

## Button

`id: atom.button` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/button.tsx` · `registry: fpds-button`

### Purpose
The one pressable action. Says what happens next.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a press does something: commit, open, add, export.
- Don't use for a two-state choice (→ `atom.toggle` / `atom.toggle-group`), a chip (→ `atom.pill`), or an add on a table (→ `molecule.hanging-add`).

### Anatomy
Optional leading Lucide icon (16px) · label · optional trailing icon. `asChild` renders the look on another element.

### Look
`rounded-md text-sm font-medium gap-2`, no shadow.

| Variant | Classes |
|---|---|
| `default` | `bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary/90` — **the CTA** |
| `outline` | `border border-input bg-transparent hover:bg-accent`; dark `bg-fp-field-fill` |
| `secondary` | `bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-secondary/80` |
| `ghost` | no fill; `hover:bg-accent` (`/50` dark) |
| `destructive` | `bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground` (`/60` dark) — final guarded confirm only |
| `success` | `bg-status-success text-status-success-foreground` — the one act that moves something forward |
| `link` | `text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline` |

| Size | Height | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `xs` | 24px (`h-6`) | `text-xs`, icon 12px |
| `sm` | 32px (`h-8`) | toolbars, dialog footers in panels |
| `default` | 36px (`h-9`) | forms, sheet footers |
| `lg` | 40px (`h-10`) | |
| `icon-xs` / `icon-sm` / `icon` / `icon-lg` | 24 / 32 / 36 / 40px square | icon only — needs `aria-label` |

### States
default · hover (variant hover fill) · focus (ring) · disabled (50%) · invalid (destructive rim).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click / Enter / Space | Runs `onClick` |
| Tab | Focus ring appears |

### Outcomes
The act runs; a toast may confirm it (`molecule.toast`). A primary that cannot act yet stays pressable and says why (pending bar), rather than going dead.

### Data contract
`variant`, `size`, `asChild`, native button props. Emits `onClick`.

### Rules
1. **One primary (`default`) per view; never `bg-brand` as a CTA.** — *Orange is the mark; the CTA is neutral.* `(constitution §2)`
2. **Outline buttons are `bg-transparent`, never `bg-background`.** — *`--background` is the gray app ground; on the white layer it painted 51 gray buttons.*
3. **Destructive fill only on the last, guarded confirm** (armed second press or AlertDialog). Menu rows and row actions color the label only. — *At that point the fill is the warning.* `(DS ui §9)`
4. **A sheet footer's primary is the next act** ("Create order"), never Save / Update / Cancel on an autosaving sheet. `(feedback_ui_rules)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<Button>Create order</Button>` in the footer; `<Button variant="outline" size="sm">` in a toolbar.
- ❌ `className="bg-brand"` on a CTA; a red-filled Delete in a row menu.

### Checks
`tokens`. By eye: one primary per view; icon-only buttons have `aria-label` and a tooltip.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order sheet footer (Create order); every list toolbar (Export, Filters).

---

## Toggle

`id: atom.toggle` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/toggle.tsx` · `registry: fpds-toggle`

### Purpose
A single on/off pressable — a button that stays pressed.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for a single view option that is on or off.
- Don't use for a setting in a form (→ `atom.switch` in a menu, `atom.checkbox` in a table/form); for one of several (→ `atom.toggle-group`).

### Anatomy
Icon and/or label inside a pressable box.

### Look
`rounded-md text-sm font-medium`. `default`: `bg-transparent`; `outline`: `border border-input`. Sizes `sm` 32px · `default` 36px · `lg` 40px (min-width = height). **On is greyscale:** `bg-muted text-foreground`, outline adds `border-foreground/25`.

### States
rest (bare) · hover (`bg-accent`) · on (`bg-muted`, unchanged on hover) · focus · disabled.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click / Space | Flips `pressed` |

### Outcomes
The option it names turns on or off.

### Data contract
`pressed`, `onPressedChange`, `variant`, `size`.

### Rules
1. **Segment and toggle "on" are greyscale — no brand, no accent.** — *Every segment control, every module (Pavan, 2026-10-16).*
2. **Hover and on never look the same.** — *They were pixel-identical once and neither read.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Gray on state.
- ❌ `data-[state=on]:bg-brand`.

### Checks
`tokens`. By eye: hover ≠ on.

### Reference
DS showcase › Toggle (no module uses a lone toggle today).

---

## Toggle group

`id: atom.toggle-group` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/toggle-group.tsx` · `registry: fpds-toggle-group`

### Purpose
A segment control: pick one (or several) of a few short options that change a view.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for 2–5 short scopes on a dashboard or list (line of service, version).
- Don't use for a form field with many options (→ `atom.select` / `molecule.combobox`), or for switching between views of one record (→ `atom.tabs`). Not for line-of-service on lists — that is a filter.

### Anatomy
Root (`type="single" | "multiple"`) · items (`ToggleGroupItem`), joined when `spacing=0`.

### Look
Items take the Toggle recipe, `px-3`. `spacing=0`: inner corners square, first `rounded-l-md`, last `rounded-r-md`; outline items share borders (`border-l-0`, first keeps `border-l`). On = `bg-muted text-foreground` + `border-foreground/25` (outline).

### States
As Toggle; exactly one on for `single`.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click an item | It turns on; the previous turns off (`single`) |
| Arrow keys | Move focus between items |

### Outcomes
The view re-scopes.

### Data contract
`type`, `value`, `onValueChange` (guard empty: `v && set(v)`), `variant`, `size`, `spacing`.

### Rules
1. **Greyscale on state.** `(2026-10-16)`
2. **A single-select group never empties** — ignore the empty value. — *A segment with nothing chosen says nothing.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<ToggleGroup type="single" size="sm" variant="outline">` for Transport / Tank wagon on the dashboard.
- ❌ A brand-filled active segment.

### Checks
`tokens`. By eye: one item always on.

### Reference
3PL › Dashboard (line scope); 3PL › Recurring Orders.

---

# Inputs

## Input

`id: atom.input` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/input.tsx` · `registry: fpds-input`

### Purpose
A one-line text or number field.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for free text, a number, an email, a phone.
- Don't use for prose (→ `atom.textarea`), a fixed list (→ `atom.select` / `molecule.combobox`), a date (→ `molecule.date-field`), or a read-only value (→ `molecule.copy-value`). Inside an editable table use the `TableCell*` cells, never a bare Input.

### Anatomy
Box · value / placeholder. Leading icon and trailing clear are added by the molecule (`molecule.search-field`).

### Look
`h-9 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-fp-surface-1b px-3 py-1`, `text-base` → `md:text-sm`, placeholder `text-muted-foreground`; dark `bg-fp-field-fill`. Filled white, not transparent.

### States
default · focus (`border-ring` + ring) · disabled (50%) · invalid (destructive rim) · placeholder.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Type | Value changes |
| Tab | Focus ring |

### Outcomes
A value; on an existing record's sheet, autosaved.

### Data contract
Native input props. `autoCapitalize` defaults to `sentences`, `none` for email / url / password / number / tel / numeric `inputMode`.

### Rules
1. **Fields are filled white (`bg-fp-surface-1b`), never transparent.** — *Transparent fields were findable only by their border.*
2. **Fields in a row share the row** (`1fr`/`flex-1`, 24px gap). — *Fixed columns left a grown sheet a third empty.* `(DS ui §10e)`
3. **No helper text under a field.** Only text that reports state or warns of a consequence. — *Said three times, then made standing.* `(feedback_no_unrequested_ui)`
4. **An optional field keeps its empty choice; a required field is marked with the destructive asterisk.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<Input className="h-8">` in a toolbar; full-width in a form row.
- ❌ `w-[240px]` on a field container without `FIXED WIDTH`; a "Enter the customer's name" hint below.

### Checks
`tokens`, `fields-fill`, `microcopy`.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order sheet (Order details); every list search (`h-8`).

---

## Textarea

`id: atom.textarea` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/textarea.tsx` · `registry: fpds-textarea`

### Purpose
Multi-line prose — notes, instructions, reasons.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for notes and free-form text longer than a line.
- Don't use for a single value (→ `atom.input`).

### Anatomy
Box that grows with content (`field-sizing-content`).

### Look
Input's recipe: `min-h-16 rounded-md border border-input bg-fp-surface-1b px-3 py-2`, `text-base md:text-sm`; dark `bg-fp-field-fill`.

### States
As Input.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Type / Enter | New line; box grows |

### Outcomes
Text saved with the record.

### Data contract
Native textarea props; `autoCapitalize="sentences"` by default.

### Rules
1. **A section's notes are their own unit below that section's table, inside the same group.** `(feedback_shared_sheet_cards, 2026-10-07)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Notes under the Products table, same heading.
- ❌ A notes box in a separate "Notes" card far from what it describes.

### Checks
`tokens`, `microcopy`.

### Reference
Sales › Order sheet (notes); 3PL › Recurring delivery sheet.

---

## Select

`id: atom.select` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/select.tsx` · `registry: fpds-select`

### Purpose
Pick one value from a short fixed list.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for a short, closed list where search adds nothing.
- Don't use when the list can grow, needs search, a second line per option, an empty choice or create (→ `molecule.combobox` — every list searches by default). In a table cell use `TableCellSelect`.

### Anatomy
Trigger (value + `ChevronDown` at 50%) · content (popper, below trigger) · items (check at right) · optional label / separator / scroll buttons.

### Look
Trigger = `selectTriggerClasses`: `rounded-md border border-input bg-fp-surface-1b px-3 py-2 text-sm`, `h-9` (`sm` `h-8`), placeholder muted; dark `bg-fp-field-fill` / hover `-hover`. Content: `rounded-md border bg-popover shadow-md p-1`, min width = trigger. Item: `rounded-sm py-1.5 pl-2 pr-8 text-sm`, focus `bg-accent`.

### States
closed · open · item focused (`bg-accent`) · selected (check) · disabled · placeholder · invalid.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click / Enter / Space on trigger | Opens below (popper, `sideOffset 4`, `align start`) |
| Arrow keys / type-ahead | Moves through items |
| Enter / click item | Picks it, closes |
| Esc | Closes, no change |

### Outcomes
One value chosen.

### Data contract
`value`, `onValueChange`, `SelectTrigger size`. Width is set on the trigger.

### Rules
1. **The list drops below the field (popper), never over it.** — *Item-aligned covered the field you clicked.*
2. **Anything that passes for a select wears `selectTriggerClasses`.** — *Two triggers a pixel apart read as two controls.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Combobox for customers, products, vendors.
- ❌ A Select of 40 customers.

### Checks
`tokens`. By eye: trigger identical to a Combobox's.

### Reference
Sales › Order sheet (short pickers); Rows-per-page in every table footer.

---

## Checkbox

`id: atom.checkbox` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/checkbox.tsx` · `registry: fpds-checkbox`

### Purpose
A boolean, or one pick in a multi-pick list.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for every boolean in a table or form (Top off, row select, facet options, multi-pick rows).
- Don't use a switch or toggle for a table boolean.

### Anatomy
16px box · `Check` or `Minus` (indeterminate) indicator, 14px.

### Look
`size-4 rounded-[4px] border border-input`; checked / indeterminate `bg-primary border-primary text-primary-foreground`; dark unchecked `bg-fp-field-fill`. Tick is `text-current` so a parent can't gray it.

### States
unchecked · checked · indeterminate · focus · disabled · invalid.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click / Space | Toggles |
| Header select-all | Ticks all; indeterminate when some |

### Outcomes
A boolean value; in a row-adding picker, rows.

### Data contract
`checked: boolean | "indeterminate"`, `onCheckedChange`.

### Rules
1. **Booleans are checkboxes, not toggles.** `(feedback_table_conventions, 2026-10-07)`
2. **Top off: header label + select-all at the right (`justify-between`), row ticks right-aligned; it sits right before Qty.** `(DS ui §10i)`
3. **The tick stays white on the filled box.** — *A muted parent once turned it gray.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ A checkbox column for Top off.
- ❌ A switch in a table cell.

### Checks
`table-rows` (Top off before Qty). By eye: indeterminate in headers.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order › Products table (Top off); every list's select column.

---

## Radio group

`id: atom.radio-group` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/radio-group.tsx` · `registry: fpds-radio-group`

### Purpose
Pick one of 2–4 options that each need their words visible.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use when every option should be read before choosing (fill type, scope of an edit).
- Don't use for long lists (→ `atom.select` / `molecule.combobox`) or view scopes (→ `atom.toggle-group`).

### Anatomy
Group (`grid gap-3`) · items (16px circle, 8px filled dot) · labels.

### Look
`size-4 rounded-full border border-input text-primary`; indicator `size-2 fill-primary`; dark `bg-fp-field-fill`.

### States
unselected · selected · focus · disabled · invalid.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click item / label | Selects it |
| Arrow keys | Moves selection |

### Outcomes
One value chosen.

### Data contract
`value`, `onValueChange`; each `RadioGroupItem value`.

### Rules
1. **No em-dash clauses or sub-labels on options.** — *Ship the label, the value and the control.* `(feedback_no_unrequested_ui)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ "This order" / "All orders".
- ❌ "All orders — changes every order that…" as the label.

### Checks
`microcopy`.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order sheet; Service › Service Order sheet.

---

## Switch

`id: atom.switch` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/switch.tsx` · `registry: fpds-switch`

### Purpose
Turn a setting on or off immediately, outside a table.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use in a menu of settings (card fields) or a settings row that applies at once.
- Don't use in a table cell or for a form boolean that a list compares (→ `atom.checkbox`).

### Anatomy
Track · thumb.

### Look
`default` 18×32px (`h-[1.15rem] w-8`), thumb 16px; `sm` 14×24px, thumb 12px. On `bg-primary`; off `bg-fp-switch-track`. Thumb `bg-background` (dark: on `bg-primary-foreground`, off `bg-foreground`).

### States
off · on · focus · disabled.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click / Space | Flips, applies now |

### Outcomes
The setting changes at once.

### Data contract
`checked`, `onCheckedChange`, `size`.

### Rules
1. **The off track is `bg-fp-switch-track`, never a stroke token.** `(2026-10-21)`
2. **No sub-label under a switch.** `(feedback_no_unrequested_ui)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<Switch size="sm">` in the board's Card fields menu.
- ❌ Switches in a Setting · Value table (use checkboxes).

### Checks
`tokens`, `microcopy`.

### Reference
Lists › Board view › Card configuration menu; 3PL › Recurring rule frequency.

---

## Slider

`id: atom.slider` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/slider.tsx` · `registry: fpds-slider`

### Purpose
Choose a value or range on a continuous scale.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for a level that is felt rather than typed (Accessibility sliders) or as the track inside a window control.
- Don't use for a delivery window in a form (→ `DeliveryWindowTimeline` unit), or an exact number (→ `atom.input`).

### Anatomy
Track · range · one thumb per value.

### Look
Track `h-1.5 rounded-full bg-accent`; range `bg-primary`; thumb `size-4 rounded-full border border-primary bg-white`, hover / focus `ring-4 ring-ring/50`.

### States
default · hover (ring) · focus (ring) · disabled (50%).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Drag thumb / click track | Value moves |
| Arrow keys | Steps by `step` |

### Outcomes
A number or a pair.

### Data contract
`value[]`, `onValueChange`, `min`, `max`, `step`, `minStepsBetweenThumbs`.

### Rules
1. **A slider's value is printed beside it, never only implied by the handle.** — *A value that can only be estimated is distrusted.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Value read-out on the label line.
- ❌ A bare slider for a quantity.

### Checks
By eye: value visible.

### Reference
Accessibility panel (Text size, Stroke contrast…); `molecule.time-window-slider`.

---

## Label

`id: atom.label` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/label.tsx` · `registry: fpds-label`

### Purpose
Names a field.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use above (or beside) every field and for a checkbox / radio's words.
- Don't use for section headings or helper text.

### Anatomy
Text · optional required asterisk (`text-destructive`) supplied by the form.

### Look
`text-sm leading-none font-medium`, `gap-2`; disabled peer → 50%.

### States
default · disabled (via peer / group).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click | Focuses / toggles its control |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`htmlFor`.

### Rules
1. **The label is the app's term, not the PRD's.** — *Same data, same control, same words.* `(feedback_ui_rules, 2026-10-13)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ "Ship-to".
- ❌ "Delivery location (where the truck goes)".

### Checks
`microcopy`.

### Reference
Every sheet field.

---

## Calendar

`id: atom.calendar` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/calendar.tsx` · `registry: fpds-calendar`

### Purpose
The month grid inside the date picker.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use only inside `DatePickerPanel` (via `molecule.date-field`, `DateRangePopover`, `DatePanel`).
- Don't use a bare Popover + Calendar on a screen (→ `molecule.date-field`). Exception: the technician phone app's full-width calendar.

### Anatomy
Nav (prev / next ghost buttons) · month caption · weekdays · day buttons.

### Look
Cell `--cell-size` 32px; days are ghost icon buttons. Single / range ends `bg-primary text-primary-foreground`; days between `bg-accent`; today `bg-accent`; outside days muted. Transparent inside a popover.

### States
day: default · hover · focus (ring) · today · selected · range start / middle / end · outside · disabled.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click a day | Sets the draft (nothing applies until Apply) |
| Arrow keys | Move the focused day |
| Prev / next | Change month |

### Outcomes
A draft date / range inside the panel.

### Data contract
react-day-picker props: `mode`, `selected`, `onSelect`, `month`, `numberOfMonths`, `disabled`.

### Rules
1. **One date picker everywhere** (DS ui §10p, 2026-10-21).

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `DateField` in a form.
- ❌ Popover + Calendar hand-built in a sheet.

### Checks
By eye: no screen imports `Calendar` directly except the picker and the technician app.

### Reference
Lists › date filter panel; 3PL › Recurring delivery sheet (date fields).

---

# Display

## Badge

`id: atom.badge` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/badge.tsx` · `registry: fpds-badge`

### Purpose
The DS's low-level chip geometry and the round counter (`BadgeNumber`).

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use `BadgeNumber` for a counter inside a button or nav item; `shape="disc"` only through `atom.code-disc`; `verified` for the verified mark.
- Don't use Badge for a status, tag, flag or "+N" (→ `atom.pill` / `atom.status-chip`).

### Anatomy
Badge: `rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium` (+ 12px icon). BadgeNumber: 20px circle, `max` → "99+".

### Look
Variants: `default` primary · `secondary` `bg-chip` · `outline` · `ghost` · `destructive` (`/10` soft) · `verified` (`bg-verified`, white) · `link` · `pastel-*` pairs. Disc: 22px (`size-5.5`), `text-xs font-semibold`.

### States
default · hover (fill /80–/90) · focus.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | Display only |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`variant`, `shape`; `BadgeNumber count max`.

### Rules
1. **Chips and badges are pills** — Badge is not the chip. **Counters inside a button or nav item are `BadgeNumber`, not pills.** `(DS ui §11b)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<BadgeNumber count={3} variant="secondary" />` beside a nav item.
- ❌ `<Badge>Assigned</Badge>` in a status column.

### Checks
`status-kinds`. By eye: no Badge in status / type / tag columns.

### Reference
Nav counters; DS showcase › Badge.

---

## Pill

`id: atom.pill` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/pill.tsx` · `registry: fpds-pill`

### Purpose
The chip and badge of the system — a status, tag, flag, type or "+N".

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for every chip/badge: status (via `atom.status-chip`), a table's Type column (full word), flags (Urgent, Over SLA), "+N".
- Don't use for plain text values (products, driver names), counters in buttons (→ `BadgeNumber`), board-card type initials (→ `atom.code-disc`).

### Anatomy
Rounded box · truncating label. `PillList`: first value pill + "+N" pill (hover card) — prefer `molecule.overflow-chip` in lists.

### Look
`PILL_SHAPE`: `inline-flex h-7 rounded-full px-3 text-xs font-normal`, no border.

| `kind` | Classes |
|---|---|
| neutral | `bg-muted text-muted-foreground` |
| info | `bg-fp-banner-accent-info/10 text-fp-banner-accent-info` |
| caution | `bg-fp-banner-accent-caution/10 text-fp-banner-accent-caution` |
| success | `bg-status-success/10 text-status-success` |
| critical | `bg-status-failed/10 text-status-failed` |

`color` (user-chosen, over kind): `bg-fp-lane-<hue>/12` (yellow `/18`, orange `/15`) with text = hue mixed 55–70% into `--foreground`; `violet` = `bg-accent-violet-soft text-accent-violet-ink`.

### States
default only (display). Pressable pills (+N) get the focus ring from their button.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | Display; a +N pill opens its breakdown (molecule) |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`kind` (`neutral | info | caution | success | critical`), `color?` (`PillColor`), `children`.

### Rules
1. **One pill size, 28px, everywhere.** `(2026-10-09)`
2. **Color by kind; a user-chosen color overrides it** (Sales stages, order types). `(DS ui §11b)`
3. **A table's Type column is a pill with the full word**; the disc's hue carries over via `discTonePill`. `(2026-10-16)`
4. **Apply the pill only where chips already exist** — never turn plain text into pills. — *Pavan rejected that.*
5. **Red only for a warning;** the rose type maps to pink. `(2026-10-16)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<Pill kind="caution">Needs approval</Pill>`, `<Pill color={discTonePill(tone)}>Delivery</Pill>`.
- ❌ `<Pill className="bg-amber-100">`; a red pill for a type.

### Checks
`status-kinds`, `tokens`.

### Reference
Sales › Orders table (Status, Type); 3PL › Delivery Orders (Type).

---

## Status chip

`id: atom.status-chip` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/status-chip.tsx` · `registry: fpds-status-chip`

### Purpose
A status word, drawn as a pill in its kind's color.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use in every status column, sheet header and board card.
- Don't use for tags / types (→ `atom.pill`). `variant="dot"` only for a dense grid where coloured words would shout.

### Anatomy
`text` / `soft`: the pill. `dot`: 6px dot (`aria-hidden`) + neutral `text-sm` word.

### Look
Tone → kind: `success→success`, `processing→caution`, `failed→critical`, `neutral→neutral`, `info→info` (pill classes as `atom.pill`). Dot fills: `bg-status-success`, `bg-status-processing`, `bg-status-failed`, `bg-muted-foreground`, `bg-fp-banner-accent-info`.

### States
display only.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | — |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`tone` from the module's map (`X_TONE[status]`, built by `statusTones(kinds)`), `variant`, `color?`.

### Rules
1. **A module declares kinds, never tones;** `statusKind.ts` is the only kind→tone map. — *Authors reaching for swatches made "Assigned" three colors.* `(DS ui §10b)`
2. **A status is its word — no dot beside a pill.** `(DS ui §11)`
3. **A status column always shows the pill, never coloured text alone.** `(2026-10-09)`
4. **Canceled is neutral; Paused is caution; active is info.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `const ORDER_TONE = statusTones({ Unassigned: "caution", Assigned: "info", Delivered: "success", Blocked: "critical", Cancelled: "neutral" })`.
- ❌ `<StatusChip tone="processing">` written at a call site.

### Checks
`status-kinds`.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Orders (Status); Service › Service Orders board cards.

---

## Avatar

`id: atom.avatar` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/avatar.tsx` · `registry: fpds-avatar`

### Purpose
A person's picture or initials.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for a person: profile, assignee stacks.
- Don't use as a type marker (→ `atom.code-disc`).

### Anatomy
Root · image · fallback (initials) · optional badge · group (`-space-x-2`, `ring-2 ring-background`) · group count.

### Look
`sm` 24px · `default` 32px · `lg` 40px, `rounded-full`. Fallback `bg-accent text-sm text-muted-foreground` (`text-xs` at sm).

### States
image · fallback.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | — |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`size`; `AvatarImage src`; `AvatarFallback` children.

### Rules
1. **Show only what was asked** — no avatar added to rows that didn't have one. `(feedback_no_unrequested_ui)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Initials fallback.
- ❌ Coloured initials circles per person.

### Checks
`tokens`.

### Reference
Profile screen; Fuel Orders bulk bar.

## Kbd

`id: atom.kbd` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/kbd.tsx` · `registry: fpds-kbd`

### Purpose
Shows a keyboard key or shortcut.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use beside a command that has a shortcut (global search).
- Don't use as a chip or tag.

### Anatomy
Key box; `KbdGroup` for combos.

### Look
`h-5 min-w-5 rounded-sm bg-accent px-1 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground`.

### States
display only.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | — |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
children.

### Rules
1. **`bg-accent`, not `bg-muted`** — *in dark a sheet's popover surface equals muted and the key vanished.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<Kbd>⌘</Kbd><Kbd>K</Kbd>`.
- ❌ Shortcut text in brackets.

`tokens`.

## Code disc

`id: atom.code-disc` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/code-disc.tsx` · `registry: fpds-code-disc`

### Purpose
A type's initial in a 22px pastel circle, where only a letter fits.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use **only on board cards**.
- Don't use in a table (→ `atom.pill` with the full word and `discTonePill(tone)`).

### Anatomy
Disc (`Badge shape="disc"`) · tooltip with the full word.

### Look
22px circle, `text-xs font-semibold`, `bg-pastel-<hue> text-pastel-<hue>-foreground`.

### States
default · focus (tabbable, shows tooltip).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Hover / focus | Tooltip with `label` |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`code` (1–2 chars), `label` (required — tooltip + `aria-label`), `tone` (`pastel-*`).

### Rules
1. **The letter is never the only carrier of meaning** — label required. 
2. **Discs only on board cards; tables show the pill.** `(2026-10-16)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<CodeDisc code="D" label="Delivery" tone="pastel-blue" />` on a card.
- ❌ A disc in a table's Type column.

### Checks
By eye: no discs in tables.

3PL › Brokerage board cards.

## File type icon

`id: atom.file-type-icon` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/file-type-icon.tsx` · `registry: fpds-file-type-icon`

### Purpose
Shows a file's format beside its name.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use left of every uploaded file's name.
- Don't use as a generic document icon in menus.

Document outline (folded corner, two lines) 24×28px · format tag over the left edge.

Outline `text-foreground/70`. Tag `text-[7px] font-bold text-white rounded-[3px]`: PDF `bg-fp-lane-red`; images `bg-fp-lane-violet`; docs/txt `bg-fp-lane-blue`; xls/csv `bg-fp-lane-green`; zip `bg-fp-lane-orange`; other `bg-foreground text-background`.

### States
display only.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | — |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`name` (extension read from it).

### Rules
1. **Every file name has its icon.** `(2026-10-14)`
2. **The tag's text carries the format; color only groups.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ In `DocumentsTable`.
- ❌ A plain `FileText` icon.

### Checks
By eye.

Shared Documents table (3PL Delivery Order › Documents & notes).

## Metric icon (term icons)

`id: atom.metric-icon` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/term-icons.tsx` · `registry: fpds-metric-icon`

### Purpose
The tile a figure card wears, and the one icon per term.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use `MetricIcon` on metric / figure cards and dashboard units; use `TERM_ICONS` wherever deliveries, products, gallons or ship-tos are named.
- Don't use trend lines on figure cards; don't draw a different icon for a term.

### Anatomy
48px tile · 28px term icon. Icons: `TruckIcon`, `DropIcon`, `DrumIcon`, `PinIcon`.

### Look
Tile `size-12 rounded-lg bg-accent-violet/6 text-accent-violet`; icon `size-7`, `strokeWidth 1.75`, soft fill 22%, liquid levels as a wave.

### States
display only.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | — |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`icon` (a `TERM_ICONS` entry).

### Rules
1. **Beside the figure, centred on the figure + change chip + "vs" line, not in the title row.** `(DS ui §10k, 2026-10-20)`
2. **Same term → same icon in every module.**
3. **The card's own design is unchanged — the icon is added.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<MetricIcon icon={TERM_ICONS.gallons} />`.
- ❌ An arrow in a circle; a Lucide `Fuel` for gallons.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Customer Portal › Dashboard (Schedule card figures); Orders overview.

---

## Quiet pattern

`id: atom.quiet-pattern` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/quiet-pattern.tsx` · `registry: fpds-quiet-pattern`

### Purpose
The one decorative fill for an empty stretch of a card.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use as the last child of a card's flex column with leftover space.
- Don't place it in sheets by hand (`RecordSheet` does it when content ≤ 50% of the body); never behind text.

### Anatomy
SVG grid of quarter discs in 2×2 groups; seeded.

### Look
Foreground at 5% and 3% only; scale 2.7; mask: clear top 12% → full at bottom; whole tiles anchored bottom-left. Bleed to the card edges (`-mx-4 -mb-4` in a 16px card).

### States
display only.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | none (`pointer-events-none`, `aria-hidden`) |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`className` (`flex-1`, bleed margins).

### Rules
1. **One pattern, one size, two grays; never a hue.** — *"Keep this pattern consistent across our designs everywhere."* `(DS ui §11d, 2026-10-15)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Under Customer Portal "Saved vs market".
- ❌ A new pattern per card; resizing it.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Customer Portal › Dashboard; short create sheets (via RecordSheet).

---

# Overlays

## Tooltip

`id: atom.tooltip` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/tooltip.tsx` · `registry: fpds-tooltip`

### Purpose
Names an icon-only control, or why something is disabled.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use on every icon-only button and on a disabled control with a reason.
- Don't use to repeat visible text, or over a value someone may copy (`molecule.copy-value` has none).

### Anatomy
Trigger · content · arrow.

### Look
`rounded-md bg-foreground px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-background` (inverted), arrow same fill. Delay 0.

### States
closed · open.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Hover / focus trigger | Opens immediately |
| Leave / Esc | Closes |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`TooltipProvider delayDuration` (0); `TooltipContent side / sideOffset`.

### Rules
1. **Icon-only means tooltip + `aria-label`.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ "Search by column" on its icon.
- ❌ A tooltip that says the button's visible label again.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
List toolbar view icons.

---

## Popover

`id: atom.popover` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/popover.tsx` · `registry: fpds-popover`

### Purpose
A floating panel anchored to a control.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for pickers (combobox, date), breakdowns, filter panels.
- Don't use for a task that needs focus held (→ `atom.dialog`) or a record (→ `atom.sheet`). Not for the Accessibility panel (fixed, because zoom misplaces popovers).

### Anatomy
Trigger · content · optional header / title / description.

### Look
`w-72 rounded-md border bg-popover p-4 shadow-md`; opens fade + zoom-95 + slide from its side; `sideOffset 4`.

### States
closed · open.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click trigger | Opens |
| Outside click / Esc | Closes |

### Outcomes
Whatever it holds.

### Data contract
`open`, `onOpenChange`, `align`, `side`, `sideOffset`.

### Rules
1. **Floating things have a shadow; controls don't.** `(2026-10-14)`
2. **Width capped to the window:** `max-w-[calc(var(--fp-vw,100vw)-2rem)]` for wide panels.

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Combobox list matches its trigger width.
- ❌ `w-[100vw]`.

### Checks
`tokens`.

### Reference
"+N" breakdowns; date picker.

---

## Dropdown menu

`id: atom.dropdown-menu` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/dropdown-menu.tsx` · `registry: fpds-dropdown-menu`

### Purpose
A list of actions (or checkable options) from one trigger.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for a row's ⋯ actions, Group By, Show / hide columns, a hanging add with a choice of row.
- Don't use to pick a field's value (→ `atom.select` / `molecule.combobox`).

### Anatomy
Trigger · content · items (icon + label) · checkbox / radio items · separators · sub-menus.

### Look
Content `rounded-md border bg-popover p-1 shadow-md`; item `rounded-sm px-2 py-1.5 text-sm`, focus `bg-accent`, icons 16px muted. Destructive item: label + icon `text-destructive`, focus `bg-destructive/10`.

### States
item: default · focus · disabled · checked.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click trigger | Opens below (`sideOffset 4`) |
| Arrows / Enter | Move / run |
| Esc | Closes |

### Outcomes
An action runs; a destructive one asks first (AlertDialog or ✕/✓).

### Data contract
Radix DropdownMenu; `DropdownMenuItem variant="destructive"`.

### Rules
1. **Only Delete's label is red;** never a red fill in a menu. `(DS ui §9)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Edit · Share · Delete (red label).
- ❌ "Edit customer" as an option inside a value picker.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Row ⋯ menus on every list; Show / hide columns.

---

## Dialog

`id: atom.dialog` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/dialog.tsx` · `registry: fpds-dialog`

### Purpose
A focused task over the page that must be finished or dismissed.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for a short decision with inputs (bulk edit, award a quote).
- Don't use for a record (→ `atom.sheet`) or a yes/no (→ `atom.alert-dialog`).

### Anatomy
Scrim · content · header (title, description) · body · footer · close ✕ (top-right).

### Look
Scrim `bg-black/50`. Content `rounded-lg border border-border bg-popover p-6 shadow-lg gap-4`, `sm:max-w-lg` (`size="lg"` `sm:max-w-2xl` for a table body). Title `text-lg font-semibold`; description `text-sm text-muted-foreground`. Footer `align="end"` (cancel / confirm) or `between` (back / forward).

### States
closed · open (200ms fade + zoom).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Esc / ✕ / scrim | Closes |
| Footer primary | Commits |

### Outcomes
Committed or dismissed.

### Data contract
`open`, `onOpenChange`; `DialogContent size showCloseButton`; `DialogFooter align`.

### Rules
1. **Use `size`, never hand-set `max-w`.**
2. **A dialog body is not a card** — a table in it is boxed (T2). `(DS ui/7-variants §3)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<DialogContent size="lg">` for a before/after table.
- ❌ `className="sm:max-w-[900px]"`.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Fuel Orders › bulk edit; 3PL › award dialog.

---

## Alert dialog

`id: atom.alert-dialog` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/alert-dialog.tsx` · `registry: fpds-alert-dialog`

### Purpose
A yes/no that guards an irreversible or lossy act.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for "Discard this order?" on closing an unsaved create, cancelling an order, deleting a record.
- Don't use for row deletes in editable tables (→ inline ✕/✓ `TableRowRemove`).

### Anatomy
Scrim · content · title · description · Cancel (outline) · Action.

### Look
As Dialog but `bg-background`, no ✕. Action defaults to `default`; may be `destructive` (the guarded final confirm).

### States
closed · open.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Cancel / Esc | Closes, nothing changes |
| Action | Runs the act |

### Outcomes
The act happens, or nothing.

### Data contract
`open`, `onOpenChange`; `AlertDialogAction variant`.

### Rules
1. **Words are the act:** "Discard this order?" → *Keep editing* / *Discard*. `(DS ui/7-variants, 2026-10-06)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Specific verbs.
- ❌ "Are you sure?" with OK / Cancel.

### Checks
`microcopy`.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order (close while creating); Sales Settings deletes.

---

## Sheet

`id: atom.sheet` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/sheet.tsx` · `registry: fpds-sheet`

### Purpose
The side panel primitive under every record sheet.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use through `RecordSheet` (the unit), which sets width, depth, steps, footer and autosave.
- Don't build a record screen on a bare `SheetContent`.

### Anatomy
Scrim · content (side `right` default) · header · footer · close ✕.

### Look
`bg-popover shadow-lg`, `border-l border-border` (right). Open 500ms / close 300ms slide. Widths come from RecordSheet: form 600, fit (tables + 34, + 364 with rail), data 1200, compact 510; clamp `100vw − 56px` (112 at level 2).

### States
closed · open · level 1 / level 2.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| ✕ / Esc / scrim | Closes (a dirty create asks "Discard this order?") |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`side`, `showCloseButton`.

### Rules
1. **Sheet width follows content.** `(DS ui/7-variants §1, 2026-10-15)`
2. **A sheet over a sheet is level 2** — always a strip of the one below shows.
3. **Existing records autosave; no Save / Cancel.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `RecordSheet fit={{ tables, rail }}`.
- ❌ `SheetContent className="w-[900px]"`.

### Checks
By eye; the `sheets` eval in `npm run eval`.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order sheet; Customer Portal › Order request sheet.

---

# Feedback

## Skeleton

`id: atom.skeleton` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/skeleton.tsx` · `registry: fpds-skeleton`

### Purpose
Holds a part's place while it loads.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for each part of a loading page in its own place (title, tabs, toolbar, header, rows).
- Don't use a spinner for a page.

### Anatomy
A block sized like what it stands in for.

### Look
`rounded-md bg-accent` + `.fp-shimmer` (a light sweeping left → right, 1.4s).

### States
loading.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | — |

### Outcomes
The page arrives in the shape it is about to take.

### Data contract
`className` (size).

### Rules
1. **Shimmer, never pulse; off with reduced motion.** `(DS ui §11c, 2026-10-10)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `ModuleSkeleton` layout.
- ❌ `animate-pulse`.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Module switch (ModuleSkeleton).

---

## Spinner

`id: atom.spinner` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/spinner.tsx` · `registry: fpds-spinner`

### Purpose
A small in-place "working" mark.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use inside a button or a toast while one act runs.
- Don't use for loading a page or list (→ `atom.skeleton`).

### Anatomy
`Loader2` icon, `role="status"`, `aria-label="Loading"`.

### Look
`size-4 animate-spin`, current color.

### States
spinning.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | — |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`className`.

### Rules
1. **Pages load as skeletons, not spinners.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ In a pressed "Export" button.
- ❌ Centred over an empty table.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Toaster loading icon.

---

## Progress

`id: atom.progress` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/progress.tsx` · `registry: fpds-progress`

### Purpose
How far a known amount has got.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for a share of a whole with a known end.
- Don't use for a tank level (→ `molecule.level-gauge`) or a lifecycle (→ `molecule.stage-track`).

### Anatomy
Track · indicator.

### Look
`h-2 rounded-full bg-primary/20`; indicator `bg-primary`.

### States
0–100.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | — |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`value` 0–100.

### Rules
1. **A value that means good/bad gets its own molecule, not a recoloured Progress.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Neutral primary bar.
- ❌ `bg-red-500` indicator.

### Checks
`tokens`.

### Reference
DS DataTable (progress column).

---

## Outline shine

`id: atom.outline-shine` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/outline-shine.tsx` · `registry: fpds-outline-shine`

### Purpose
A soft band sweeping across an element's outline (or face) to draw the eye without changing it.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use on AlertBanner CTAs (white) and pills (sweep, `fill`) — added by the banner; on the dashboard "Attention required" tile (red); a nudge on a single button.
- Don't add it from a screen to an AlertBanner (the banner does it); don't use on a ghost button with no outline.

### Anatomy
An absolutely-placed ring (mask) inside a `relative` parent, with a skewed gradient band moving through it.

### Look
Defaults: color `--color-fp-lane-red`, band 5%, thickness 1.5px, inset 0. Banner CTA: white, band 50%, thickness 2, inset −0.5. Pill sweep: `fill`, band 30%, a deeper shade of the pill's hue.

### States
running · still (reduced motion).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | One 1.9s lap every 6s, first after 1.2s |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`color`, `band`, `thickness`, `every`, `lap`, `delay`, `inset`, `fill`.

### Rules
1. **Every AlertBanner pill sweeps (collapsed only) and every CTA it carries shines.** `(DS ui §10l, 2026-10-20)`
2. **Off with reduced motion.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Parent is `relative` with a radius.
- ❌ A shine on a primary filled button.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Customer Portal › Dashboard (Attention required); Sales Orders / Portal Orders alert banner.

---

# Structure

## Separator

`id: atom.separator` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/separator.tsx` · `registry: fpds-separator`

### Purpose
A structural divider.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use between toolbar clusters and between menu groups.
- Don't use to separate stepped-form groups (the 40px gap does it) or as a card edge.

### Anatomy
1px line.

### Look
`bg-border`, `h-px w-full` or `w-px h-full`.

### States
—

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | — |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`orientation`, `decorative`.

### Rules
1. **Dividers use `--border`, rims use `--fp-outline-1`.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Between date scope and view switch.
- ❌ A separator under every field.

### Checks
`tokens`.

### Reference
List toolbar right cluster.

---

## Scroll area

`id: atom.scroll-area` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/scroll-area.tsx` · `registry: fpds-scroll-area`

### Purpose
A scrolling region with a slim custom thumb.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use rarely — the app hides scrollbars by default.
- Don't use to bring back visible scrollbars on lanes or lists.

### Anatomy
Viewport · scrollbar (10px) · thumb.

### Look
Thumb `rounded-full bg-border`.

### States
idle · scrolling · focus (ring).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Wheel / drag | Scrolls |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
children; `ScrollBar orientation`.

### Rules
1. **No scrollbars by default** — the clipped edge says there is more. — *One visible bar teaches that others' absence means "doesn't scroll".*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Native overflow with hidden bars.
- ❌ ScrollArea on board lanes.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
DS showcase › Scroll Area.

---

## Tabs

`id: atom.tabs` · `layer: atom` · `code: src/components/ui/tabs.tsx` · `registry: fpds-tabs`

### Purpose
Switch between views of one thing.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for views of one record or settings page; `line` variant for page-level tabs.
- Don't use for ordered steps (→ `molecule.stage-track` / Steps) or list views (the view row: All + saved views).

### Anatomy
List · triggers · content.

### Look
`default` list: `h-9 rounded-lg bg-accent p-[3px] text-muted-foreground`; active trigger `bg-background border-foreground/25 text-foreground` (greyscale). `line` list: transparent; active `text-selected-foreground` with a 2px `bg-selected-foreground` underline. Trigger `text-sm font-medium`, inactive `text-foreground/60`.

### States
inactive · hover (`text-foreground`) · active · focus · disabled.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click / arrows | Switch view |

### Outcomes
Another view of the same thing.

### Data contract
`value`, `onValueChange`; `TabsList variant`.

### Rules
1. **The segment look is greyscale; only the underline tabs keep the selection ink.** `(2026-10-16)`
2. **Tabs are not steps.** — *Tabs may be read in any order; steps can't.*

### Do / Don't
