- ✅ Card / Banner switch in the order summary.
- ❌ Tabs labeled "1 Details · 2 Products".

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order sheet (summary mode); Sales Settings.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/03-molecules.md ===== -->

# FPDS · 03 Molecules

Small combinations of atoms with one job each. Same spec-card sections as the
atoms, at `###` level. Token names are from [`01-foundations.md`](01-foundations.md);
atoms are referenced by id from [`02-atoms.md`](02-atoms.md).

---

# Finding and narrowing

## Search field

`id: molecule.search-field` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ListToolbar.tsx (search box)` · `registry: fpds-search-field`

### Purpose
Search every column of a list from the toolbar.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use as the list toolbar's search — one per list, every list.
- Don't use to search one column (→ Search by column, the filter row under the header) or inside a picker (the Command input does that).

### Anatomy
`Search` glyph (left, inside) · `atom.input` (`type="search"`) · clear `X` button (right, only with a value).

### Look
**FIXED WIDTH 320px** container (`relative w-[320px]`). Input `h-8 pl-8` (+ `pr-8` with a value). Glyph `size-4 text-muted-foreground` at `left-2.5`. Clear: `size-3.5` `X` in a `rounded-sm p-0.5` button at `right-2`, `text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground`.

### States
empty (placeholder, no ✕) · typing / has value (✕ shows) · focus (ring).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Type | List narrows live, every column |
| Press ✕ | Value cleared, list restored, focus stays |
| Leave the list and return | Search is still there (lists remember) |

### Outcomes
A narrowed list; "N orders" in the view bar updates.

### Data contract
`searchValue`, `onSearchChange(v)`, `searchPlaceholder`. `aria-label="Search all columns"`; clear `aria-label="Clear search"`.

### Rules
1. **The list search is 320px on every list** — the one sanctioned `FIXED WIDTH`. `(DS ui §10e)`
2. **Search sits left, after quick filters, before Filters and Group By.** — *Pick the set, then find the row.*
3. **Lists remember search, filters, sort and view when you leave and return;** only Clear all removes filters. `(2026-10-09)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Placeholder names the record ("Search orders").
- ❌ A search that stretches to fill the toolbar.

### Checks
`fields-fill` (the `FIXED WIDTH` comment). By eye: ✕ only with a value.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Orders toolbar; Sales › Orders toolbar.

---

## Filter chip

`id: molecule.filter-chip` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ListToolbar.tsx (FilterChips, ColumnSearchChip)` · `registry: fpds-filter-chip`

### Purpose
Show which filters are on, one removable pill per facet, under the toolbar.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use whenever a list has applied filters or an active column search.
- Don't use a count badge on the Filters button instead ("Filters 7" is not an answer).

### Anatomy
Shelf (`data-filter-chips`) · scroller of chips · column-search chip (inside the scroller) · **Clear all** (after the last chip, outside the scroller). Chip: label (muted) · value (body ink) **or** count bubble · ✕.

### Look
Chip: `h-7 max-w-[260px] rounded-full bg-muted pl-3 pr-1 text-xs text-foreground gap-1`; label `text-muted-foreground`; count `rounded-full bg-background px-1.5 tabular-nums`; ✕ a 20px round button (`size-5`, `X size-3`, `hover:bg-foreground/10`). Column-search chip: same, with a `Search size-3` glyph and `Label: “query”`. Clear all: `text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:underline hover:text-foreground`. Shelf `mt-3 gap-2`, one line, scrolls rather than wraps. When the view's tools are routed under the view icons, the shelf rises into that row (`[data-routed] + [data-filter-chips]`).

### States
one value ("City: Bakersfield") · several values ("City 3" — hover card lists them, each with its own ✕) · hover card open.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| ✕ on a one-value chip | That filter is removed |
| ✕ on a grouped chip | Every value of that facet is removed |
| Hover a grouped chip | Card lists values (uppercase 10px facet heading), each removable |
| Clear all | All filters off |
| ✕ on column-search chip | Column search cleared |

### Outcomes
A wider list; the Filters panel's ticks match.

### Data contract
`FilterChips`: `facets {key,label}[]` (panel order), `selected` (`facetKey:option` ids), `onChange(next)`, `children` (the column-search chip). `ColumnSearchChip`: `searchFields`, `colScope`, `colQuery`, `onClear`.

### Rules
1. **One chip per facet, not per value; one value shows itself, several show a count.** — *The shelf should be as long as the questions asked.*
2. **Removable chips are pills with the ✕ inside.** `(DS ui §11b, 2026-10-09)`
3. **Clear all sits right after the last chip,** never pushed to the far end. `(2026-10-09)`
4. **One line, always** — it scrolls. — *Every pixel of it comes out of the data.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ "Status: Assigned", "City 3".
- ❌ Three "City" chips; Clear all at the far right.

### Checks
`tokens`. By eye: chip shelf one line; Clear all adjacent.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Orders (after applying Filters); Customer Portal › Orders.

---

## Icon tool

`id: molecule.icon-tool` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ListToolbar.tsx (IconTool, BAR_TOOL)` · `registry: fpds-icon-tool`

### Purpose
An icon-only toolbar control named by a tooltip — view switch and view tools.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for the display icons (Table · Board · Map) and the view's tools (Search by column, Show/hide columns, Card configuration).
- Don't use for a labeled action (→ `atom.button` with text).

### Anatomy
Ghost `icon-sm` button · Lucide icon · tooltip. In the view bar: icon + a name that slides out.

### Look
**Display icon:** `Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm"` (32px); active `bg-chip text-foreground`; icons `Table2`, `KanbanSquare`, `Map`. **View-bar tool:** `h-8 px-2 text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground`, no fill ever (not on hover, not when on); its name reveals over 200ms on hover, focus, `aria-pressed` or `aria-expanded` and stays out while the tool is on. Tools 4px apart like the view icons.

### States
rest · hover · focus · active view (`bg-chip`) · tool on (name stays out).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Hover / focus | Tooltip (display icon) or name slides out (view-bar tool) |
| Click display icon | Switches table / board / map |
| Click Search by column | Opens / closes the filter row under the header |
| Click Show/hide columns | Opens the column menu |

### Outcomes
Another display, a column filter row, a column set, card fields.

### Data contract
`label` (tooltip + `aria-label`), `view`, `active`, `onClick`, `children` (icon).

### Rules
1. **One view → no view switch;** the board icon only where a board exists. `(DS ui §10h)`
2. **The active view's tools sit in the view bar** (table: Search by column, Show/hide columns; board: Card configuration; map: none). `(DS ui §10p, 2026-10-22)`
3. **Tool names are muted gray 12px, never black; no fill or outline.**
4. **Active display uses `bg-chip`, not `bg-accent`.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Tooltip "Search by column".
- ❌ A lone Table icon on a list with one view.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Orders (view icons + view bar).

---

# Cells and chips

## Status pill

`id: molecule.status-pill` · `layer: molecule` · `code: src/components/ui/status-chip.tsx + prototypes/unified-view/statusKind.ts` · `registry: fpds-status-pill`

### Purpose
A record's status in a cell, header or card — the status word in its kind's color.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use in every status column, every sheet header status, every board card.
- Don't use for a type or tag (→ `atom.pill` with `color`), or where every row has the same value (render nothing).

### Anatomy
The module's status → kind map · `statusTones()` · `StatusChip tone`.

### Look
`atom.pill` recipe in the kind's colors: caution amber, info blue, success green, critical red, neutral gray.

### States
One per kind.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | Display |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
A map `Record<Status, StatusKind>` in the module; `ORDER_TONE = statusTones(map)`; `<StatusChip tone={ORDER_TONE[s]}>{s}</StatusChip>`. Sales stages may carry a user `color`.

### Rules
1. **Kinds are about what the reader should do:** caution waits on somebody, info is simply true, success is done, critical is a warning, neutral is neither. `(DS ui §10b)`
2. **Canceled is neutral; Paused is caution; Skipped neutral; "Quotation received" caution; Maintenance OK neutral.**
3. **The application's docs keep ONE color-mapping table.** — *A hue cannot mean two things in two modules.*
4. **A badge of one meaning is a badge nobody needs.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Assigned = info in every module (3PL "Assigned" is the old "Accepted").
- ❌ Green "Active" customers.

### Checks
`status-kinds`.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Orders (Status column); Service › Service Orders board.

---

## Two-line cell

`id: molecule.two-line-cell` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ListTable.tsx (TwoLineCell)` · `registry: fpds-two-line-cell`

### Purpose
A record's name with the one fact that tells it apart, in one cell.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for a customer + location, an order + product, a rule + schedule summary.
- Don't use to stack two unrelated values (give each its column).

### Anatomy
Title line · description line. Matched search text is marked in both.

### Look
`flex flex-col min-w-0`; title `truncate text-sm text-foreground`; description `truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground`. Left-aligned.

### States
default · search hit (matched run marked).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Row click / Enter | Opens the record (the row, not the cell) |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`title`, `description`, `q` (column filter text).

### Rules
1. **Everything left-aligned.** `(DS ui §10p, 2026-10-22)`
2. **Rows open on click / Enter; no row chevron.** `(2026-10-19)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ "Acme Fuels / Bakersfield, CA".
- ❌ A second line that repeats the first.

### Checks
By eye: both lines truncate.

### Reference
Sales › Orders; Customers; 3PL › Recurring Orders.

---

## Overflow chip

`id: molecule.overflow-chip` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/BreakdownCard.tsx (OverflowChip) · ListTable.tsx (OverflowList)` · `registry: fpds-overflow-chip`

### Purpose
Shows the first value and a "+N" pill that opens the rest.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a cell holds a list (products, ship-tos, assets).
- Don't use a hover list or a tooltip for the rest.

### Anatomy
First value (plain `text-sm`, truncates) · "+N" `atom.pill` (`tabular-nums`) as a button · `molecule.breakdown-popover`.

### Look
`flex items-center gap-1.5`; empty → "—" muted. Card width 280 (360 when rows carry a sub-line or figure). Title "All {label} ({count})".

### States
none · one value (no chip) · overflow (chip) · open.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click "+N" | Breakdown card opens above the cell |
| Click inside | Stays in the cell — the row does not open |
| Esc / outside | Closes |

### Outcomes
The full list read in place.

### Data contract
`values: string[]`, `label` (plural noun), `rows?: BreakdownRow[]`.

### Rules
1. **"+N" opens the shared BreakdownCard on CLICK, everywhere.** — *Touch cannot hover; a card under a passing pointer is one nobody asked for.* `(2026-10-14)`
2. **Modules reuse this; never a local variant.** — *The portal's "+N" drifted into five idioms.* `(feedback_uv_source_of_truth)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<OverflowList values={products} label="products" />`.
- ❌ A `HoverCard` list on "+2".

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Customers (Ship-tos); Quotes (Products); 3PL › Directory.

---

## Breakdown popover

`id: molecule.breakdown-popover` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/BreakdownCard.tsx (BreakdownPopover, BreakdownCard)` · `registry: fpds-breakdown-popover`

### Purpose
What a "+N" chip or a total is made of.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use behind any "+N" or figure that sums things (quantity, amount, level on fulfillment).
- Don't use as a general popover form.

### Anatomy
Title · dashed rule · rows (label, optional sub-line, optional value) **or** a grouped table · dashed rule + total (optional) · warnings (optional).

### Look
Popover `side="top"`, width 280 default, `max-h-96 overflow-auto`, `p-4`. Title `text-base font-semibold`; rules `border-t border-dashed border-border`; rows `text-sm text-muted-foreground`, label in `text-foreground` when it has a sub-line (`text-xs`); values `tabular-nums`; total `text-base font-semibold`. Group band `rounded-sm bg-fp-table-header px-2 py-1 text-xs font-semibold`. Warnings `text-sm text-status-failed` with `TriangleAlert size-4`.

### States
closed · open.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click trigger | Opens above; click does not reach the row |
| Esc / outside | Closes |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`trigger`, `label` (screen reader), `card {title, rows, table?, total?, warnings?}`, `width`, `align`.

### Rules
1. **Warnings say what will go wrong, in red, after the total.** `(2026-10-20)`
2. **Where the whole cell is the figure, the figure is the trigger.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ "Qty" figure opens its per-tank breakdown.
- ❌ A second card style for the same job.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Customer Portal › Orders (level on fulfillment); Sales › Quotes (products).

---

## Trend chip

`id: molecule.trend-chip` · `layer: molecule` · `code: src/components/ui/trend-chip.tsx` · `registry: fpds-trend-chip`

### Purpose
The one up / down marker for a change against a period.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use after the figure on every figure card, with "vs N the previous …" under it.
- Don't use arrows in circles, orange, `destructive` red or trend lines on figure cards.

### Anatomy
Soft chip · small filled triangle (▲/▼, none for same) · change text.

### Look
`rounded-md font-medium tabular-nums gap-1`; `md` `h-7 px-2 text-sm`, `sm` `h-5 px-1.5 text-xs`. Good: `bg-fp-trend-up-bg text-fp-trend-up`; bad: `bg-fp-trend-down-bg text-fp-trend-down`; same: `bg-muted text-muted-foreground` ("Same"). Triangle: 5px sides, 6px tall, `currentColor`.

### States
up-good · up-bad · down-good · down-bad · same.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| — | Display |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`dir` (`up | down | same`), `good` (default `dir === "up"`), `size`, `children`.

### Rules
1. **Triangle shows direction; color shows good or bad** — late orders down is green (`good`). `(DS ui §10k, 2026-10-20)`
2. **Red is always `--fp-trend-down`.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<TrendChip dir="down" good>12%</TrendChip>` for late orders.
- ❌ A `TrendingUp` icon in an orange circle.

### Checks
`tokens`.

### Reference
Customer Portal › Dashboard; Orders overview.

---

## Copy value

`id: molecule.copy-value` · `layer: molecule` · `code: src/components/ui/copy-value.tsx` · `registry: fpds-copy-value`

### Purpose
A read-only value with its own copy button.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for contact facts someone pastes elsewhere: phone, email, IDs.
- Don't use a disabled input for read-only values; don't put one copy button over two values.

### Anatomy
Value text · 24px copy button (reserved space, appears on hover / focus).

### Look
Value `truncate text-sm text-foreground` (`tabular-nums` optional). Button `size-6 rounded-sm text-muted-foreground`, hidden (`opacity-0`) until row hover or focus; `hover:bg-accent`; glyph `Copy` / `Check` 14px; copied → `text-status-success`. No gap — the button's box is the space. No tooltip.

### States
empty (placeholder "—", not copyable) · rest · hover (button shows) · copied (tick, 1.4s).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Hover / focus | Copy button appears |
| Click copy | Value on the clipboard; tick for 1.4s |

### Outcomes
Clipboard holds the value.

### Data contract
`value`, `label` ("POC email" → `aria-label="Copy POC email"`), `placeholder`, `tabular`.

### Rules
1. **One copy button per value.** — *A pair copied together has to be edited after.*
2. **Confirm in place; no toast.**
3. **No tooltip** — it would cover the next value.

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Phone and email each with their own copy.
- ❌ A disabled `Input` showing a phone number.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
3PL › Directory record (vendor / customer contacts); 3PL › Delivery Order sheet.

---

# Inputs

## Combobox

`id: molecule.combobox` · `layer: molecule` · `code: src/components/ui/combobox.tsx` · `registry: fpds-combobox`

### Purpose
A select you can type into — the default picker for any list of records.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for customers, ship-tos, products, vendors, technicians — any list that can grow; with `allowCreate` for open sets (roles, tags).
- Don't use for several values in one field (→ `molecule.token-multi-select`) or inside an editable table (→ `TableCellCombobox`, with `multiple` when it adds rows).

### Anatomy
Trigger (same as Select: value, optional `trailing`, `ChevronDown`) · popover list: search box · create row (optional) · clear row (optional, first) · options (check · label · optional description · optional row action).

### Look
Trigger = `selectTriggerClasses` (`h-9`, `sm` `h-8`). List width = trigger (`min-w-[12rem]`), `p-0`. Option description `text-xs text-muted-foreground`; clear label muted; row action hidden until hover / keyboard focus.

### States
closed · open · filtering · no matches ("No matches.") · create offered · selected (check) · disabled option · placeholder ("Select").

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click trigger | Opens list with search focused |
| Type | Filters (label and description) |
| Enter / click option | Picks, closes, clears the query |
| Pick clear row | Value becomes "" |
| Pick "Create “…”" | `onCreate` then picks it |
| Click a row action | Runs it; does not pick |
| Esc | Closes |

### Outcomes
One value (possibly new).

### Data contract
`value`, `onValueChange`, `options` (`string` or `{value,label,description,disabled}`), `placeholder`, `searchPlaceholder` ("Search…"), `emptyText`, `allowCreate`, `onCreate`, `clearLabel`, `optionAction`, `trailing`, `size`, `className` (width).

### Rules
1. **Every list searches** (`searchThreshold` 1). — *A field that changes behavior at nine options must be looked at before use.*
2. **Optional pickers offer their empty choice first** ("No technician"); required ones don't. `(DS ux §Forms, 2026-09-25)`
3. **It wears the Select's trigger exactly.**
4. **Sample lists never come up empty** — 3–10 options. `(feedback_ui_rules)`
5. **Edit-the-option is a row action, never a menu item in the list.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<Combobox clearLabel="No technician" … />`.
- ❌ An "Edit customer" option inside the customer list.

### Checks
`fields-fill`. By eye: trigger matches Select.

### Reference
Sales › Quote sheet (Customer); Customer / Asset sheets.

---

## Token multi-select

`id: molecule.token-multi-select` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/TokenMultiSelect.tsx` · `registry: fpds-token-multi-select`

### Purpose
Several values for one field, shown as chips, added from a searchable list.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for tags, products on a bulk edit, products on a recurring program — values that live in ONE field.
- Don't use when each pick should become its own table row (→ `TableCellSelect multiple`, see "Picking several makes rows").

### Anatomy
Box (button) · chips (initial badge · name · optional highlight word · ✕) · "+ Add …" · popover: search · options (initial badge, check when chosen).

### Look
Box `min-h-11 w-full rounded-md border border-input px-2 py-2 gap-2`, hover `border-fp-outline-2`, open `border-ring`. `flush`: no border / radius, `px-4` (16px inset), hover `bg-fp-surface-1a`. Chip `rounded-md border border-border bg-fp-surface-1b py-1 pl-1.5 pr-1 text-sm`; initial badge 16px `bg-accent text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase`; ✕ `size-3.5` muted. Highlighted chip `border-fp-banner-accent-info` + word `bg-fp-banner-accent-info/10 text-xs`. Add `text-sm text-muted-foreground` with `Plus`.

### States
empty (Add centred) · has chips · open · highlighted chips · flashing (`fp-attention-info`, once).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click box | List opens, stays open while picking |
| Pick option | Toggles it in / out |
| ✕ on chip | Removes it |
| Click chip (with `onChipClick`) | Runs it (e.g. marks winter blend) instead of opening |

### Outcomes
A string array.

### Data contract
`options`, `value`, `onChange`, `addLabel`, `searchPlaceholder`, `flush`, `highlighted`, `highlightLabel`, `onChipClick`, `flashKey`.

### Rules
1. **Flush is the box, not the content** — chips keep the 16px inset. `(2026-09-25)`
2. **Chips that just became clickable blink blue once.** `(2026-10-01)`
3. **Several values in one cell stay one cell; several rows are rows.** `(feedback_multi_pick_rows)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Tags on a record.
- ❌ Tanks on an order as tokens (they are rows).

### Checks
`table-rows`.

### Reference
Fuel Orders › bulk bar; 3PL › Recurring program create.

---

## Date field

`id: molecule.date-field` · `layer: molecule` · `code: src/components/ui/date-picker.tsx (DateField, DateKeyField)` · `registry: fpds-date-field`

### Purpose
A form field that holds one date, opening the shared picker.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for every single date in a form (planned date, end date). `DateKeyField` when the value is stored as `YYYY-MM-DD` with an optional `min`.
- Don't use for a list's date range (→ `DateRangeFilter` / `DateRangePopover`) or a hand-built Popover + Calendar.

### Anatomy
Trigger (outline button: `Calendar` icon · formatted date or placeholder) · popover: presets rail (Today, Tomorrow, Yesterday, In 7 days) · one month · footer (typed date MM/DD/YYYY · Reset · Apply).

### Look
Trigger `Button variant="outline"`, `w-full justify-start font-normal`, placeholder muted ("Select date"); date as "Oct 23, 2026". Panel: presets `w-44 border-r border-border`, items `h-7 rounded-md px-2.5 text-sm`, hover `bg-fp-row-hover`, chosen `bg-selected text-selected-foreground font-medium`; footer `border-t border-border px-4 py-2.5`, typed box `h-8 w-32 tabular-nums`, Apply `size="sm"`. Popover width capped `calc(var(--fp-vw) - 2rem)`.

### States
empty · set · open · draft changed · day disabled (before `min`).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click trigger | Picker opens on the current value |
| Preset / day / typed date | Changes the draft only |
| Apply | Sets the value, closes |
| Reset | Draft back to the opening value |
| Esc / outside | Closes, no change |

### Outcomes
One date (or key).

### Data contract
`DateField`: `value: Date | null`, `onChange(Date | null)`, `placeholder`, `dayDisabled`, `disabled`, `align`, `children` (own trigger). `DateKeyField`: `value: string`, `onChange(key)`, `min`.

### Rules
1. **One date picker for every screen that picks a date.** `(DS ui §10p, 2026-10-21)`
2. **Nothing changes until Apply.**
3. **A date is a natural-size control** — a `FIXED WIDTH` is allowed beside fields that fill. `(DS ui §10e)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<DateKeyField value={start} min={today} … />`.
- ❌ `<input type="date">` or Popover + Calendar in a sheet.

### Checks
`fields-fill`. By eye: Apply / Reset present.

### Reference
3PL › Recurring delivery sheet; 3PL › Create recurring rule (start / end dates); Customer Portal › request actions.

---

## Time-window slider

`id: molecule.time-window-slider` · `layer: molecule` · `code: src/components/form-layout/TimeWindowSlider.tsx` · `registry: fpds-time-window-slider`

### Purpose
A time range on a drawn day, in 30-minute steps.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use only where it stands today: the technician phone app (Service › TechnicianHome). Its formatters (`formatMinutes`, `formatWindow`, `formatSpan`) are shared.
- **Don't use for a new delivery or scheduling window** — every window in every module is `DeliveryWindowTimeline` (preferred-time bars, hanging +). `(DS ui §10m, 2026-10-20)`

### Anatomy
Label line: label (+ required ✱) · window "8:00 AM – 12:00 PM" · span note ("4 hr window" / "All day", right). Control zone (36px): two-thumb `atom.slider` · scale (12a 6a 12p 6p 12a).

### Look
Label `text-sm font-medium`; window `text-sm tabular-nums`; span `text-xs text-muted-foreground`, truncates first. Zone `h-9`; ticks `text-[11px] text-muted-foreground`, end labels pinned to the edges.

### States
default · dragging · disabled.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Drag a thumb | Window changes in 30-min steps; never shorter than `minSpan` (60) |
| Arrow keys | Step 30 min |

### Outcomes
`[start, end]` in minutes from midnight.

### Data contract
`start`, `end`, `onChange(start, end)`, `minSpan`, `label`, `required`, `disabled`.

### Rules
1. **The window is printed and never truncates;** the span note gives way. `(2026-10-15)`
2. **One field, one height** — label and value share a line so the row lines up.

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Technician app.
- ❌ A new 3PL or Portal window field.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
Service › Technician app (home).

---

## Hanging add

`id: molecule.hanging-add` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/HangingAdd.tsx` · `registry: fpds-hanging-add`

### Purpose
The add for a table that can grow — a tab off the card's bottom-left corner.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use under every boxed table that adds rows (products, vendors, fees, preferred times).
- Don't use a full-width "add row" band inside the card; don't render it on a fixed table.

### Anatomy
Plus · label that slides out on hover / focus. May be a menu trigger (Fee or Tax).

### Look
`h-8 rounded-t-none rounded-b-md bg-fp-table-header px-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-foreground`, hover `bg-accent`; label reveals to `max-w-[140px]` with `pl-1.5` over 200ms.

### States
rest (plus only) · hover / focus (label out) · disabled.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Hover / focus | Label slides out |
| Click | Adds a row and puts the cursor in it (or opens the choice menu) |

### Outcomes
A new blank row with focus.

### Data contract
`label` (the table's own noun — "Add products"), `onClick`, `disabled`, trigger passthrough.

### Rules
1. **Filled with the header tint, square on top.** — *It continues the table; an outline would be a second box.* `(2026-09-22)`
2. **The row picker of a row-adding table takes several (`multiple`, Apply → one row each).** Said three times. `(feedback_multi_pick_rows)`
3. **No table shows an empty message instead of rows** — editable tables show one blank row. `(2026-10-14)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<HangingAdd label="Add products" />` under the Products card.
- ❌ A dashed "+ Add product" box inside the card.

### Checks
`table-rows`.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order (Products, Vendor); Sales › Quote sheet (Fees / Tax).

---

# Progress and readings

## Stage track

`id: molecule.stage-track` · `layer: molecule` · `code: src/components/ui/stage-track.tsx · src/components/ui/steps.tsx` · `registry: fpds-stage-track`

### Purpose
Where a record is in its lifecycle, or which step of a form you are on — one look for both.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use `StageTrack` for a record's stages on its detail; `Steps` / `SheetSteps` for a stepped create form (passes `onStageClick`).
- Don't use tabs for steps; don't show off-line states (Canceled, Blocked) as a position — nothing is current then.

### Anatomy
`rings` (default): equal columns, each a 24px ring + name under it; a 2px line between first and last center, green up to the current. `track`: dots on a line with the current stage as a named pill. `compact`: steps 48px apart.

### Look
Rings: done `bg-status-success text-status-success-foreground` + `Check` 14px, name `text-status-success`; current `border-[5px] border-accent-violet bg-fp-surface-1b`, name `font-medium text-accent-violet-ink`; upcoming `border-[5px] border-fp-outline-2`, name muted. Line `h-0.5 bg-border`, done part `bg-status-success`. Names `text-sm`. Track pill `h-8 rounded-full border-2 px-3.5 text-sm font-semibold`, tone accent (violet wash 12%), brand (10%) or neutral.

### States
done · current · upcoming · disabled (`opacity-50`, reason on hover) · no current.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click a step (forms) | Moves to that step |
| Hover a disabled step | Shows why |
| Stage changes | Line runs on (500ms), left stage takes its tick, pill zooms in; never on first paint |

### Outcomes
Orientation; in forms, a step change.

### Data contract
`StageTrack`: `stages {key,label}[]`, `current`, `onStageClick?`, `disabledReason?(key)`, `tone` (`accent` default), `variant` (`rings` default), `compact`. `Steps`: `steps {value,label,disabledReason?}[]`, `value`, `onValueChange`.

### Rules
1. **Steppers are rings everywhere** — create forms and record tracks. `(2026-10-16)`
2. **Done is green; in progress is violet.** `(2026-10-19)`
3. **A step that can't open says why.** — *A control that refuses a click without saying why gets clicked again.*
4. **No rule under the track; the strip's ground is the sheet's own `bg-popover`.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `<Steps steps={…} value={step} onValueChange={…} />`.
- ❌ Numbered tabs.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order (stage track); 3PL › Create recurring rule (steps); Customer Portal › asset / ship-to create.

---

## Level gauge

`id: molecule.level-gauge` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/customer-portal/TankLevel.tsx (LevelGauge, LevelReading, BatteryReading, DaysToEmpty)` · `registry: fpds-level-gauge`

### Purpose
A tank's level, its monitor's battery and its days to empty — each in its own cell.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use in asset / tank columns where a monitor reports levels.
- Don't add band words ("Low"); don't propose level-triggered rules (Pavan raises those).

### Anatomy
**Level:** gauge (bar) + "62%". **Battery:** Lucide battery glyph + "%". **Days to empty:** "N days" / "Empty". No monitor → "No monitor" (level) or "—".

### Look
Gauge `h-2 w-14 rounded-full bg-muted`, fill min 3%: ≤ 10 `bg-fp-lane-red` + `.fp-urgent-blink`; < 50 `bg-fp-lane-red`; < 80 `bg-fp-lane-yellow`; ≥ 80 `bg-fp-lane-green`. Value `text-sm tabular-nums`. Battery text by the same bands (`text-status-failed`; yellow / green mixed 60–65% into `--foreground`); glyph: < 10 `BatteryWarning`, < 20 `BatteryLow`, ≥ 80 `BatteryFull`, ≥ 40 `BatteryMedium`, else `Battery`. Days `text-sm tabular-nums`, `text-status-failed` when it runs out before delivery.

### States
no monitor · sufficient · moderate · low · urgent (blinking; steady with reduced motion).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Hover battery | Title "Monitor battery N%" |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`LevelGauge pct`; `LevelReading asset`; `BatteryReading pct`; `DaysToEmpty asset days` (days until delivery).

### Rules
1. **Level is shown by color only, no band words.** `(2026-10-17)`
2. **Separate cells, separate columns** (level, battery, days to empty, suggested, on fulfillment).
3. **Urgent blinks and keeps blinking** — the tank is still nearly empty.

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Gauge + % in one cell, battery in its own.
- ❌ "Low (32%)".

### Checks
`tokens`.

### Reference
Customer Portal › Orders (stacked tank lines); Customer Portal › Order request sheet.

---

## Stat tile

`id: molecule.stat-tile` · `layer: molecule` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/DashboardParts.tsx (StatTile)` · `registry: fpds-stat-tile`

### Purpose
A headline number on a module dashboard that opens the rows it counts.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for headline counts on the 3PL and Sales dashboards.
- Don't use for a figure with a period change and term icon (→ figure card with `atom.metric-icon` + `molecule.trend-chip`).

### Anatomy
Label · value · optional note. The whole tile is a button.

### Look
Card `rounded-xl bg-fp-surface-1b shadow-xs p-5 gap-2` (dark `bg-fp-surface-1a`) on the dashboard ground (`rounded-xl bg-fp-surface-1a p-3`, dark `bg-fp-surface-1b`). Label `text-sm font-medium`; value `text-3xl font-semibold tabular-nums`; note `text-xs text-muted-foreground`. Hover `shadow-sm`.

### States
rest · hover · focus · disabled (no `onOpen`).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click / Enter | Opens the list narrowed to exactly those rows |

### Outcomes
A filtered list.

### Data contract
`label`, `value` (formatted string), `note?`, `onOpen?`.

### Rules
1. **Everything on a dashboard is a way into a list** — never a figure the reader can't go and check. `(2026-10-05)`
2. **A dashboard drill wins over the list's remembered filters.**

### Do / Don't
- ✅ "Unassigned 14" → Delivery Orders filtered to Unassigned.
- ❌ A number that opens nothing.

### Checks
By eye.

### Reference
3PL › Dashboard; Sales › Dashboard.

---

# Feedback

## Toast

`id: molecule.toast` · `layer: molecule` · `code: src/components/ui/sonner.tsx` · `registry: fpds-toast`

### Purpose
Confirms an act that leaves the screen, in one line.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use after create, export, status change, cancel.
- Don't use for an in-place copy (`molecule.copy-value` ticks), autosave, or a validation error (pending bar).

### Anatomy
Type icon · title · optional second line.

### Look
Mounted once, `position="bottom-right"`. Surface `--popover`, text `--popover-foreground`, border `--border`, radius `--radius`. Icons 16px: `CircleCheck`, `Info`, `TriangleAlert`, `OctagonX`, spinning `Loader2`. Follows `.dark`.

### States
success · info · warning · error · loading.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Appears | Auto-dismisses |
| Hover | Holds |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
`toast.success(title, { description? })`.

### Rules
1. **One line. A second line only to warn of a consequence.** Never repeat a name / number on screen or restate the obvious. `(feedback_toasts, 2026-10-07)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ `toast.success("Order created")`; "Paused R-1042" / "4 unfinished orders canceled."
- ❌ "Exported 80 vendors" / "The filtered list was exported."

### Checks
`microcopy`; G49 in `scripts/eval/parity-shell.mjs`.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Order (create); every list Export.

---

## Empty state

`id: molecule.empty-state` · `layer: molecule` · `code: src/components/ui/empty.tsx` · `registry: fpds-empty-state`

### Purpose
Tells the reader why a whole region has nothing in it (variant E3).

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a whole list / region is empty and needs a reason (no results for the filters, nothing yet).
- Don't use for an empty table inside a form (→ one blank row, or padded text E1); never a dashed box round an empty table (E2 is only an optional section that is off).

### Anatomy
`Empty` · `EmptyHeader` (`EmptyMedia variant="icon"` · `EmptyTitle` · `EmptyDescription`) · `EmptyContent` (actions).

### Look
Centred `flex-col gap-6 p-6 md:p-12 text-center`, no rim. Media 40px `rounded-lg bg-accent`, icon 24px. Title `text-lg font-medium tracking-tight`; description `text-sm/relaxed text-muted-foreground`, max `sm`.

### States
no records · no matches.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Action (e.g. Clear all) | Resolves the emptiness |

### Outcomes
—

### Data contract
Composition of the parts.

### Rules
1. **A report is not an invitation.** E1 padded text = nothing here; E2 dashed box = an optional section you can switch on; E3 = this. `(DS ui/7-variants §5)`
2. **Description reports state, not a lesson.** `(feedback_no_unrequested_ui)`

### Do / Don't
- ✅ "No orders match these filters" + Clear all.
- ❌ A dashed CTA box around an empty table.

### Checks
`microcopy`.

### Reference
Standard list (no results); Sales › Orders; Customers.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/alert-banner.md ===== -->

# Alert banner

`id: unit.alert-banner` · `layer: unit` · `code: src/components/ui/alert-banner.tsx, src/components/ui/banner.tsx` · `registry: fpds-alert-banner`

## Purpose
Tells the reader what on this list needs attention now, and lets them act on it in place: narrow the list to it, step through several kinds, act on an item, snooze or dismiss. Answers "what needs me on this page, and where is it?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a banner ACTS on the page: it applies a filter, holds several kinds of alert, or lists items each with its own action. That is the B2 banner, `AlertBanner`, and it carries the whole anatomy.
- Use on a list page for standing conditions (overdue, needs action, at risk, past due).
- Don't use when the banner only says one thing with at most one action or a ✕ — use the B1 banner without interaction, `BannerCallout` (solid · soft) or `BannerInline` (same files, `banner.tsx`).
- Don't dress a one-line message in B2 parts: no filter, no items and no stepping = B1.
- Don't use for the dashboard's **Attention required** tiles — a different pattern by design (red outline shine on the banner), not an `AlertBanner`.
- Don't use to explain why a control is disabled — that is a tooltip on the press (interactions §7b).

## Anatomy
One line (one alert KIND at a time), then an optional item list under it.

Left, in this order:
1. **Tag pill** — uppercase tag ("Needs action", "Overdue", "At risk", "Exception", "Due soon", "Disputed") with the `TriangleAlert` icon INSIDE it. The icon stands alone only when the alert has no tag.
2. **Count in the tag** — a small pill inside the tag pill (filtering alerts, `ctaMode`).
3. **Title** — what it is, one sentence, `font-medium`.
4. **Detail** (optional) — muted; gives way (truncates) first.
5. **"View details"** — ghost button with a dotted underline (filtering alerts); becomes **"✕ Clear filter"** while its filter is on.
   — or, for an alert with no filter: **count pill with the chevron inside** (opens the item list; chevron flips), no divider before it, no "Details" word. Without items, a plain count pill.

Right, in this order:
6. **The alert's own action** (optional) — a `bannerSoftAction` button, wrapped in the CTA shine.
7. **‹ 1 of N ›** — stepper between kinds; only when N > 1.
8. **Snooze** — clock icon button, tooltip "Remind me later" (`BannerSnooze`).
9. **✕ Dismiss** — always the LAST thing on the right.

Under the line (open, item alerts only): **item rows** — title (pressable when it can narrow), detail, optional `facts` (the record's own readings in equal columns, e.g. a tank's ship-to · level gauge · % · battery · gallons of capacity), item action (shined).

## Look
- Shell: `calloutVariants({ status, variant: "soft" })` — `rounded-md`, 1px rim; fill and rim are alphas of the status's inline accent `--fp-banner-accent-{info|caution|warning|success}`: `/10` wash + `/30` rim; **warning in light is `/5` wash + `/20` rim** (dark keeps `/10` · `/30`).
- Line: `min-h-11`, padding `px-3 py-1.5` (12px sides / 6px top-bottom — settled, don't change), gap `gap-2.5`.
- Tag pill: DS pill shape (`PILL_SHAPE`) coloured by kind via `pillColorClass` — warning → `critical`, caution → `caution`, info → `info`, success → `success`; `h-6`, `px-2`, 11px uppercase `font-medium tracking-wide`, icon 14px. Count inside: `h-4` round, `bg-background/80`, 10px `font-semibold tabular-nums`.
- Title `text-sm font-medium text-foreground`; detail `text-muted-foreground`.
- Icon buttons (snooze, ✕): 28px square, `rounded-sm`, `text-muted-foreground`, hover `bg-accent` + `text-foreground`.
- Stepper: `text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground`, chevrons 14px, disabled at the ends `opacity-30`.
- Item list: on white — `bg-fp-surface-1b`, `border-t` + row dividers `current/10`, rows `min-h-11`, indented to line up under the title.
- Item / own action button: `variant="outline" size="sm"` + `bannerSoftAction` (`bg-fp-surface-1b` light, `bg-fp-surface-base` dark).
- Motion (ui §10l): **every pill sweeps** — a bar through the fill in a deeper shade of its own kind (`--color-fp-lane-red` / `-orange` / `-blue` / `-green` mixed), **only while collapsed**. **Every CTA the banner carries shines** — white `OutlineShine` on its outline (2px ring centred on the 1px border, 50% band). The ghost "View details" / "Clear filter" has no outline, so no shine. Both are added by the banner, never by screens.

## States
- **collapsed (default)** — one line; pills sweep.
- **open** — item list shown on white under the line; chevron rotated; sweeps stop.
- **filter applied** — "View details" reads "✕ Clear filter" (non-CTA alerts with a filter also gain a muted "✕ Clear filter").
- **several kinds** — ‹ i of N ›; the whole banner takes the shown kind's status color; first/last chevron disabled at the ends.
- **hover** — pressable title/item: pointer + fade to 75% opacity, no underline; count pill `brightness-95`; icon buttons get `bg-accent`.
- **focus** — `ring-2 ring-ring/50` on every pressable part.
- **snooze custom** — popover with Hours (0–168) and Minutes (0–59) boxes and a Snooze button, disabled while the duration is 0.
- **empty** — no alerts left (all resolved, dismissed or snoozed) → renders nothing.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press "View details" (filtering alert) | The alert's filter is applied to the list's own filters (facets, flags, date range); the button turns into "✕ Clear filter" |
| Press "✕ Clear filter" | The list goes back to its default (e.g. portal Orders: today, nothing narrowed); the banner stays |
| Change the filter by hand after applying | "Clear filter" reverts to "View details" by itself |
| Press the title (alert with a filter, `ctaMode={false}`) | Applies the filter |
| Press the count pill with chevron | Opens the item list; chevron flips; press again folds it |
| Press an item's title | Narrows the list to that one record |
| Press an item's action ("Create Order", "Order the rest") | Runs that act — e.g. opens a new order sheet for that tank / for the undelivered rest |
| Press the banner's own action | Runs it |
| Press ‹ / › | Shows the previous / next kind; the item list closes |
| Press the clock | Menu: 1 hour · 2 hours · 4 hours · 8 hours · Tomorrow, same time · Custom… |
| Pick a snooze duration | That kind hides until the time passes, then returns |
| Pick Custom… → enter hours/minutes → Snooze | Same, for the typed duration |
| Press ✕ | That kind is dismissed for the visit; the next kind shows; last one gone → banner disappears |

## Outcomes
- A narrowed list (filter chips show it; Clear all also undoes it).
- A list narrowed to one record (item press).
- A record sheet opening from an item action (new order draft, order the rest).
- A kind hidden for a while (snooze) or for the visit (dismiss).
- No banner at all when nothing qualifies — never an empty banner.
- Nothing changes to data: the banner only filters and hands off to sheets.

## Data contract
- `alerts: BannerAlert[]` — each: `id`, `status` (info · caution · warning · success), `tag?`, `title`, `detail?`, `count?`, `onApply?` (its filter), `action?` (node), `items?` (`id`, `title`, `detail?`, `facts?`, `action?`, `onApply?`). Ordered most serious first.
- `appliedId?` — which alert's filter the list is showing now; `onClearApplied?` — puts the list back to its default.
- `onSnooze?(id, ms)`, `onDismiss?(id)` — the screen keeps snoozed/dismissed state and filters `alerts`.
- `ctaMode` (default true) — filtering alerts use the CTA anatomy; `false` restores the old one.
- Snooze set: `BANNER_SNOOZE_OPTIONS` (one set, every module); `BannerSnooze` is exported for standalone use.

## Rules
1. **The question is whether pressing it does something to the page** — yes = B2 `AlertBanner` with the whole anatomy; no = B1 `BannerCallout`/`BannerInline` — *two banners kept apart so a message never looks pressable and an alert never looks inert* — `(source: docs/ds/ui/7-variants.md §6; interactions §7, §7a; memory feedback_banner_final 2026-10-13)`
2. **The banner is a filter** — its CTA applies exactly the narrowing its words describe, onto the list's own filters — *so the chips show it and Clear all undoes it* — `(source: interactions §7a; alert-banner.tsx header)`
3. **Filtering alerts: count inside the tag, plain title, ghost "View details" → "✕ Clear filter"; no count pill, no list** — *tried on portal Invoices, then made standard everywhere* — `(source: interactions §7a, Pavan 2026-10-15)`
4. **Clear filter returns the list to its default and the banner stays until its own ✕** — *clearing the filter is not dismissing the alert* — `(source: alert-banner.tsx appliedId, Pavan 2026-10-14)`
5. **One kind at a time, most serious first, ‹ 1 of N ›** — the banner itself stays the info blue (rule 14); the shown kind's severity is in its tag — *same stepper as the sheet's Pending strip* — `(source: interactions §7a; Pavan 2026-10-25)`
6. **✕ is always last on the right; snooze sits beside the stepper** — *dismissing is the way out, so it sits at the edge* — `(source: alert-banner.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-13)`
7. **Snooze = `BANNER_SNOOZE_OPTIONS` + Custom… (hours and minutes, no calendar)** — *the Sales Orders set became the one standard* — `(source: banner.tsx; interactions §7a)`
8. **Title pressable with no underline** (pointer + slight fade) — *the underline was rejected; pointer and fade say it acts* — `(source: alert-banner.tsx PRESSABLE, Pavan 2026-10-13)`
9. **Fills are SOLID, never opacity** — banner = the info blue at 4% mixed into the content surface, rim at 16% — *see-through washes read differently on every ground* — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-25)`
10. **Padding stays 12px sides / 6px top-bottom** — *white fill and 6px/4px were tried and reverted* — `(source: memory feedback_banner_final 2026-10-14)`
11. **Tag pills sweep (collapsed only); every CTA shines — added by the banner, not by screens; the count pill does NOT sweep** — *one component, one behavior across modules* — `(source: ui §10l, Pavan 2026-10-20, 2026-10-26)`
12. **Opened items sit on white inside the banner's coloured outline; rich facts are the record's own readings in equal columns** — *a tank is drawn the way the app draws it everywhere* — `(source: alert-banner.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-20)`
13. **On every list the banner sits under the toolbar (search, Filters, chips), right above the table or board** — *the tools first, then what needs attention, then the list it narrows* — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-25)`.
14. **The banner is always the info blue; the pills carry the severity** (red Overdue/Needs action, amber caution…) — *a banner is information; the pill says how serious* — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-25)`.
15. **View all** beside ‹ 1 of N › lays every alert out as its own banner, one under another; the first carries **Collapse** to fold them back to one at a time — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-25)`.
16. **Every alert wears the same anatomy** — tag pill (icon · label · count inside) · title · ghost "View details" that filters the list → "✕ Clear filter". An alert with items still filters; no count/chevron pill outside the tag — *"Needs action" looked different from the rest* — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26; misses M-003)`
17. **Tag pills are WHITE with the severity ink** for the words, the icon and a 1px rim (red for Overdue/Needs action/Exception, the lane yellow for At risk/Due soon); the count inside the tag sits on a very light fill of the tag colour (12%) — *the banner is blue; the pill alone says how serious* — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
18. **A count pill outside a tag (non-filtering alerts) is a regular neutral pill** — grey fill, dark text, no sweep — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Pass `onApply` for every alert that is about a slice of the list; let the banner render "View details".
- ✅ Order alerts most serious first; let the screen filter out snoozed/dismissed ones.
- ✅ Give item actions `variant="outline" size="sm"` + `bannerSoftAction` — the banner adds the shine.
- ✅ Write the title as a sentence with the number: "2 invoices are past due".
- ❌ Add a "Details" word, an underline on the title, or a divider before the count pill.
- ❌ Hand-add sweeps or shines in a screen.
- ❌ Put anything after ✕.
- ❌ Use `AlertBanner` for a single static message (use `BannerCallout`/`BannerInline`).
- ❌ Use a calendar for custom snooze.

## Checks
- Eval: `tokens` (no raw colors), `wcag-contrast` (soft fills/ink pairs), `microcopy` (titles/labels), `parity-shell`.
- By eye: tag pill has icon inside; ✕ last; stepper only when N > 1; pills sweep only collapsed; every outline CTA shines; filtering alerts show "View details" → "✕ Clear filter"; banner position relative to the toolbar per module.

## Reference
- Customer Portal › Orders — four kinds: Needs action (nearly empty tanks, item list with tank facts + "Create Order"), Overdue (invoices past due), Exception (partial deliveries, "Order the rest"), At risk (late).
- Customer Portal › Invoices — Overdue · Disputed · Due soon.
- Sales › Orders — one kind: Overdue ("N urgent orders overdue for dispatch").
- Service Work › Service Orders — Needs action (urgent, no technician) · Needs action (needs approval) · Overdue (payment delayed), each a facet filter.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/app-shell.md ===== -->

# App shell

`id: unit.app-shell` · `layer: unit` · `code: src/components/app-shell, prototypes/unified-view/ModuleSidebar.tsx, src/components/top-bar/TopBar.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/GlobalSearch.tsx, src/components/theme/AccessibilityPanel.tsx, src/components/theme/AccessibilityIcon.tsx, src/components/theme/a11y.ts, prototypes/unified-view/ModuleSkeleton.tsx` · `registry: fpds-app-shell`

## Purpose
The frame every page sits in: the top bar (brand, global search, notifications, account), the one-panel navigation (modules and their pages, Accessibility at its foot) and the content layer. It answers "where am I, where can I go, and how do I find anything".

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when building any page of the Unified View (3PL, Sales, Service Work, Pricing, Lease) or the Customer Portal — the portal reuses `AppShell`, `TopBar` and `ModuleSidebar` with one module.
- Use `ModuleSkeleton` for a module's page while its data loads after a module switch.
- Don't use the two-layer rail + drawer (`NavRail` + `NavigationDrawer`) — it is kept behind `NAV_STYLE = "two-layer"` and comes back only when Pavan says "Bring in the two-layer navigation drawers".
- Don't put a theme toggle in the top bar — Light/Dark lives only in the Accessibility panel.
- Don't put Profile or Settings in the navigation — they are in the account menu.

## Anatomy
1. **`AppShell`** on the shell ground (`className="bg-fp-shell-ground"`), `headerDivider={false}`.
2. **`TopBar`** (full width, top): left — brand tile (`Boxes` icon) + "FleetPanda"; center — search field (`Search` icon, placeholder "Search everything", `⌘K` `Kbd`) + Keyboard shortcuts ghost icon (opens the shortcuts `SideSheet`); right — Notifications bell (brand dot when unread > 0) + Account menu (avatar + chevron → name/email label · Profile · Settings · separator · Log out).
3. **`ModuleSidebar`** (left, in a box): collapse button in the top-right corner · module rows (open module first, then the rest in `APP_MODULES` order: 3PL · Sales · Service Work · Pricing · Lease) · under the open module its pages on a guide line — loose pages, small uppercase section headings for areas with sub-pages (3PL Orders, Directory), or a **tree** (`nested`) where a page keeps its row and its pages hang under it on bars (Customer Portal Orders; UV `SUB_NAV`) · a divider · **Accessibility** item (`AccessibilityIcon` + "Accessibility") always in view at the foot.
4. **Collapsed rail**: expand button · rule · the open module's pages as icons (sections split by short rules) · rule · other modules as icons · Accessibility icon at the bottom; every icon has a right-side tooltip.
5. **Module-switch progress**: a 2px bar along the bottom of the chosen row + "Loading data for this module." under it.
6. **Accessibility panel** (fixed, pinned beside the nav at the window's foot): header "Accessibility" + Reset icon · Theme (Light | Dark) · Text weight (steps) · Text size (steps, − / +) · Component width & space (steps 100–150%: lanes and table columns widen) · Stroke contrast · Fill contrast · divider · Display size (steps 50–150%, − / +) · Screen (Default / Laptop / Tablet / Monitor / Large monitor / TV).
7. **Content layer**: page title (`h1 text-xl font-semibold`) + muted count outside the container; the page body inside (see the page units).
8. **`GlobalSearch`** dialog (opens from the top-bar field or ⌘K): query row (`Search` icon · input · `Esc`) · scope tabs + live count · results grouped by kind (icon tile · title · subtitle · kind `Pill`) · keyboard-hint footer.
9. **`ModuleSkeleton`**: tabs + right controls · toolbar · table header + 9 rows, all shimmering `Skeleton` blocks; the page title is a `Skeleton` too.

## Look
- **Shell tokens** (`--fp-shell-*`): ground `bg-fp-shell-ground` (everything but the content layer, top bar included), nav box `bg-fp-shell-panel`, selected/hover item `bg-fp-shell-selected`, guide line and dividers `bg-fp-shell-guide` / `border-fp-shell-guide`, current-page marker `bg-fp-shell-marker` (a 2px bar sitting on the guide line).
- **Nav box:** `rounded-lg`, no rim, 8px clear of the edges (`py-2 pl-2`); expanded width `w-(--fp-nav-width)` (`calc(15rem * var(--fp-nav-k))`), collapsed `w-14`.
- **Module row:** `h-10 rounded-lg px-2.5 text-sm`, icon 18px; open module = `font-medium`, no fill; others `text-sidebar-foreground/80`, hover `bg-fp-shell-selected`; disabled while switching `text-sidebar-foreground/35`.
- **Page row:** `h-9 rounded-lg px-2.5 text-[13px]`, icon 16px; current = `bg-fp-shell-selected font-medium` + marker; others `text-sidebar-foreground/70`. Section heading `text-[10px] font-medium tracking-wider uppercase text-muted-foreground`. Pages block `ml-5 border-l border-fp-shell-guide pl-2.5`.
- **Collapse/expand button:** `size-6`, `text-muted-foreground/40`, hover `text-muted-foreground`.
- **Progress bar:** `h-0.5 bg-brand`, CSS `.fp-nav-progress` 3000ms linear; note `text-xs text-muted-foreground`, flush with the row (`px-2.5`), no box.
- **Top bar:** `h-12`, `border-b border-border`, no fill of its own (`bg-transparent`, sits on the shell ground), `px-3 gap-3`; brand tile `size-8 rounded-lg bg-brand text-brand-foreground`; search `h-9 rounded-md`, no rim (`border-transparent`), `max-w-md`; unread dot `size-2 bg-brand ring-2 ring-background`. `data-fp-frame` on the bar, `data-fp-on-frame` on what sits straight on it.
- **Global search:** `Dialog` `sm:max-w-2xl`, `top-[12%]`, no padding; result icon tile `size-8 rounded-md bg-fp-card-1`; active scope `bg-accent font-medium`; list `max-h-[52vh]`.
- **Accessibility panel:** `rounded-md border border-border bg-popover shadow-md`, `w-80`, `max-h-[calc(var(--fp-vh)*0.85)]`, scrolls inside; rows `text-sm font-medium` label + value, slider, `text-xs` end labels; `gap-6` body `p-4`.
- **Skeleton:** DS `Skeleton` = `bg-accent` block with the `.fp-shimmer` sweep (1.4s), never a pulse; table header block on `bg-fp-table-header`, rows `h-14`.

## States
- **default** — open module at the top with its pages unfolded; current page marked.
- **hover** — rows `bg-fp-shell-selected`; top-bar icons ghost hover.
- **focus** — `ring-2 ring-ring/50` on every nav button.
- **selected** — current page `aria-current="page"`, fill + green marker (tree children show no guide marker).
- **collapsed** — icon rail; preference saved (`fp-unified-sidebar-collapsed`).
- **loading (module switch)** — chosen row runs the progress bar with "Loading data for this module." (`role="status"`); every other module row and the left module's pages are disabled and faint until the new module has unfolded; page title and content are `ModuleSkeleton`.
- **open** — Accessibility item filled while its panel is open; account menu; search dialog.
- **disabled** — Reset icon disabled when all accessibility settings are default; Screen size slider disabled while Auto is on.
- **unread** — brand dot on the bell when `unread > 0`.
- **search empty** — no query: "Recent" (All scope only) + "Pages"; no match: "No results for "…"" (+ " in <scope>").

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press another module row | 2px brand bar fills the row L→R over 3s, "Loading data for this module." shows, page becomes `ModuleSkeleton`; then the module switches: pages fold (200ms) → rows glide to the new order (260ms) → new pages unfold (240ms) |
| Press a module row with reduced motion on | Switches at once, no bar or fold/move/unfold |
| Press another module while one is loading | Nothing — the other rows are disabled |
| Press a page row | Navigates to that page; it becomes current |
| Press a collapsed-rail module icon | Switches module (no progress bar) |
| Press the corner collapse / rail expand button | Collapses to / expands from the icon rail; remembered |
| Press Accessibility | Opens the pinned panel; focus moves into it |
| Escape or press outside the panel | Closes it; focus returns to the item (menus the panel opens don't count as outside) |
| Change Theme / a slider in the panel | Page changes live, setting saved (`fp-theme-choice`, `fp-a11y`), applies in every module |
| Press Reset (or ⌘⌥0 / Ctrl+Alt+0, or `?a11y=reset`) | Every accessibility setting back to default; theme kept |
| Toggle Auto on Screen size | Zoom picked from this screen's width; slider disabled |
| Click/focus the top-bar search, or ⌘K / Ctrl-K | Opens `GlobalSearch` (the field itself is read-only) |
| Type in Global search | Results across every segment, grouped, ≤6 per group + "+N more in …"; count updates |
| Tab in Global search | Cycles the scope |
| ↑ ↓ / ↵ in Global search | Moves the active row / opens it: switches module + page, opens the record if any, closes the dialog |
| Esc in Global search | Closes; next open starts empty on All |
| Press Keyboard shortcuts | Opens the shortcuts side sheet |
| Press the avatar | Account menu: Profile · Settings · Log out |

## Outcomes
- A different page or module on screen (with its skeleton first after a module switch).
- A record's sheet opened from search.
- Accessibility settings saved and applied app-wide; or reset.
- Navigation collapsed/expanded and remembered.
- Nothing, when pressing a disabled module during a switch or the already-open module.

## Data contract
- `ModuleSidebar { modules[{key,name,icon}], moduleNav, subNav, activeModule, activeArea, activeSub, onModuleChange(key), onLoadingChange?(key|null), onNavigate(area, sub), nested?[] }`.
- `TopBar { user{name,email,fallback,avatarSrc?}, unread?, onSearchActivate?, searchPlaceholder?, onProfile?, onSettings?, onLogout? }` — without `onSearchActivate` the field is a plain input.
- `GlobalSearch { open, onOpenChange, onNavigate(moduleKey, navKey, recordId|null) }`; index from `searchIndex` (`searchAll`, `SEARCH_SCOPES`, `groupByKind`).
- `a11y.ts`: `useTheme`, `useA11y`, `setTheme`, `setA11y`, `resetA11y`, `A11y { text, stroke, background, ink, screen }`, `SCREENS` (Laptop 16″ 100% · Monitor 24″ 110% · Large monitor 34″ 125% · TV 150%).
- `ModuleSkeleton` — no props; shown while `onLoadingChange` reports a module.

## Rules
1. **One left panel** — modules as rows, the open module's pages under it, collapsible to an icon rail; the two-layer design stays archived behind `NAV_STYLE` — *after the Lovable reference, navigation menu only* — `(source: memory project_nav_style, 2026-10-06; ModuleSidebar.tsx)`. This supersedes the 52px rail + drawer in docs/ds/ux/2-rules.md "Navigation — two tiers".
2. **The open module sits first; nothing else marks it** (weight, no fill, no ring) — `(source: project_nav_style, 2026-10-07)`.
3. **Module switch is sequential: progress → fold → move → unfold** — *Pavan rejected all-at-once; modules take 3–5s to load* — `(source: project_nav_style 2026-10-07/08/09)`.
4. **While a module loads, everything else is locked and the page is a skeleton in its own shape** — *nothing should suggest another module can be picked; the page arrives in the shape it is about to take* — `(source: project_nav_style 2026-10-10, 2026-10-16; docs/ds/ui/2-rules.md §11c)`.
5. **Skeletons shimmer, never pulse** — `(source: ui §11c)`.
6. **A page with sub-pages stays a page row, its pages on tree bars** (`nested`) — `(source: project_nav_style 2026-10-15)`.
7. **Module order 3PL · Sales · Service Work · Pricing · Lease** — `(source: project_nav_style 2026-10-09)`.
8. **Accessibility lives at the foot of the navigation in every module; no header theme icon** — `(source: ui §10n; memory feedback_wcag_modes)`.
9. **Panel order: Theme · Text weight · Text size · Component width & space · Stroke contrast · Fill contrast · divider · Display size · Screen; Reset is the icon alone** — no Text color slider (text follows Fill contrast) — `(source: ui §10n, Pavan 2026-10-21 → 2026-10-26)`
10. **Text size grows the text only** — no box widens; text wraps inside its box and the box grows taller; record numbers and dates never wrap — their table column widens just enough — *"only the text size should increase without the card size increasing"* — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-23)`
11. **Always reachable, always resettable** — panel pinned beside the nav, never wider/taller than the window; ⌘⌥0 and `?a11y=reset`; a narrow zoomed window scrolls sideways — `(source: ui §10n)`.
12. **The brand fill never moves** with Contrast — `(source: feedback_wcag_modes 2026-10-21)`.
13. **Anything sized to the window uses `var(--fp-vw)` / `var(--fp-vh)`** — *a zoomed page multiplies viewport units* — `(source: ui §10n)`.
14. **The top bar has no fill of its own and the search has no rim** — *it sits on the shell ground, which tints it* — `(source: TopBar.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-06)`.
15. **Anything new that sits straight on the shell ground gets `data-fp-on-frame`** — it turns white when the frame goes dark at Contrast ≥ 80 — `(source: ui §10n)`.
16. **Profile and Settings are account destinations** — in the account menu, never in the navigation — `(source: docs/unified-view/2-layouts.md §2)`.
17. **Title and primary CTA sit outside the content container; everything that acts on the data inside** — `(source: 2-layouts.md §1)`.
18. **Sizes are STEPS with − and +** (a dot per step), like Android's "Display size and text"; no preview card — the page is the preview — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-23)`
19. **Component width & space (100–150%, 10% steps) widens board lanes and table columns** — cards follow the lane and keep their padding; cut-off text shows once it fits; nav and sheets keep their width — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-25)`
20. **Display size (50 · 75 · 100 · 125 · 150%, 100 in the middle) zooms everything together; no Auto, not tied to monitors** — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-23)`
21. **Text weight sits on top: six steps, each adding 25 to every weight together** — regular, medium, semibold and bold rise by the same amount, so the hierarchy holds — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
22. **Screen (after Display size): Default · Laptop · Tablet · Monitor · Large monitor · TV or projector** — Default changes nothing; each screen sets the zoom it is read at (×1, ×1, ×0.9, ×1.1, ×1.25, ×1.5), multiplied with Display size — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Reuse `ModuleSidebar` + `TopBar` + `AppShell` for any new module, the portal included.
- ✅ Give a new color token WCAG light + dark values, an eval pair and a place in `a11y.ts` (Stroke / Background / Ink / `ON_FILL`).
- ✅ Write a new fixed width holding text as `w-[calc(Npx*var(--fp-scale))]` or with `--fp-nav-k` / `--fp-lane-k`.
- ❌ Don't add section headings inside a module's collapsed rail beyond the short rules, or a second drawer.
- ❌ Don't use `100vw`, `100vh` or `h-screen`.
- ❌ Don't make the Accessibility panel a Radix popover (CSS zoom misplaces it).
- ❌ Don't fill a surface with a stroke token.

## Checks
- `parity-shell` — G52 Dashboard first in the rail, module opens on Orders.
- `wcag-contrast` — every bg+text pairing and the hand list pass at the WCAG and Maximum ends.
- `tokens` — no raw hex.
- `fpds-cards`, `fpds-units` — this card's format.
- By eye: module switch runs bar → fold → move → unfold; other rows faint while loading; skeleton matches the page shape; panel opens beside the nav at every Screen size; text grows without cut-offs; frame turns dark at Contrast ≥ 80 with white text on it.

## Reference
- Unified View — every page (3PL › Delivery Orders, Sales › Orders, Service Work › Service Orders); switch modules from the sidebar to see the progress bar and skeleton.
- Customer Portal — same shell, one module, Orders as a tree (Order Requests · Recurring Orders).
- Top bar ⌘K → Global search (Unified View).
- Navigation foot › Accessibility panel (all modules).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/board.md ===== -->

# Board

`id: unit.board` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/threepl/BrokerBoard.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/ListToolbar.tsx (BoardWithBar, cardFields)` · `registry: fpds-board`

## Purpose
Shows a list's records as cards in lanes, one lane per stage of the record's lifecycle, read left to right. Answers "where is every order in the work, and which ones need me?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when the records move through a status workflow (orders, service work) and the list offers a board view (`ListToolbar boardView`).
- Use when a stage's cards need to say different things per stage (card fields are per lane).
- Don't use when the list has no workflow — Customers, Assets, Directory lists, standing arrangements (no lanes to put them in) → use the list table.
- Don't use when the question is geographic ("what else goes out near this one") → use `unit.map`.
- Don't use for a one-view list: no view switch, no board icon (ui §10h).

## Anatomy
1. **View bar** (`BoardWithBar`) — the top strip of the board's own box, a line under it, the lanes below in the same box — attached like the table's bar (2026-10-25). Left: the statement ("62 orders across 9 of 9 lanes · …"). Right: the board's own tool, **Card fields**.
2. **Lane row** — lanes side by side, scrolling sideways.
3. **Apart line** — the first lane that is not a step of the work (Canceled, Blocked) has a 3px left line instead of the 1px one.
4. **Lane** — a column of the board's box: header (title as a pill in the lane colour · count pill · lane sort icon) with a line under it → scrollable card stack, or the empty label. No colour strip (2026-10-25).
5. **Card** (the whole card is the button):
   - Head: order number (mono) + type `CodeDisc` beside it · badges (pills) at the right · optional subtitle lines (e.g. a movement's route).
   - **Units**, grouped by spacing, no rules between them (2026-10-25): a 10px uppercase heading + one shape — `stack` (lines at three levels, optional figure at the right), `inline` (short values split by a hairline), `rows` (asset · product · qty, optional dashed-rule Total), `quotes` — a light green band tucked 4px inside the card on every side (`-mx-2 rounded-sm bg-fp-quote-band px-2 py-1`, card `pb-1` when it is last), text on the card's 12px line, heading inside, vendor + price, "Best price"/"2nd best" as text, ‹ 1/N › stepper at the bottom right, under the price (24px targets); price may open a breakdown popover. As the last unit it closes the card, and the card drops its "N of N quotes — review pricing" alert.
   - **Alert strip** (optional) — info icon + text in the alert color, optional outline action at the right.
   - **Footer action** (optional) — one state label at the left (with a tick when waiting), the act that moves it on at the right.
6. **Lane sort menu** — "Sort <lane> by", radio list: "Lane order" first, then the lane's own option, then the common options.
7. **Card fields menu** (toolbar, board only) — scope select (All lanes / one lane) above the ticks, ticks per unit with child lines nested, "Show all", counter "shown/total".

## Look
- Board: the lanes edge to edge inside `BoardWithBar`'s box, horizontal scroll. Lanes `border-l border-border first:border-l-0`; apart lane `border-l-3 border-fp-outline-2`.
- Box + view bar: `rounded-md border border-border bg-fp-surface-1b`; the bar is its top strip with `border-b border-fp-outline-1`.
- Lane: width `--fp-board-lane-width` (340px × `--fp-lane-k`, Accessibility › Component size), square, `bg-fp-surface-1b`. Title pill `rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5` in `bg-fp-lane-<color>-vivid text-fp-lane-<color>-vivid-ink` (2026-10-27, after a ClickUp board: a saturated fill, white text — dark on yellow — the same in both themes, white at AA; deeper at the WCAG end for AAA; gray, blue, yellow, green, cyan, purple, violet, pink, orange, red).
- Lane header `border-b border-border px-5 py-3`; title `text-sm font-semibold`; count pill `bg-fp-lane-count` (#dde1e7 light / #3d4550 dark — a step off the lane, 2026-10-27), `text-[11px] font-medium`, neutral ink (never lane-coloured with white text). Sort icon `ArrowDownUp` size-4, `text-muted-foreground`, `text-foreground` when a sort is chosen.
- Card stack `gap-3 px-5 py-4` (20px either side of the cards), `overflow-y-auto`; cards `shrink-0`.
- Card: `rounded-md border border-fp-outline-1 bg-fp-surface-1b`; `pb-3`; head `px-3 pt-3`, number `font-mono text-sm font-semibold`; badges = DS `Pill` (h-7). Units `px-3 pt-4 gap-1.5` — 12px inset, 16px between units, 6px inside; unit heading `text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.14em] uppercase text-muted-foreground`.
- Stack levels: 1 `text-sm font-semibold text-foreground` · 2 `text-[13px] font-medium text-muted-foreground` · 3 `text-xs text-muted-foreground`. Figures / quote price `text-lg font-semibold tabular-nums` (the largest thing on the card). Inline hairline `h-3.5 w-px bg-fp-outline-2`. Rows grid `1fr · 1.05fr · auto`, asset id `font-mono text-[11px]`, Top-off qty soft (`font-medium text-muted-foreground`).
- Alert strip: `px-3 pt-4 text-xs`; text color `text-fp-banner-accent-caution` / `-warning` / `-success` or `text-foreground` (default); action `Button outline xs`.
- Footer: `border-t border-fp-outline-1 px-3.5 py-2.5`, label `text-xs`; act `Button` variant `success` (filled) or `outline`, size `xs`.

## States
- **Card default** — outline-1 rim on surface-1b.
- **Card hover** — rim `fp-outline-2` + `shadow-fp-surface`.
- **Card focus** — `border-ring` + 2px `ring-ring/50`.
- **Card active/pressed** — rim outline-2, fill `bg-fp-card-1`.
- **Lane empty** — the stack is replaced by `emptyLabel` in `text-xs text-muted-foreground` ("No orders in quoting"; default "Nothing here").
- **Lane sort chosen** — icon in body ink; aria-label names the chosen sort.
- **Quote stepper** — chevrons disabled (30% opacity) at the first / last quote.
- **Footer waiting** — muted label with a tick, no button.
- **Long lane** — cards keep full height, the lane scrolls (cards never compress).

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click a card, or Enter / Space on a focused card | Opens the record's sheet (`onOpenCard(id)`). |
| Click the alert strip's action (e.g. "Select Vendor", "Review", "View") | Runs that action only — the card does not also open (CP opens the order). Clicking a card that carries quotes opens the 3PL sheet at the Pricing heading. |
| Click the footer act (e.g. "Request invoice") | Runs the act; the card does not open. Lane does not change for invoice acts. |
| Click ‹ / › on a quotes unit | Steps to the previous / next quote (cheapest first); the card does not open. |
| Click a quote price with a breakdown (dotted underline) | Popover "Delivery Details" with the Asset · Product · Quantity table and total; the card does not open. |
| Hover the Delivery Details line ("3 assets · 3 products · 2,600 gal", one dotted underline) | Hover card: Asset · Product · Quantity table on the left (every row shows, total included — at least 2 rows tall, at most 4.5, then it scrolls; white in dark, like the tooltip), ship-to map on the right at the same height — radius as a dashed circle, no site pin, every asset at the site inside it (the order's highlighted, the rest dimmed); hover a dot for its name and lat/lng. No map on the card. |
| Click the lane's sort icon | Menu "Sort <lane> by" with "Lane order" + options. |
| Pick a sort option | That lane reorders by it; only that lane. "Lane order" restores the lane's own order. |
| Card fields → scope select | Switches what the ticks govern: All lanes or one lane. |
| Card fields → tick a unit heading | Shows/hides the unit with all its lines, in the scoped lanes. |
| Card fields → untick the last line of a unit | The heading goes too (a heading with nothing under it is not a unit). |
| Card fields → Show all | Every unit on in the scoped lanes; disabled when nothing is hidden. |
| Drag a card | Nothing — there is no drag between lanes. |
| Switch to table / map, then back | Board returns with its lane sorts and card fields (part of the saved view). |

## Outcomes
- A record sheet opens (card click, or alert/footer act that opens it).
- A lane is reordered, by a named sort, kept in the saved view.
- Card fields changed per lane or for all lanes, kept in the saved view.
- An act runs from the card footer (the record's own state moves on, e.g. invoice requested; the lane stays).
- Empty lane shows its empty label; the lane still stands.
- No records at all → the list's empty body replaces the board (the board is not drawn).
- The board is not paged; the view bar states the total and its scope instead.

## Data contract
- `lanes: BoardLane[]` — each `key`, `title`, `color` (lane palette name), `cards`, optional `emptyLabel`, optional `apart` (its left line is 3px).
- `BoardCard` — `id`, `title` (record number), optional `code` (`text`, `label`, `tone` for the CodeDisc), `badges[]` (`label`, `tone`: critical · neutral · caution · success · info), `subtitle[]`, `units[]` (stack / inline / rows / quotes), optional `alert` (`status`: default · caution · warning · success, `text`, `actionLabel`, `onAction`), optional `footerAction` (`label`, `actionLabel`, `actionVariant` success | outline, `onAction`, `waiting`).
- `onOpenCard(id)` — reports the opened card.
- `laneSortOptions(laneKey) → {key,label}[]`, `laneSort` (chosen per lane), `onLaneSortChange(laneKey, option | null)`.
- Card fields (`ListToolbar cardFields`): `units`, `hidden`, `scope`, `scopes`, `onScopeChange`, `onToggle(key)`, `onShowAll()`.
- `BoardWithBar barRef` — the toolbar renders the statement and tools into the bar.

## Rules
1. **Lanes are the lifecycle, in order** — *sorting by status sorts by lane position; a board read left to right claims each lane follows the one before.* `(source: docs/unified-view/2-layouts.md §6)`
2. **A lane that is not a step sits apart, behind a 3px line** (Canceled, Blocked) — *a gap alone reads as a mistake; the rule tells the reader the lanes after it are somewhere else, not the end of the line.* `(source: interactions/2-rules.md §8.1b; BrokerBoard.tsx)`
3. **No drag between lanes** — *a stage change is a workflow act with side effects (mail leaves, accounting push), not a reorder; stages change from the record's next-act control.* `(source: interactions/2-rules.md §8.1b)`
4. **A card is units grouped by spacing, no rules, no field labels inside** — *the heading names the unit and weight carries the rest; spacing groups without drawing lines (2026-10-25).* `(source: interactions/2-rules.md §8.1b)`
5. **A heading with nothing under it is not a unit** — *it reads as a failure to load.* `(source: interactions/2-rules.md §8.1b)`
6. **Card fields are per lane, scope control above the ticks** — *what a first-lane card should say is not what a last-lane card should; on All lanes a field is ON only when on in every lane.* `(source: interactions/2-rules.md §8.1b)`
7. **An alert only on a card with something outstanding** — *an alert on every card is a border.* `(source: 2-layouts §6)`
8. **Lane sort is per lane, named, and one option may belong to the lane** — *which end of a queue you work from has a reason; the reason is the label. "Lane order" is a choice, not the absence of one.* `(source: BrokerBoard.tsx; interactions §8.1b)`
9. **The whole card is the button; no "View details" row** — *a strip naming that is a label for what the reader already stands on.* `(source: BrokerBoard.tsx)`
10. **Every control on a card stops the click** — *otherwise stepping a quote or pressing an act also opens the order.* `(source: BrokerBoard.tsx)`
11. **Count pill is neutral, the lane's color lives in the title pill's vivid fill** — *white text holds AA because the vivid fills are deepened for it; the count stays neutral so the lane reads one colour.* `(source: BrokerBoard.tsx DS follow-up 8)`
12. **Cards never shrink; the lane scrolls** — *a compressed card clips its content silently.* `(source: BrokerBoard.tsx DS follow-up 7)`
13. **Lane color = the status it ends on, by the binding table** (gray starts, red stops, green finishes, purple hands off) — *a hue must not mean two things in two modules.* `(source: docs/unified-view/1-components.md §5 Lane fills)`
14. **Type is a code disc on board cards only** (tables use a pill with the full word) — *on a card space is a letter.* `(source: ui/2-rules.md §11b)`
15. **Badges are pills coloured by kind; render the absence** — *a chip every card carries says nothing (no "Normal" priority, no status repeating the lane).* `(source: ui/2-rules.md §10b, §11b)`
16. **Footer act is filled (`success`)** — *an outline read as one more thing to look at, not the thing to press.* `(source: BrokerBoard.tsx)`
17. **Board view bar is the top strip of the board's box, statement left, Card fields right** — *every list with a table or board has one; the statement replaces the footer total.* `(source: ui/2-rules.md §10p, 2026-10-22)`
18. **Lane ground one step darker (`--fp-card-1`)** — *on older laptops the lighter lane washed into the white layer.* `(source: src/index.css; memory 2026-10-22 darker board lanes)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Reuse `BrokerBoard` + `BoardWithBar` for every board (3PL, Sales, Service, Customer Portal).
- ✅ Give each lane an `emptyLabel` naming the lane ("No orders in quoting").
- ✅ Put the quote price / figure at the card's right edge in `text-lg`.
- ✅ Offer each lane only the card fields it can draw.
- ❌ Drag-and-drop between lanes, or a per-lane "+ Create" footer.
- ❌ A lane-coloured count pill with white text.
- ❌ An alert or badge on every card; a status badge that repeats the lane title.
- ❌ Red for a lane that is just a place (Canceled is gray).
- ❌ Paginating the board.
- ❌ Reusing the table's Show/Hide columns icon for card fields.

## Checks
- `parity-shell` and `parity-sheets` (3PL guardrails on the list and the sheet the card opens); `status-kinds` (badge kinds); `wcag-contrast` (lane strip, count pill, ground); `tokens`.
- By eye: lanes in lifecycle order, 3px apart line before Canceled/Blocked, card focus ring and Enter opening, card controls not opening the card, lanes scroll with full-height cards, card fields scope above ticks, view bar statement.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders board (Orders · Quoting · Assigned · Completed · Review · Verified · Synced ┃ Canceled · Blocked).
- Sales › Fuel Orders and Asset Orders board.
- Service Work › Service Orders board.
- Customer Portal › Orders board (lane per status, Canceled apart; Awaiting pill + caution strip with "View").


> Earlier board designs (V1–V3) are archived in `docs/archive/board-versions-2026-10-25.md`.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/bulk-bar.md ===== -->

# Bulk bar & bulk edit
