`id: unit.bulk-bar` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/FuelOrdersBulkBar.tsx, src/components/tables-layout/BulkActionBar.tsx, src/components/bulk-edit/BulkEditDialog.tsx, src/components/bulk-edit/parts.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/BulkEditDialog.tsx` · `registry: fpds-bulk-bar`

## Purpose
Acts on several ticked rows at once: change one field across them, or apply an action (archive, delete, export). Answers "do this to all of these — and show me what will change before it does."

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a list table has row checkboxes and rows are ticked.
- Use the field actions + `BulkEditDialog` when the edit changes a value per row (date, time, driver, products, tags).
- Use `BulkActionBar` with its own `actions` for a list whose verbs are its own (3PL Directory).
- Don't use on a board — row ticks live in the table (Sales › Orders shows the bar in the table view only).
- Don't use for a property of the whole round (e.g. "ask these for a price") — that is a switch over the round, not a selection.
- Don't build a second bar in place — pass `actions` to the one bar.

## Anatomy
**The bar** (floating, centred over the bottom of the list), left to right:
1. **Count chip** — "N Selected" with a clear ✕ inside.
2. **Field / verb actions** — icon + label each (Fuel orders: Products · Driver · Planned date · Planned time · Add tags).
3. **Hairline** — before the destructive zone.
4. **Delete** — icon-only trash, always behind a confirm.
(DS default set, when no `actions` are passed: Assign · Unassign · Unblock · Verify · Unverify, then Copy · Delete · ··· More with a searchable, grouped field menu — Assignments · Scheduling · Order Details.)

**The bulk edit dialog**, two steps:
1. **Pick** — title = the field; description = scope + count ("Assign driver to selected **25 orders**"); body = the picker; footer Cancel · Proceed and Confirm.
2. **View Summary** — description = what the commit does; body = per-row before/after table (or standing load + orders being added); footer Go back (left) · Confirm (right).

Parts: `BeforeAfterTable` (ID · Current {field} · New {field}), `StandingItems` (what the target already holds, collapsed), `RecordMiniTable` (Order No · Order Type · ShipTo/Terminal).

## Look
- Bar: `rounded-lg`, 1px `border-fp-outline-1` (dark `fp-outline-2`), fill `bg-fp-surface-1a` (dark `bg-fp-card-1`), `p-1.5`, `gap-1`, elevation `shadow-fp-overlay`; enters with `bulk-bar-in` (slides up 1rem, fades in, 200ms ease-out).
- Placement: absolutely positioned, centred, `bottom-5` (Sales) / `bottom-4` (Directory) over the list.
- Count chip: `rounded-md bg-primary text-primary-foreground text-sm font-medium`, ✕ 14px at `primary-foreground/80`.
- Actions: `Button variant="ghost" size="sm"`, icon + label, equal weight, no primary fills.
- Hairline: `h-5 w-px bg-border`, `mx-1`.
- Delete / destructive: `size="icon-sm"` ghost, `text-destructive`, hover `bg-destructive/10` — color on the glyph only, no fill.
- Dialog: `default` size on Pick, `lg` on Summary; body scrolls (`max-h-[55vh]`), header and footer stay.
- Before cell: `text-muted-foreground line-through`, empty → "—"; after cell `font-medium`.
- `StandingItems`: `rounded-md border bg-fp-surface-1b`, list capped `max-h-56` and scrolls within itself.

## States
- **hidden** — nothing ticked (count 0) → no bar.
- **shown** — slides in when the first row is ticked.
- **edit reach 0** — Sales mixes fuel and asset orders; if none of the ticked rows is a fuel order, only Delete is offered.
- **action disabled** — greyed with a reason in `title` (e.g. "You do not have permission to archive records").
- **dialog step Pick, nothing picked** — Proceed and Confirm disabled.
- **dialog step Summary** — before/after shown; for a `destructive` replace, Confirm first arms, then reads "Yes, replace N orders".
- **dialog reopened** — always starts on step 1.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Tick a row checkbox (table view) | Bar slides in with "1 Selected" |
| Tick more / header select-all | Count updates |
| Press ✕ in the count chip | Selection clears; bar disappears |
| Press a field action (Products, Driver, Planned date, Planned time, Add tags) | `BulkEditDialog` opens on Pick, scoped "… selected N orders" |
| Pick a value | Proceed and Confirm enables |
| Press Cancel / close the dialog | Dialog closes; pending value discarded; nothing changed |
| Press Proceed and Confirm | Step 2 "View Summary" with per-row before/after (Driver: that driver's standing load on the same day + orders being added) |
| Press Go back | Back to Pick with the value kept |
| Press Confirm | Commits to every selected row; toast "Updated N orders"; dialog closes; columns recompute |
| Driver: pick "— Unassigned" | Summary says the orders return to Unassigned; Confirm clears the driver |
| Products: pick several | Each order's lines are replaced; that order's own total gallons split across them, so Qty is unchanged |
| Add tags | Tags are added to what each order carries; nothing removed (after = union) |
| Press Delete (trash) | Confirm dialog "Delete N orders?" — "This will affect N orders and cannot be undone." Cancel / Delete |
| Confirm Delete | Rows removed |
| Directory: press a destructive verb (Archive) | Same confirm, titled "{Verb} for N {noun}s?" |
| Directory: press Export selection / Add "Watch List" tag / Deactivate / Enable TopOff / Mark under maintenance | Applied to all ticked records; toast; selection clears |
| DS default bar: press ··· More | Searchable two-column field menu opens above the bar |

## Outcomes
- A field changed on every selected row, with a success toast and recomputed columns (Qty, status).
- Orders returned to Unassigned (driver cleared).
- Rows deleted or records archived/restored after a confirm.
- An export toast ("N customers exported").
- Nothing, on Cancel / closing / Go back then close.
- No bar on Sales › Orders board or map views.

## Data contract
- `FuelOrdersBulkBar`: `count`, `editCount?` (how many the field edits reach; 0 → Delete only), `onClear`, `onSetProducts(string[])`, `onSetDriver(string | null)`, `onSetPlannedDate(Date)`, `onSetPlannedTime(string)`, `onAddTags(string[])`, `onDelete`, `productOptions`, `driverOptions`, `tagOptions`, `selectedOrders`, `unselectedOrders` (for standing load).
- `BulkActionBar`: `count`, `onClear?`, `actions?: BulkAction[]` (`key`, `label`, `icon`, `disabled?`, `title?` reason, `destructive?`, `onSelect`), `tools?` (Copy/Delete/More zone; off when `actions` given), `noun?` for confirm copy.
- `BulkEditDialog`: `open`, `onOpenChange`, `title`, `scopeVerb` (composed "{scopeVerb} selected {N items}"), `count`, `itemNoun?`, `canProceed`, `children` (picker), `summary`, `summaryDescription`, `onConfirm`, `toastMessage?` (string or `false`), `destructive?`.

## Rules
1. **One bar, actions passed in** — *what can be done to a selection belongs to the records, not the bar; a second bar built in place breaks the feel of selection everywhere* — `(source: interactions §4b)`
2. **A selection starts empty and is transient** — *a row that arrives ticked answers a question nobody asked and puts a bar on screen too early; selection belongs to the visit* — `(source: interactions §4b)`
3. **Don't make a selection carry a second meaning** — *two decisions sharing one control make one default wrong* — `(source: interactions §4b)`
4. **Bar comes last on the page** — *it appears and disappears; anything below it would jump* — `(source: interactions §4b)`
5. **Compact neutral floating pill, not a toolbar; actions icon + label, equal weight, no primary fills; destructive = small red glyph** — `(source: interactions §4b; ux/2-rules)`
6. **Labels are mandatory on field actions** — *these are never icon-only* — `(source: FuelOrdersBulkBar.tsx; BulkActionBar.tsx)`
7. **Delete always confirms** ("Delete N orders?") — *irreversible across many rows* — `(source: BulkActionBar.tsx; ux/2-rules)`
8. **Pick the editor tier by cardinality** — scalar (date, time) · one value per row (driver) · collection per row (products, tags) — `(source: interactions §4b)`
9. **Two steps when a mutation changes values per row: Pick (scope + count) → per-row before/after → Confirm** — *the operator must see what already exists before replacing it; collapsing to one step removes the only chance to notice* — `(source: BulkEditDialog.tsx; interactions §4b)`
10. **The dialog always reopens on step 1** — *reopening mid-flow would show Confirm for a value no longer visible* — `(source: BulkEditDialog.tsx)`
11. **Width per step: `default` while picking, `lg` for the summary** — `(source: ux/2-rules Side-sheet, width per step)`
12. **Apply → toast → recompute across every affected row**; toast is one line — `(source: interactions §4b; memory feedback_toasts)`
13. **A dead action says why** — disabled with the reason as its title — `(source: BulkActionBar.tsx; interactions §7b)`
14. **Driver load is scoped to the selection's delivery date(s)** — *the load on the day you assign is the number that decides* — `(source: FuelOrdersBulkBar.tsx)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Show the bar only when rows are ticked, in the table view.
- ✅ Pass a list's own verbs via `actions` and a `noun` for the confirm copy.
- ✅ Show "Current / New" per row before committing a replace.
- ✅ Strike through the old value; show "—" when there was none.
- ❌ Pre-tick rows on arrival.
- ❌ Commit a field edit on the first click from a popover.
- ❌ Fill actions with brand/primary colors or tint the delete background.
- ❌ Hide inapplicable actions — show them disabled with the reason.

## Checks
- Eval: `tokens`, `microcopy` (toast and confirm wording), `parity-shell` (toasts one line).
- By eye: bar centred and floating with rim + overlay shadow; count chip primary; hairline before Delete; dialog header leads with scope + count; summary shows before/after; toast after Confirm.

## Reference
- Sales › Orders (table view) — `FuelOrdersBulkBar`: Products · Driver · Planned date · Planned time · Add tags · Delete.
- 3PL › Directory › Customers · Vendors · Products · Rental Assets — `BulkActionBar` with list verbs (Export selection, Add "Watch List" tag, Deactivate/Reactivate, Enable TopOff, Mark under maintenance, Archive/Restore).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/chip-shelf.md ===== -->

# Chip shelf

`id: unit.chip-shelf` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ListToolbar.tsx (FilterChips, ColumnSearchChip), src/index.css ([data-routed] + [data-filter-chips])` · `registry: fpds-chip-shelf`

## Purpose
The one-line strip under the toolbar that says out loud which filters are on, and takes them off. It answers "why is this list shorter than I expect, and how do I undo it?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use on every list that has a Filters panel — directly after `unit.toolbar`, inside the same `mt-4` shelf.
- Use `ColumnSearchChip` as a child when the list still has the one-column search (popover form) — it joins the same shelf.
- Don't use for the column search row's boxes — those are visible in the table itself (`unit.data-table`).
- Don't use in the Export sheet — that sheet lists narrowings as rows of chips per category (`unit.export-sheet`).
- Don't use for form token chips — same pill recipe, different unit.

## Anatomy
1. **Scroller** — one line of chips, `overflow-x-auto`, as wide as its chips.
2. **Facet chip** — one per facet, in the panel's facet order:
   - one value: `Label: ` (muted) + value (ink) + ✕;
   - several values: `Label ` (muted) + count bubble + ✕; hover opens a **hover card** listing each value with its own ✕ (heading = facet label, uppercase).
3. **Column search chip** (`ColumnSearchChip`, optional child) — `Search` glyph + `Column: "query"` + ✕.
4. **Clear all** — text button right after the last chip, outside the scroller.

## Look
- Shelf: `mt-3 flex items-center gap-2` (12px under the toolbar); chips `gap-2`.
- Chip = the removable pill: `h-7` (28px), `rounded-full`, `bg-muted`, `pl-3 pr-1`, `text-xs` regular, `text-foreground`; label part `text-muted-foreground`; `max-w-[260px]`, truncates.
- Count bubble: `rounded-full bg-background px-1.5 tabular-nums`.
- ✕: 20px round button (`size-5`), icon `size-3`, `text-muted-foreground`, `hover:bg-foreground/10 hover:text-foreground`, focus ring.
- Hover card: `min-w-[180px] max-w-[280px] p-1.5`; heading `text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.12em] uppercase text-muted-foreground`; rows `text-xs`, `hover:bg-accent/60`.
- Clear all: `text-sm text-muted-foreground`, `hover:text-foreground hover:underline underline-offset-2`.
- Routed layout (only when the toolbar hangs view tools under its icons, `data-routed`): the shelf rises into that row (`margin-top: -1.75rem`) and stops short of the right cluster (`padding-right: var(--fp-routed-w)`). Every list now uses the view bar, so this rule is dormant.

## States
- **empty** — no filter and no column search: nothing renders (no reserved height).
- **default** — chips + Clear all.
- **hover** — multi-value chip opens its hover card (120ms open/close delay); ✕ darkens.
- **focus** — ring on each ✕ and on Clear all.
- **overflow** — chips past the row's width scroll sideways; Clear all stays put.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press ✕ on a one-value chip | Removes that value; list re-narrows; page → 1. |
| Press ✕ on a multi-value chip | Removes all values of that facet. |
| Hover a multi-value chip | Hover card lists its values. |
| Press ✕ on a value in the hover card | Removes only that value; the chip's count drops (becomes a one-value chip at 1). |
| Press ✕ on the column search chip | Clears the column search. |
| Press **Clear all** | Removes every facet filter (not the search box). |
| Scroll the shelf sideways | Reveals chips past the edge. |
| Reopen the Filters panel after removing a chip | The panel shows it unticked. |

## Outcomes
- A wider list, page 1, with the chip gone.
- A facet reduced to fewer values.
- No filters at all (Clear all) — the shelf disappears.
- The active view becomes dirty (update icon / dot in `unit.view-row`), because filters are part of its state.

## Data contract
- `FilterChips`: `facets` `{key, label}[]` (panel order), `selected` (applied `facetKey:value` ids), `onChange(next)`, `className?`, `children?` (e.g. `ColumnSearchChip`).
- Ids split on the FIRST colon only (a ship-to value may hold one); ids for an undeclared facet keep their raw key and go last.
- `ColumnSearchChip`: `searchFields`, `colScope`, `colQuery`, `onClear()`, `className` (default `mt-3`; pass `""` when it joins a shelf that already has chips).

## Rules
1. **One chip per facet, not per value.** — *the shelf grows with the questions asked, not the ticks; six cities can't bury the one status filter.* — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx FilterChips)`
2. **One value shows itself; several show a count, and the hover card shows which — each with its own ✕.** — *the usual reason to look is to take ONE thing off.* — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx)`
3. **One line, always — it scrolls rather than wraps.** — *every pixel of this strip comes out of the data.* — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx)`
4. **Removable chips are pills**: 28px, fully round, `text-xs` on `bg-muted`, label muted, value in ink, ✕ inside as a 20px round button. — `(source: ui §11b, Pavan 2026-10-09)`
5. **Clear all sits right after the last chip**, `text-sm` muted, underline on hover — never pushed to the far end; pinned outside the scroller. — *the way out of a narrowing must not be somewhere you scroll to.* — `(source: ui §11b; ListToolbar.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-09)`
6. **Chips follow the panel's facet order.** — *the shelf and the menu read the same way round.* — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx)`
7. **A narrowing that is on is visible as a chip, and the chip removes it.** — `(source: docs/unified-view/3-interactions.md §7)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Place it in the same shelf as the toolbar: `<div className="mt-4 shrink-0"><ListToolbar/><FilterChips/></div>`.
- ✅ Pass the same `facets` the Filters panel uses.
- ❌ One chip per value, or a wrapping second line.
- ❌ Push Clear all to the far right, or put it inside the scroller.
- ❌ Bordered or square chips; a ✕ outside the pill.

## Checks
- `parity-lists` G7 — applied filters show as `FilterChips` on the screen.
- `tokens` — no raw colors on chips.
- `fpds-units` — chip anatomy and Clear all position.
- By eye: chips 28px pills; Clear all directly after the last chip; nothing renders when nothing is applied.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders, 3PL › Recurring Orders, 3PL › Directory.
- Sales › Orders, Sales › Quotes, Sales › Customers, Sales › Assets.
- Service Work › Service Orders and every Service Work reference page.
- Customer Portal › Orders, › Invoices, › Sites & Assets, › Team & Access.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/dashboard.md ===== -->

# Dashboard tiles & cards

`id: unit.dashboard` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/DashboardParts.tsx, src/components/ui/term-icons.tsx, src/components/ui/trend-chip.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/threepl/ThreePLDashboard.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/SalesDashboard.tsx, prototypes/customer-portal/DashboardScreen.tsx` · `registry: fpds-dashboard`

## Purpose
A module's overview: a few figures, charts and short tables that say how much work there is, where it sits and what needs somebody now. In the Unified View every figure is a way into the list it counts; in the Customer Portal it is a few things for the customer to glance at, with "Attention required" first.

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a module needs an overview page — 3PL and Sales (Unified View, built from `DashboardParts`) or the Customer Portal landing page.
- Use the figure-card style (figure · `TrendChip` · "vs …" line · `MetricIcon`) for any metric card that leads with a number, in any module.
- Don't use for a list's own filtering cards above a table — those are list overview cards, not a dashboard.
- Don't use for a banner that acts on a list — use `unit.alert-banner`. The dashboard's "Attention required" is a different pattern by design.
- Don't use for a report to generate and take away — Customer Portal "Delivery performance" is a report on the Reports page, not a dashboard card.
- Don't build a cross-module dashboard; Lease and Pricing have none.

## Anatomy
**Unified View (3PL, Sales) — `DashboardParts`, top to bottom:**
1. Scope row — line segment (`ToggleGroup`, `LINE_SCOPES`: All · Fuel · Lubricants · Propane) left, `DateRangeFilter` right (3PL).
2. `DASHBOARD_GROUND` — the gray ground that wraps every card.
3. Stat row — four `StatTile`s (label · figure · note), 2 across, 4 from `lg`.
4. `Panel`s in two-up rows — title + `aside` (muted count) header, then one of:
   - `BarColumns` — vertical bars, value above, label under, peak lit;
   - `BarList` — ranked rows (name, figure, share %, bar under the name);
   - `BarList share` — one segmented bar + a dot-and-name row per part;
   - a short DS `Table` (e.g. Partner spend, Needs attention with `StatusChip`).

**Customer Portal — `DashboardScreen`, top to bottom:**
1. "Attention required" — collapsed banner header (bell disc · title · count pill · chevron); opened, a grid of tiles: `CreditHoldTile` (one tile for all credit holds) and `ActionTile`s (icon tile · "<order no.>: Problem" · ≤6-word line · act pill · facts grid · planned date + countdown).
2. `Section` "Schedule for the next 7 days" — one card, four `ScheduleTile`s ruled apart (Deliveries · Products · Gallons · Ship-tos), each: title · figure + unit + `TrendChip` · "vs N the previous 7 days" · `MetricIcon` beside; round ↗ button in the card's corner.
3. `Section` "Tank monitor readings" — `TankHealthCard`.
4. `Section` "Spend" — range toggle (Last 4/8/12 weeks) in the section's `aside`; `SpendCard`, `SpendByProductCard`.
5. `Section` "Billing & credit" — `InvoicesCard`, `CreditCard` (only where the tenant gives credit).
6. Portal `Card` parts: title (+ ⓘ `note` explainer) · `aside` · body; `bare` = part of a shared card, set apart by a divider; `Legend` under charts; `QuietPattern` fills an empty stretch of a card.

## Look
- **UV ground and cards:** ground `DASHBOARD_GROUND` = `rounded-xl bg-fp-surface-1a p-3` (dark: `bg-fp-surface-1b`); cards `rounded-xl bg-fp-surface-1b shadow-xs` (dark: `bg-fp-surface-1a`), no rim. Gaps `gap-3`.
- **Panel:** header `px-5 pt-4`, title `text-base font-semibold`, aside `text-xs text-muted-foreground`; body `px-5 pt-4 pb-5`.
- **StatTile:** `p-5`, label `text-sm font-medium`, figure `text-3xl font-semibold tabular-nums`, note `text-xs text-muted-foreground`.
- **Charts color by job, from `--fp-viz-*`:** a count over stages/time = one series, slot `fp-viz-1`, the peak bar solid, the rest `fp-viz-1/15` (hover `/40`; dark `/25`, `/50`); a zero bar `bg-fp-outline-1` at 3px. A ranking = `fp-viz-1` throughout on a `fp-viz-1/12` track (dark `/20`). A share = `fp-viz-1…8` in fixed order, 2px gaps, rounded only at the two ends, a `size-2.5` dot beside each name. Bars: 4px rounded top, 2px gap, `max-w-11`, default height 140.
- **Portal cards:** `rounded-xl border border-border bg-fp-surface-1b`, `p-4`, `gap-4`; card title `text-sm font-semibold`; section title `text-base font-semibold`; sections `gap-3`, page `gap-4`.
- **Metric icon:** `MetricIcon` — 48px `rounded-lg` tile, `bg-accent-violet/6`, icon 28px, line 1.75, `text-accent-violet`. Icons from `TERM_ICONS`: deliveries `TruckIcon`, products `DropIcon`, gallons `DrumIcon`, ship-tos `PinIcon` (Lucide 24 grid, 22% soft fill, wavy level for liquids).
- **TrendChip:** `rounded-md`, `h-7 px-2 text-sm` (md) / `h-5 px-1.5 text-xs` (sm); good = `bg-fp-trend-up-bg text-fp-trend-up`, bad = `bg-fp-trend-down-bg text-fp-trend-down`, same = `bg-muted text-muted-foreground`; a small filled triangle ▲/▼.
- **Attention required:** `border-status-processing/20 bg-status-processing/5`, soft blurred `status-processing/10` glow top-right; tiles `rounded-xl border border-border bg-fp-surface-1b`, icon tile `size-10 rounded-lg` in the violet pill's colors (`pillColorClass("neutral","violet")`); countdown `text-status-failed` when ≤ 2 days, else `text-status-processing`.

## States
- **default** — figures and charts for the current scope/range.
- **hover** — StatTile `shadow-sm`; bar tint deepens; BarList row `bg-accent/50`; share segment `opacity-80`; portal headline figure softens to `opacity-60` (no underline); action tile rim `fp-outline-2`. Every chart mark has a tooltip (label · detail).
- **focus** — `ring-2 ring-ring/50` on every pressable tile, bar, row.
- **disabled** — a tile/bar/row with nothing to open (`onOpen` absent, or a count of 0) is a disabled button: no hover, no press.
- **empty** — `BarList` with no data: "Nothing in this range."; Partner spend: "No partner holds an order in this range."; Needs attention: "Nothing waiting on anybody."; Schedule sheet: "No deliveries planned."; "Attention required" with nothing in it is not rendered.
- **collapsed / open** — "Attention required" is collapsed by default (header + count); while collapsed a red band shine sweeps its outline (`OutlineShine`, still for reduced motion); open shows the tiles.
- **no change** — `TrendChip dir="same"` gray "Same"; portal `Change` shows "No change".

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press a UV `StatTile` (e.g. 3PL Live orders, Needs action) | Opens Delivery Orders narrowed to exactly those rows (line, date range, status facets) |
| Press a bar in "Orders by lane" | Opens Delivery Orders filtered to that status |
| Press a bar in "Orders by delivery date" | Opens Delivery Orders on that one day (one-day range) |
| Press an Order types segment/row | Opens the list with that Order Types facet (only where the type is a facet) |
| Press a Top customers row / Partner spend row | Opens the list filtered by that customer / vendor |
| Press a Needs attention row | Board loads that order's day, then the order's sheet opens |
| Hover any bar, segment or row | Tooltip: name · detail (e.g. "3 orders · $9,323") |
| Change the line segment or date range (3PL) | Every figure recomputes for that scope; drills carry it |
| Press "Attention required" header (Portal) | Toggles open/closed; shine stops while open |
| Press an `ActionTile` | Opens the 600px `ActionSheet` (what is needed, why, facts) → "View order details" → order sheet |
| Press an order number in `CreditHoldTile` | Opens that order's sheet |
| Press a portal headline figure | Popover with its explanation ("Compared with the N weeks before") |
| Press ⓘ beside a portal card title | Opens the card's explainer |
| Press the ↗ corner button on the Schedule card | Opens a `RecordSheet` "Next 7 days" with the schedule table; a row closes it and opens the order |
| Change "Last 4/8/12 weeks" on Spend | Spend cards rescope; the range label beside it updates |
| Tank health "Create order" | Creates a draft order for that ship-to and tank (product = last ordered), opens it in create mode |

## Outcomes
- A list opened already narrowed to the rows a figure counts (UV).
- An order sheet, an action sheet or the "Next 7 days" sheet opening (Portal).
- A new draft order from a tank card (Portal).
- A popover/tooltip explaining a figure.
- Nothing, when a tile or bar has no count (disabled).
- An empty message in a card when its range holds nothing; no "Attention required" banner when nothing needs action.

## Data contract
- `StatTile { label, value, note?, onOpen? }` — `onOpen` absent → not pressable.
- `BarDatum { key, label, value, detail?, onOpen? }`; `BarColumns { data, height? }`; `BarList { data, format?, share? }` (share keeps ≤ 8 parts).
- `Panel { title, aside?, className?, children }`.
- `ThreePLDashboard { onDrill(ThreePLDrill{ line, range, facets }), onOpenOrder(id) }`; Sales: `onDrill(SalesDrill)` (its day as a one-day range + column boxes).
- `MetricIcon { icon }` with `TERM_ICONS.deliveries | products | gallons | shipTos`.
- `TrendChip { dir: up|down|same, good? (defaults to up = good), size?: sm|md, children }`.
- Portal reads the order store (`useRequests`) and `dashboard.ts` weekly history; reports nothing back except opening sheets.

## Rules
1. **Everything on a UV dashboard is a way into a list** — every tile, bar and row opens its list narrowed to exactly what it counts — *so the dashboard never states a figure the reader cannot go and check* — `(source: DashboardParts.tsx header; docs/unified-view/2-layouts.md "Module dashboards", Pavan 2026-10-05)`.
2. **One dashboard vocabulary** — a module dashboard is built from `DashboardParts` (Panel · StatTile · BarColumns · BarList · DASHBOARD_GROUND) — *3PL and Sales must read as one product* — `(source: parity-shell G50)`.
3. **White cards on a gray ground, swapped in dark** — *after the reference dashboard; the cards must still sit above their ground in dark* — `(source: 2-layouts.md, 2026-10-05)`.
4. **Color by the job it does** — count = one series with the peak lit; share = palette in fixed order with a dot beside each name; ranking = one series — *the eye finds the busiest bar first, and color is never alone* — `(source: DashboardParts.tsx; 2-layouts.md)`.
5. **Dashboard is first in the rail, but the module opens on Orders** — `MODULE_LANDING` 3PL → Orders, Sales → Orders — `(source: parity-shell G52; UnifiedView.tsx)`. The Customer Portal opens on its Dashboard — `(source: memory project_customer_portal)`.
6. **One icon per term, one style** — `MetricIcon` + `TERM_ICONS` beside the figure, centred on the figure-and-change unit, not in the title row — *the word and its picture stay one thing in every module* — `(source: docs/ds/ui/2-rules.md §10k, Pavan 2026-10-20)`.
7. **One up/down marker: `TrendChip`** — no arrows in circles, no orange, no `destructive` red; down is always `--fp-trend-down`; `good` makes a wanted fall green — `(source: ui §10k; memory feedback_metric_icons)`.
8. **No trend lines on figure cards** — the term's `MetricIcon` sits there instead — `(source: ui §10k, 2026-10-20)`.
9. **No explainer lines on the portal dashboard** — a headline figure softens on hover and explains on click; a chart's explainer is an ⓘ beside its title — *Pavan, 2026-10-14* — `(source: customer-portal README)`.
10. **"Attention required" first, collapsed by default, only real kinds** — credit holds (one tile, a count, no balance), running late, no proof of delivery — *Anirudh review: what needs the customer comes first* — `(source: DashboardScreen.tsx; customer-portal README 2026-10-19)`.
11. **An action tile's title is the action/problem, led by the order number; a delivered order has no countdown** — `(source: DashboardScreen.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-15)`.
12. **Side-by-side cards are the same height; charts fill** — `(source: memory project_customer_portal "Alignment rule", 2026-10-14)`.
13. **Empty card space uses `QuietPattern` only** — never a new pattern per card — `(source: ui §11d)`.
14. **Credit only where the tenant gives it** — `(source: customer-portal README; DashboardScreen.tsx)`.

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Give every UV tile/bar/row an `onOpen` that drills into the list with the same scope.
- ✅ Use `TERM_ICONS` for deliveries, products, gallons, ship-tos — same icon in every module.
- ✅ Put the range control on the section it scopes (portal Spend).
- ❌ Don't color a count chart by category, or use hues for identity without a label/dot.
- ❌ Don't add delta chips where there is no prior-period data (UV dashboards have none).
- ❌ Don't draw a new violet, a new trend color, or a sparkline on a figure card.
- ❌ Don't turn "Attention required" into an `AlertBanner`.

## Checks
- `parity-shell` — G50 (built from DashboardParts), G52 (Dashboard first in the rail, module opens on Orders).
- `tokens` — no raw hex; `wcag-contrast` — trend, viz and status pairs.
- `fpds-cards`, `fpds-units` — this card's format.
- By eye: every pressable figure opens a correctly narrowed list; peak bar lit; side-by-side cards equal height; `MetricIcon` centred on figure + chip + "vs" line; shine runs only while collapsed.

## Reference
- 3PL › Dashboard (`ThreePLDashboard`) — stat row, Orders by lane / by delivery date, Order types (share), Top customers, Partner spend, Needs attention.
- Sales › Dashboard (`SalesDashboard`).
- Customer Portal › Dashboard (`DashboardScreen`) — Attention required, Schedule for the next 7 days (metric tiles), Tank monitor readings, Spend, Billing & credit.
- DS showcase › Metric Icons.

> **Figure tiles (Pavan, 2026-10-26):** NO metric icon — the title, figure and comparison as one block, and EVERY tile carries its own open arrow (outline, round) at the right that opens its own sheet or list. (An icon leading the tile was tried and removed the same day.)


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/data-table.md ===== -->

# Data table

`id: unit.data-table` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ListTable.tsx (ListTable, TwoLineCell, Hit, OverflowList), src/components/tables-layout/TableColumnHeader.tsx, useTableColumns.ts, useColumnOrder.ts, useColumnWidths.ts, GroupHeaderRow.tsx, src/index.css (th[aria-sort], .fp-sorted-cell), docs/ds/ui/column-widths.md` · `registry: fpds-data-table`

## Purpose
The list's table: every record a row, every column sortable, movable and resizable, the whole row opening the record. It answers "which records match, in what order — and let me open one."

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use for the table display of any list page (with `unit.view-bar` as its `topBar`).
- Use `alignTop` when rows grow tall with stacked child lines (Customer Portal › Orders: one line per tank).
- Use grouped rows (`nodes`) when the toolbar's Group by is on.
- Don't use for an editable table inside a sheet or form — that is the boxed table unit (ui §10f, `TableCell*`).
- Don't use for a list with no list chrome — a table page always comes with the whole unit (ui §10c); prefer `unit.standard-list`.

## Anatomy
1. **Frame** — rounded, bordered box; holds the view bar (`topBar`) above a scroll area.
2. **Header row** (sticky) — optional **select-all checkbox** cell (44px), then one `TableColumnHeader` per visible column: label as a sort button + sort icon; hover shows the move grip (left padding) and the resize edge (`┆│┆`, right boundary).
3. **Column search row** (second header row, only while open) — one "Filter…" box per column.
4. **Body rows** — optional **row checkbox** cell, then cells from `renderCell`. Cell vocabulary in this file: `TwoLineCell` (title over a muted line), `OverflowList` (first value + "+N" chip → BreakdownCard), `Hit` (matched text marked).
5. **Stacked child lines** (Customer Portal › Orders) — per-tank columns print one 24px line per tank inside the row; the order's own cells print once, top-aligned.
6. **Group header rows** (`GroupHeaderRow`) — chevron, dimension label, group label, count bubble, summed measure; nested one level.
7. **Nested layer** (`renderExpanded`, optional) — chevron leading the first cell opens a full-width white row with a 2px green rail. No list passes it today.
8. **Skeleton rows** while `loading`.

## Look
- Frame: `rounded-md border border-border`; table `table-layout: fixed`, width = sum of column widths (+44 with selection).
- Header: 40px (`h-10`), `sticky top-0`, `bg-fp-table-header`, `border-b border-fp-outline-1`, `px-3 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground`.
- Sort icon `size-3.5`: unsorted `ChevronsUpDown` at 50% opacity; sorted `ArrowUp` / `ArrowDown` full.
- **Sorted column in grays**: its `th[aria-sort]` on `--chip` with the label `--foreground` and semibold (600); its cells `.fp-sorted-cell` = `--fp-row-hover` at 55%. No brand, no underline.
- Column search row: 44px (`h-11`), `bg-fp-surface-1b` (quieter than the header), `px-2`; boxes `h-7 rounded-md px-2.5 text-xs shadow-none`; placeholder "Filter…" only when the column is ≥ 96px.
- Body rows: 56px (`h-14`), `px-3` cells; hover/focus `bg-fp-row-hover`; selected `bg-fp-row-selected` (via `data-state="selected"`); `alignTop` → `align-top py-3`.
- Checkbox column: fixed 44px, never moves, hides, resizes or sorts.
- Hit mark: `rounded-xs bg-brand/25 font-medium text-foreground`.
- TwoLineCell: title `text-sm text-foreground`, line two `text-xs text-muted-foreground`, both truncate.
- Group header: `bg-fp-lane`, 40px, label `text-sm font-medium`, dim label `text-xs text-muted-foreground`, count `rounded-full bg-muted px-1.5 text-[11px]`.
- Nested layer: `bg-fp-surface-1b`, rail `before:w-0.5 bg-status-success/70`; open parent keeps `bg-fp-row-hover`, no bottom border.
- Skeletons: DS `Skeleton` (shimmer), widths varied per cell (45–90%).
- **Everything left-aligned** — every header and cell, numbers and money included.

## States
- **default** — rows sorted as given, no sort active.
- **hover** — row tint; on the hovered header only: move grip + its resize edge.
- **focus** — a row is focusable (`tabIndex 0`), ring inset `ring-ring/50` + tint.
- **sorted** — header greyed with arrow; column cells light gray.
- **selected** — row tint; header checkbox `indeterminate` when some, checked when all on the page.
- **filtering** — column search row open; boxes with text narrow rows.
- **grouped** — group headers, collapsible; pagination replaced by the grouped footer.
- **expanded** — nested layer open under its parent.
- **loading** — skeleton rows (default 10), never a spinner.
- **empty** — no rows: with the column search row open the table stays (header + boxes, no rows); otherwise the page shows its empty state (see `unit.standard-list`).

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Click a header label | Sort asc → desc → unsorted (cycle); page → 1. |
| Drag a header body | Reorders columns (drop indicator); checkbox column is fixed. |
| ⌘/Ctrl + ← / → on a focused header | Moves the column one step. |
| Drag a header's right edge | Resizes the column (floor 64px). |
| Double-click the edge | Restores the declared width. |
| ← / → on a focused edge | Nudges the width by 16px. |
| Type in a column's "Filter…" box | Narrows to rows whose printed value in that column contains the text (AND across boxes); page → 1; match marked. |
| Click anywhere on a row | Opens the record (its side sheet). Clicks on a button, link, input, label, checkbox or `[data-no-row-open]` don't; nor does a text selection. |
| Enter / Space on a focused row | Opens the record. |
| Tick a row checkbox | Adds/removes it from the selection (bulk bar appears on screens that have one). |
| Tick the header checkbox | Selects / clears all rows on this page. |
| Click a "+N" chip | Opens the BreakdownCard listing all values. |
| Click a group header | Collapses / expands that group. |
| Click a row's expand chevron (nested layer) | Opens / closes its layer. |
| Frame resizes | Columns re-fill the width (see Rules 2). |

## Outcomes
- A re-ordered list (sorted), a narrowed list (column boxes), page 1.
- A record's sheet opening.
- A rearranged / resized table — saved into the active view (it becomes dirty) or kept for the session.
- A selection for bulk actions.
- Grouped sections with counts and totals; collapsed groups.
- Zero rows with the boxes still showing, so the reader can change them.
- Nothing when a click lands on an inner control or ends a text selection.

## Data contract
- Required: `rows`, `columns` (`{key, label, width, sortable?}`), `sort` + `onSort(key)`, `rowId`, `renderCell(row, key)`, `openLabel(row)` ("Open order 4592866"), `actionsKey` (the legacy chevron column key — it is dropped).
- Opening: `onOpenRow?(row)` — omit for rows that open nothing.
- Arrangement: `hidden?`, `columnOrder?` + `onReorderColumns?`, `columnWidths?` + `onResizeColumns?` (supply to keep them in a saved view; omit and the table remembers for the session).
- Selection (opt-in): `selectedIds`, `onToggleRow(id)`, `onToggleAll(next)`.
- Column search: `showColumnFilters`, `columnFilters`, `onColumnFilterChange(key, value)`.
- Grouping: `nodes?` (from `groupRows`), `collapsedGroups`, `onToggleGroupCollapse`, `groupNoun`, `groupUnit`.
- Other: `topBar?`, `alignTop?`, `loading?`, `skeletonRows?`, `renderExpanded?` + `expandLabel?`.
- `width` per column = the widest rendered cell (see Rules 1); the `align` field exists on `ListColumn` but is not rendered — leave it off.

## Rules
1. **A column starts at its widest RENDERED cell** — text, "+N" chips, pills, day grids — plus padding, never narrower than header + sort arrow, rounded up to 10px. Customer starts at 250, Ship-to / Ship-Tos 170; rule tables give Schedule and the column before it +20. — `(source: ui §10p; docs/ds/ui/column-widths.md; memory feedback_column_widths)`
2. **No empty space on the right, ever.** Spare width goes to columns in proportion to how far each is wider than 120px (icons, counts, codes stay put; names, addresses, contacts grow); rounding leftovers go to the widest; wider than the frame → scroll. No filler column. — `(source: ListTable.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-22; ui §10p)`
3. **Everything is left-aligned** — headers and cells, numbers and money too, in every table. Only row action buttons sit at the end. — *right-aligned Qty sat misaligned with its header.* — `(source: ui §10p, Pavan 2026-10-22)`
4. **The sorted column is shown in grays with an arrow** (`ArrowUp`/`ArrowDown`); unsorted columns keep a faint `ChevronsUpDown`. No brand color. — `(source: ui §10p; index.css, Pavan 2026-10-21)`
5. **Every column sorts, asc → desc → unsorted.** — `(source: docs/unified-view/3-interactions.md §7; parity G5)`
6. **Every column moves and resizes — no enable flag.** The divider resizes (`col-resize`), the header body moves (`cursor-grab`); marks show only on the hovered header. — `(source: docs/ds/ux/2-rules.md "Every table's columns move and resize"; parity G4)`
7. **The whole row opens the record; no chevron column.** — *clicking the row is what everybody tries first.* — `(source: ux 2-rules, Pavan 2026-10-19)`
8. **Search by column is a row of boxes under the header**: AND-ed, cleared on close, stays when nothing matches, table only, kept by saved views. — `(source: ui §10g)`
9. **Selection is opt-in** — no handlers, no 44px checkbox column. — *a column that leads nowhere costs width.* — `(source: ListTable.tsx)`
10. **Rows that grow tall read from the top** (`alignTop`): stacked child lines, one per tank, line up across columns; no nested layer. — `(source: customer-portal/OrdersScreen.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-21)`
11. **Loading is skeleton rows, never a spinner.** — *a list that collapses loses its height and the page jumps.* — `(source: interactions 2-rules §4c; ui §11c)`
12. **Status and Type columns are pills; "+N" opens the BreakdownCard on click.** — `(source: ui §11b; memory feedback_uv_source_of_truth)`
13. **A missing value prints `–` (applies, not filled) or `N/A` (does not apply)** — never a blank cell. — `(source: interactions 2-rules §4c)`
14. **Flat headers — no two-tier column groups** unless Pavan asks. — `(source: ui §10e)`
15. **Full content width, never capped.** — `(source: docs/ds/ux/2-rules.md "Tables — full width…"; parity-tables G43)`
16. **Every header declares its alignment** (`text-left` unless Pavan names a screen) — a bare `<th>` centres — `(source: misses M-005; eval th-align)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Measure real rendered cells when adding a column; set `width` from that.
- ✅ Pass `columnOrder`/`columnWidths` handlers when the list has saved views.
- ✅ Use `TwoLineCell` for a record number over its date; `OverflowList` for many values.
- ❌ `align: "right"` or centred numbers.
- ❌ A trailing chevron column or a "View" button to open rows.
- ❌ A filler column to soak up width; a `max-w-*` on the table's container.
- ❌ Brand color or underline on the sorted header.
- ❌ A spinner while loading; an empty message instead of the column search row.

## Checks
- `parity-lists` G2, G4, G5; `parity-tables` G39 (booleans are checkboxes), G43 (no width cap).
- `table-rows` (for editable variants), `tokens` (no raw colors).
- `fpds-units` — header, column search row, body anatomy.
- By eye: no gap on the right at any window width; all columns left-aligned; sorted column gray with arrow; a click anywhere on a row opens it.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders (reference table; grouped), 3PL › Directory (loading skeletons, selection).
- Sales › Orders (selection, grouped), Sales › Customers, Sales › Assets.
- Customer Portal › Orders (stacked tank lines, `alignTop`, grouped).
- Service Work › Parts, Price Book, Technicians (via `unit.standard-list`).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/date-picker.md ===== -->

# Date picker

`id: unit.date-picker` · `layer: unit` · `code: src/components/ui/date-picker.tsx (DatePickerPanel, DateRangePopover, DateField, DateKeyField, DatePopover, DatePanel, dateWindowOf, formatDateRange), prototypes/unified-view/threepl/DateRangeFilter.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/DateFilter.tsx` · `registry: fpds-date-picker`

## Purpose
The one date picker for every screen that picks a date — a list's date filter and a form's date field alike: named presets, a calendar, typed dates, a live count, and nothing changes until Apply. It answers "which day or window do I mean, and how many records fall in it?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use the **range** form (default) for a list's date window: toolbar stepper `DateRangeFilter` (3PL › Delivery Orders, Sales › Orders, Service Work › Service Orders, Customer Portal › Orders) or `DateRangePopover` behind your own trigger.
- Use the **single** form (`select="single"`) for a field that holds one date: `DateField` (a `Date`), `DateKeyField` (a `YYYY-MM-DD` key, optional `min`), or `DatePopover` + `DatePanel` as a drop-in for a Popover + single Calendar in an existing form.
- Use `DateFilter` (‹ one day ›) for a list scoped to one day (the toolbar's `dateScope`).
- Don't use a bare `Calendar` in a popover, or any other picker.
- Don't use in the technician phone app — it keeps its full-width calendar (no room for a rail).

## Anatomy
**Panel (`DatePickerPanel`)**
1. **Preset rail** (left) — range: *All dates* (only when the list passes `all`), Today, Yesterday, This week, Last week, Last 7 days, Last 30 days, Next 7 days, Month to date, Last month, This quarter, **Custom range**. Single: Today, Tomorrow, Yesterday, In 7 days.
2. **Calendar** — two months side by side (range) or one month (single).
3. **Footer** — typed **Start date → End date** (MM/DD/YYYY; one "Date" field in single) · live **"N {noun} match"** · **Reset** · **Apply**.

**Triggers**
- `DateRangeFilter`: ‹ previous · **label** (a date, a span, or "All dates") · next ›.
- `DateFilter`: ‹ previous day · **full date** · next day ›.
- `DateField` / `DateKeyField`: an outline, full-width field button with `CalendarIcon` and the date, or "Select date".

## Look
- Popover: `w-auto p-0`, never wider than the viewport minus 2rem.
- Rail: `w-44` (176px), `border-r border-border`, `p-2 gap-0.5`; exactly as tall as the calendar — it scrolls inside that height. Preset rows `h-7 rounded-md px-2.5 text-sm text-left`, hover `bg-fp-row-hover`; the preset the draft is on: `bg-selected text-selected-foreground font-medium`.
- Calendar: DS `Calendar`, `px-3 py-1`; two months with a wide gap (`gap-12`); range ends filled, days between tinted (DS range styles).
- Footer: `border-t border-border px-4 py-2.5 gap-3`; inputs `h-8 w-32 tabular-nums`, placeholder "MM/DD/YYYY", "→" muted between; count `ml-auto text-sm text-muted-foreground tabular-nums`; Reset `variant="ghost" size="sm"`; Apply `size="sm"` primary.
- Stepper: arrows `variant="outline" size="icon-sm"` (32px); label `variant="outline" size="sm"`; `gap-1`. In the Export sheet the label takes the slack (`fullWidth`), arrows stay 32px.
- Tokens only — the reference image's indigo is not used.

## States
- **default** — draft = the current value; its preset (if any) selected, else Custom range.
- **picking** — after the first click in range mode, that day is a one-day range waiting for its other end.
- **hover / focus** — preset hover tint; `focus-visible:ring-2 ring-ring/50`.
- **selected** — the preset matching the draft; range ends filled.
- **disabled** — days ruled out by `disabled` / `min` can't be picked, presets that fall on them are not listed, typed dates on them are rejected. Reset disabled while the draft equals the reset target. Apply disabled with no draft (range). Stepper arrows disabled while on "All dates".
- **empty** — single field with no value shows "Select date" in muted; no count when the screen passes no `count`.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press the trigger (label / field) | Opens the panel; the draft starts at the current value. |
| Press a preset | Draft = that window; the calendar jumps to it; count updates. |
| Press **Custom range** | Puts the caret in the typed start field. |
| Click a day (range) | First click: a one-day draft, waiting. Second click: the other end (earlier day becomes the start). |
| Click a day, then Apply | Applies that one day (a range of one). |
| Click a day (single) | Draft = that day. |
| Type a date + Enter or leave the field | Valid & allowed → draft updates (ends swapped if reversed); invalid or ruled out → the field reverts. |
| Press **Reset** | Draft back to the reset target (3PL's stepper: today; a form field: the value it opened with). |
| Press **Apply** | Commits the draft to the screen and closes the popover. |
| Esc / outside click | Closes; nothing changes. |
| ‹ / › on the stepper | Moves the window by its own length (a week steps a week; a day steps a day). |
| Month arrows in the calendar | Browse months; draft unchanged. |

## Outcomes
- A new window on the list (rows, view statement, export count follow) or a new date in a form field.
- "All dates" — the span of the list's own records — with the stepper arrows disabled.
- A one-day range printed as one date ("Oct 22, 2026"); a span as "Oct 20, 2026 – Oct 26, 2026".
- Nothing, when closed without Apply or when a typed date is invalid.
- "0 orders match" shown before applying, so the reader can widen the window first.

## Data contract
- `DatePickerPanel`: `value` (`{from, to}` or null), `onApply(next)`, `select?` ("range" | "single"), `all?` (span for "All dates"), `resetTo?`, `count?(range) => number`, `noun?` (plural, default "records"), `disabled?` (react-day-picker matcher).
- `DateRangePopover`: panel props + `value`, `onChange(range)`, `children` (the trigger), `align?` (default "end").
- `DateField`: `value: Date | null`, `onChange(date | null)`, `placeholder?` ("Select date"), `disabled?`, `dayDisabled?`, `align?`, `children?` (custom trigger). `DateKeyField`: `value: "YYYY-MM-DD"`, `onChange(key)`, `min?`.
- `DatePopover` + `DatePanel`: `selected`, `onSelect(date)` (runs on Apply), `disabled?`.
- `dateWindowOf(keys)` → `{all, count}` from a list's `YYYY-MM-DD` record days — feed both to the range picker.
- `DateRangeFilter`: `value`, `onChange`, `fullWidth?`, `all?`, `count?`, `noun?`. `DateFilter`: `value`, `onChange`.

## Rules
1. **One date picker, used by every screen that picks a date** (filters and form fields, every module). — *several pickers (DateRangeFilter, DateFilter, DateButton, Calendar in ~25 files) taught several interactions.* — `(source: ui §10p "One date picker", Pavan 2026-10-21; memory project_feedback_round_2026_10_21 point 4)`
2. **Nothing changes until Apply** — presets, calendar and typed dates all edit a draft. — `(source: date-picker.tsx; ui §10p)`
3. **The preset rail is fixed and in this order**; "All dates" only when the list passes `all`, meaning the span of its records. — `(source: ui §10p; memory execution notes 2026-10-21)`
4. **A range picker picks one day too** — one click + Apply. — `(source: date-picker.tsx; ui §10p)`
5. **Typed dates are MM/DD/YYYY.** — *what a US dispatcher writes on a form.* — `(source: date-picker.tsx)`
6. **Live "N {noun} match" in the footer** when the list can count. — *read the result before committing.* — `(source: ui §10p)`
7. **The rail is as tall as the calendar, no taller; more air between the two months.** — `(source: date-picker.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-22)`
8. **Single-date fields use the same panel in one-date mode** (day presets, one month, one typed date). — `(source: memory project_feedback_round_2026_10_21 execution notes; ui §10p)`
9. **One day reads as one day** — never "Sep 7 – Sep 7". — `(source: DateRangeFilter.tsx; formatDateRange)`
10. **The list's date sits in the toolbar, not in the Filters panel.** — *every row on screen is scoped by it; its value must stay legible.* — `(source: DateFilter.tsx)`
11. **3PL-style lists scope by a range, stepped by its own length.** — *a broker's delivery windows run over weekends; a one-day view keeps looking empty.* — `(source: DateRangeFilter.tsx)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Feed `dateWindowOf(keys)` → `all` + `count` on every dated list.
- ✅ Swap a form's Popover + Calendar for `DatePopover` + `DatePanel` (same stored value).
- ✅ Pass `min` / `dayDisabled` to rule out impossible days.
- ❌ Apply on preset press or on day click.
- ❌ Lay the calendar out flat in a sheet (the Export sheet uses the stepper).
- ❌ Brand/indigo fills from the reference image; raw colors.

## Checks
- `tokens` — no raw colors in the panel or triggers.
- `fpds-units` — rail · calendar · footer present; single mode shows day presets and one month.
- `microcopy` — preset labels exactly as listed; "Custom range", "MM/DD/YYYY", "N … match", "Reset", "Apply", "Select date".
- By eye: nothing moves until Apply; rail height equals calendar height; a one-day window prints as one date.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders (toolbar range stepper and Export sheet Date), Sales › Orders, Service Work › Service Orders, Customer Portal › Orders (range, with "All dates" and live count).
- Sales dashboard (one-day `DateFilter`).
- Form fields: the order sheets in 3PL › Delivery Orders, Sales › Orders and Quotes, Service Work › Service Orders, Customer Portal › Orders (`DatePanel`); the recurring rule frequency picker shared by 3PL › Recurring Orders and Customer Portal › Recurring Orders (`DateKeyField`); Customer Portal › Orders sheet's "Paid on" field (`DateKeyField`).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/delivery-timeline.md ===== -->

# Delivery window & preferred time

`id: unit.delivery-timeline` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/DeliveryWindowTimeline.tsx, src/components/form-layout/TimeWindowSlider.tsx (formatWindow, formatSpan, formatMinutes — and the retired slider), prototypes/unified-view/HangingAdd.tsx` · `registry: fpds-delivery-timeline`

## Purpose
One day drawn once, full width: the delivery window as a range on a 24-hour track, and the customer's preferred times as bars on the same scale beneath it. It answers "between when can we deliver, and when would they rather we came".

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use for every delivery or scheduling window in every module — order sheets, recurring rules, a ship-to's default hours, a service order's scheduling window.
- Don't use `TimeWindowSlider` for a new window — it remains only in the technician mobile app (`TechnicianHome`); its formatters are still shared.
- Don't use a dropdown of fixed windows — a window is two times, not a category.
- Don't use where a module has no window (Sales orders).
- Don't use for a single moment (a date) — use the DS date field in the record sheet's field row.

## Anatomy
1. **Label, outside the box** — `SheetField` label (each module words it: "Delivery Window & Preferred Time", "Scheduling window & preferred time", "Delivery hours & preferred time"; default "Delivery window & preferred time"), required mark when required.
2. **Outlined box** (input-style):
   - **Read-out line** — the window ("8:00 AM – 12:00 PM", `formatWindow`) left; the span ("4 hr window" / "All day", `formatSpan`) right.
   - **Track** — DS `Slider`, two thumbs, midnight to midnight, 30-minute steps, minimum one hour apart.
   - **Hour ticks** — 12a · 3a · 6a · 9a · 12p · 3p · 6p · 9p · 12a; end labels pinned to the edges.
   - **Preferred-time bars** — one per preferred time, under the track, each with dashed lines up to the track at its two ends, a resize grip at each end, and a ✕ just past its right end (on hover/focus).
   - **Time labels** — short times under each bar's ends ("8a", "1:30p"), dotted-underlined, pressable; a narrow bar shows one label "8a–9:20a"; where two bars meet the shared time prints once.
3. **Hanging add** — `HangingAdd` "Add preferred time" off the box's squared bottom-left corner.
4. **No-room alert** — beside the add: warning icon + "Delete or adjust the current preferred time to add a new one."
5. **Times editor** (popover from a time label) — Start and End time inputs to the minute, and the problem line when the pair doesn't fit.

## Look
- Box: `rounded-md border border-input bg-fp-surface-1b px-4 pt-3 pb-3`, dark `bg-fp-field-fill`; focus-within `border-ring` + `ring-2 ring-ring/50`; bottom-left corner squared (`rounded-bl-none`) while the add shows.
- Read-out: window `text-sm tabular-nums text-foreground`, never truncates; span `text-xs text-muted-foreground`, truncates first.
- Track: thumbs `size-5`, track `h-2.5`. Scale area `h-10`, `h-[78px]` once a bar exists.
- Ticks and time labels: `text-xs tabular-nums`; ticks `text-muted-foreground`, bar times `text-foreground`.
- Bars: `h-2.5 rounded-xs bg-fp-lane-blue/30`, hover `bg-fp-lane-blue/45`, 1px air each side so two touching bars stay two; dashed lines `border-dashed border-muted-foreground/60`.
- Bar ✕: `size-5`, `text-muted-foreground` → `text-foreground`.
- No-room line and editor problem text: `text-xs text-status-failed`.
- Times editor inputs: `w-[130px]` each (natural-size time control).

## States
- **default** — window range on the track, no bars, scale short (`h-10`).
- **with preferred times** — scale grows; bars, dashed guides and time labels show.
- **hover bar** — bar darkens, ✕ appears, cursor grab (grabbing while dragging); ends show resize cursor.
- **focus bar** — focus ring, ✕ visible; keyboard moves it.
- **narrow bar** — one combined label, no end label.
- **full** — + pressed with no free half hour: the alert line shows; clears on any change to window or times.
- **add hidden** — when disabled, or the window is shorter than 30 minutes.
- **disabled** — slider and bars inert, time labels plain text, no ✕, no add (locked records; 3PL rule tab until a ship-to is chosen).
- **editor error** — "Enter both times" · "End after the start" · "Inside the delivery window, …" · "Overlaps another preferred time".

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Drag a window thumb | Window start/end moves in 30-min steps; read-out and span update; callers clamp existing preferred times into the new window and drop any under 30 min |
| Press "Add preferred time" | New bar in the window's first free stretch of ≥30 min, up to 2 h long; it takes focus |
| Press + with no free 30 min | Alert line: "Delete or adjust the current preferred time to add a new one." |
| Drag a bar | Moves in 30-min steps; stops at neighbours and the window edges |
| Drag a bar's end | Resizes; never shorter than 30 min, never past a neighbour or the window |
| ← / → on a focused bar | Moves it half an hour |
| Shift + ← / → | Moves its end half an hour |
| Delete / Backspace on a focused bar | Removes it |
| Press a bar's ✕ | Removes it |
| Press a time label | Popover with Start and End to the minute, the pressed one focused |
| Type a pair that fits | Kept at once; bar moves/resizes to match |
| Type a pair that doesn't fit | Problem line shows; value not kept |

## Outcomes
- A window set (start, end in minutes from midnight).
- Preferred times added, moved, resized, typed to the minute, or removed — never overlapping, always inside the window.
- The no-room message, and nothing added, when the window is full.
- Nothing changes while disabled.
- Autosaved with the record; a recurring rule's orders inherit its window and preferred times.

## Data contract
- In: `start`, `end` (minutes from midnight), `preferred` (list of `{start, end}`), `label`, `required`, `disabled`.
- Out: `onWindowChange(start, end)`; `onPreferredChange(nextList)`.
- The caller re-clamps preferred times when the window moves (all current callers do).
- Shared formatters: `formatWindow`, `formatSpan`, `formatMinutes` from `TimeWindowSlider.tsx`.

## Rules
1. **Every window field in every module is this timeline** — *Pavan: "apply wherever applicable, all modules and service work"* — `(source: ui §10m; memory feedback_delivery_timeline 2026-10-20)`
2. **Label outside, the unit inside an input-style outlined box** — *every field's label sits outside; the timeline is a field* — `(source: ui §10m; DeliveryWindowTimeline.tsx comment)`
3. **The add is the table's hanging + off the box corner** — *same affordance as adding a table row* — `(source: ui §10m; ui §10f)`
4. **Preferred times never overlap; a bar stops at its neighbours whichever way it is dragged or typed** — *Pavan, 2026-10-20* — `(source: ui §10m; memory project_customer_portal 2026-10-20)`
5. **With no free half hour, + says "Delete or adjust the current preferred time to add a new one." where it was pressed** — *the reader learns why nothing was added, at the press* — `(source: ui §10m; interactions §7b a dead control says why on the press)`
6. **A window is two times on a drawn day, in 30-minute steps** — *six fixed options could not say "7 to 11" or "all day"; delivery isn't scheduled to the minute* — `(source: TimeWindowSlider.tsx comment)`
7. **Pressing a bar's time opens start and end to the minute — the same value as the bars** — `(source: ui §10m)`
8. **The window read-out never truncates; the span note gives way** — *sheets fit their content, so a half-width field is narrow* — `(source: TimeWindowSlider.tsx 2026-10-15)`
9. **A new bar takes focus** — *so ← → move it at once* — `(source: DeliveryWindowTimeline.tsx; ui §10f)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Full width under the date row (portal: Ship-to · PO # · Planned date on one row, timeline below).
- ✅ Word the label for the module, keep the unit identical.
- ✅ Clamp preferred times into a moved window.
- ❌ A new `TimeWindowSlider`, a window dropdown, or separate start/end fields.
- ❌ A separate "Preferred windows" table.
- ❌ Helper text under the box.
- ❌ Overlapping bars, or a silent no-op on a full window.

## Checks
- `fields-fill` — the row it sits in fills its width.
- `tokens`, `wcag-contrast` — bar and box colors are tokens with WCAG pairs.
- `parity-sheets` — the same field on create and edit sheets.
- By eye: label outside, + joins the box corner, bars don't overlap, short bars carry one label, the window read-out is never cut.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders › Delivery Order sheet — Schedule Details, "Delivery Window & Preferred Time".
- 3PL › Recurring Orders › Create rule — each ship-to tab.
- Service Work › Service Orders › service order sheet — "Scheduling window & preferred time".
- Customer Portal › Orders › order sheet (Delivery section); Customer Portal › Recurring Orders › rule sheet (Frequency section); Customer Portal › Sites & Assets › ship-to sheet — "Delivery hours & preferred time".


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/documents-table.md ===== -->

# Documents table

`id: unit.documents-table` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/DocumentsTable.tsx, src/components/ui/file-type-icon.tsx` · `registry: fpds-documents-table`

## Purpose
The one table for files kept on a record — BOLs, tickets, invoices, dispute evidence. It answers "what files does this record have, when did they arrive, who added them?" and, where allowed, lets the reader add one.

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when any record keeps files (order, invoice dispute, order request).
- Don't use a gallery, a card grid or an empty-state panel for documents.
- Don't use it for lines or other editable rows — use `unit.lines-table` / the boxed table.

## Anatomy
1. **Section** — `SheetSection title="Documents" flush`; where the reader can add, a `HangingAdd` "Add document" off the bottom-left corner and a hidden multi-file input.
2. **Header row** — Document · Added · By · (blank download column).
3. **Rows** — Document cell: `FileTypeIcon` (format tag) then the name · Added (formatted date) · By · download icon button.
4. **Empty row** (always one row) — with upload: "Upload a document" (upload icon) · — · —; without upload: one cell with the caller's words.

## Look
- `table w-full table-fixed text-sm`; columns: Document takes the rest · Added 18% · By 20% · download `w-12`.
- Header: `border-b border-border bg-fp-table-header`, cells `px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground`.
- Rows `h-12`, `border-b border-border`, last without border; cells `px-3`. Name `text-foreground` truncated with full name as title; Added / By `text-muted-foreground`.
- Icon + name gap 2.5. Download: ghost `icon-sm` button, `text-muted-foreground`.
- Upload row: `cursor-pointer`, hover `bg-fp-row-hover`; label `text-muted-foreground` → `text-foreground` on hover; focus ring `ring-ring/50`.
- `FileTypeIcon`: document outline with a format tag (PDF, PNG, SVG, JPG, DOC, XLS, CSV…); tag color by family — PDF red · images violet · documents blue · sheets green · archives orange · other ink; the text carries the format so color is never the only cue.
- All left-aligned.

## States
- **empty, can upload** — one "Upload a document" row; the whole row is pressable.
- **empty, read-only** — one row stating what will arrive (3PL: "Nothing yet. A BOL arrives when the order is assigned; the ticket and the invoice follow the delivery.") or the default "No documents yet."
- **filled** — one row per file.
- **hover (upload row)** — row hover fill, label darkens.
- **focus** — ring on the upload button and download buttons.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press anywhere on the "Upload a document" row | Opens the file picker |
| Press "Add document" (hanging add) | Opens the file picker |
| Pick one file | Row added; toast "<name> added" (Customer Portal Invoices) / "Document added · <name>" (Customer Portal Orders) |
| Pick several files | One row each; toast "N documents added" |
| Cancel the picker / pick nothing | Nothing changes |
| Press the download icon | `onDownload(row)`; Customer Portal toast "<name> downloaded" |
| Hover a long name | Full name in the native title |

## Outcomes
- Files added to the record (and its activity, where the caller logs it).
- A file downloaded.
- Nothing, when the picker is canceled.
- The section never changes shape when the first file lands.

## Data contract
- `rows: { id, kind, name, added (already formatted), by }[]` — `kind` is carried but not shown.
- `onAdd?()` — given, the empty row is the upload slot.
- `emptyText?` — the empty row's words when there is no upload (default "No documents yet.").
- `onDownload?(row)`.

## Rules
1. **Always a table, never empty of rows** — empty with upload = the upload row; read-only = says what will arrive — *the section must not change shape when the first file lands; a table with columns says what a document is made of* — `(source: docs/ds/ux/2-rules.md "Tables — one row is always there", Pavan 2026-10-14)`.
2. **Every file name has its format icon on the left** — *the format is read at a glance; text in the tag means color is never the only cue* — `(source: ux/2-rules "Documents — the format icon beside every file", Pavan 2026-10-14)`.
3. **No Kind column** — *the format icon says what it is* — `(source: DocumentsTable.tsx; ux/2-rules, Pavan 2026-10-14)`.
4. **One shared documents table everywhere; the 3PL anatomy is the source of truth** — `(source: DocumentsTable.tsx; feedback_table_conventions 2026-10-14; feedback_uv_source_of_truth)`.
5. **Everything left-aligned; only the row action sits at the end** — `(source: ui/2-rules §10p; feedback_column_widths)`.
6. **A sheet holding it passes `fit`** — `(source: ui/7-variants §1; eval sheets)`.
7. **A toast is one line** — `(source: feedback_toasts; unified-view/3-interactions §2)`.

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Put it in a flush `SheetSection` titled "Documents".
- ✅ Give the empty row an upload where the reader may add; otherwise say what will arrive.
- ✅ Accept several files in one pick.
- ❌ A "No documents" panel or dashed box in place of the table.
- ❌ A Kind / Type column.
- ❌ A file name without its `FileTypeIcon`.

## Checks
- `sheets` — a sheet with `DocumentsTable` must pass `fit`.
- `boxed-tables`, `tokens`, `wcag-contrast`.
- By eye: one row is always present; icons left of every name; columns left-aligned.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders — order sheet, Documents section, read-only (`threepl/BrokerOrderSheet.tsx`).
- Customer Portal › Orders — order sheet, Documents with upload, open in every status (`customer-portal/OrderRequestSheet.tsx`).
- Customer Portal › Invoices — dispute Documents (`customer-portal/InvoiceSheet.tsx`).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/export-sheet.md ===== -->

# Export sheet

`id: unit.export-sheet` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ExportSheet.tsx (ExportSheet, ValueChip, ExportFilterRow), prototypes/unified-view/RecordSheet.tsx (size "compact")` · `registry: fpds-export-sheet`

## Purpose
The side sheet that opens from a list's Export button and shows exactly what is about to leave — the same rows you are looking at, the narrowings that made them, how many — before you pick a format and export. It answers "what am I exporting, and is that what I mean?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use for every list's Export (the `Upload` icon at the right end of `unit.view-row`).
- Don't export silently from the toolbar — the sheet is the only path.
- Don't use for importing, for printing a single record, or for downloading a document from a row (Customer Portal › Invoices' Download column is its own cell).
- Don't use as a place to build a different filter — it edits the list's own state.

## Anatomy
1. **Header** — title "Export {Noun}" (e.g. "Export Orders"), the sheet's close ✕. No step column.
2. **Date** section (only when the list is date-scoped) — the screen's own date control, full width (3PL: the ‹ range › stepper).
3. **Filters** section —
   - the screen's own **Filters** trigger, full width (opens `unit.filter-panel`);
   - under it, one **row per narrowing that is on**: label (140px column) · value chips (each with ✕) · **Clear** (only when the row holds several values). Rows are separated by a full-width rule. Narrowings include the search text and the column search, not only facets.
4. **Footer** — left: the **count** ("62 orders"); right: **format** select (.csv · .pdf · .doc) 12px off the **Export** button. No Cancel.

## Look
- Sheet: `RecordSheet` `size="compact"` (510px — controls, not a form), `stepNav={false}`, `mode="create"`.
- Sections: `SheetSection variant="plain"` (heading + content, no card box); control and its list 12px apart (`gap-3`).
- Narrowing rows: `flex items-start gap-3`; label `w-[140px] text-xs text-muted-foreground pt-0.5`; chips `flex-wrap gap-1.5`; separator `border-b border-border pb-3` (not after the last row).
- Value chip = removable pill: `h-7 rounded-full bg-muted pl-3 pr-1 text-xs`, ✕ 20px round `hover:bg-foreground/10`.
- Clear: `variant="ghost" size="xs"`, `h-5 px-1 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground`.
- Footer count: number `font-medium tabular-nums text-foreground`, noun `text-muted-foreground`, `text-sm`.
- Format select: `size="sm"`, `w-[92px]`, `aria-label` "File format". Export: primary button.

## States
- **default** — format .csv; Export enabled; Filters section shows only the trigger when nothing is on.
- **with narrowings** — one row per narrowing, chips removable.
- **open (nested)** — the Filters popover or date picker open over the sheet.
- **disabled** — Export disabled when the count is 0 (Enter does nothing either).
- **empty** — no narrowings: no rows under the Filters trigger; the count is the whole list.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press **Export** on the view row | Opens the sheet with the list's current narrowing. |
| Step / pick the date | Changes the LIST's date window (visible behind the sheet); count updates. |
| Press **Filters** → tick → **Apply Filters** | Changes the list's filters; rows and count update. |
| Press ✕ on a value chip | Removes that one value from the list's narrowing; count updates. |
| Press **Clear** on a row | Removes every value in that row. |
| Change the format | Sets .csv / .pdf / .doc for this export. |
| Press **Export** (or Enter) with count > 0 | Closes the sheet; toast "Exporting 62 orders as .csv". |
| Press ✕ / Esc / outside | Closes; any narrowing changed in the sheet stays on the list (they were the list's own). |

## Outcomes
- A one-line success toast naming count, noun and format ("Exporting 1 order as .pdf").
- A list narrowed or widened from inside the sheet — the same change is visible behind it and after it closes.
- Nothing exported when the count is 0 (button disabled).
- Close without exporting: no file; narrowings kept as edited.

## Data contract
- `open`, `onOpenChange(open)`, `title?` ("Export {Noun}"), `noun` (plural), `nounOne?` (defaults to `noun` minus a trailing s), `count` (rows the narrowing leaves).
- `date?` — the screen's own date control node (`fullWidth`).
- `filtersControl?` — the screen's own `ListFilters` with `fullWidth`.
- `filters: ExportFilterRow[]` — `{key, label, values: {value, onRemove}[], onRemoveAll?}`; rows with no values are skipped.
- `onExport(format)` — `"csv" | "pdf" | "doc"`; the screen closes the sheet and toasts.

## Rules
1. **The export is the list** — filtered, searched, sorted, all pages. — *the file is the rows you're looking at, narrowed by the same questions.* — `(source: ExportSheet.tsx; ui §10c "Export means the rows you are looking at")`
2. **Export always opens this sheet first.** — *exporting silently hid the narrowing at the one moment it matters — when data goes where it can't be corrected.* — `(source: ExportSheet.tsx)`
3. **The sheet's controls are the screen's controls** (same date stepper, same Filters panel, staging until Apply). — *one interaction to learn instead of three.* — `(source: ExportSheet.tsx; 3PL BrokerOrdersScreen.tsx)`
4. **It edits the list's own state, never a private copy.** — *two filter models that disagree is how you export one thing while looking at another.* — `(source: ExportSheet.tsx)`
5. **The count keeps the footer's left; the format travels with the Export button, 12px off it.** — *the count is what you're deciding about; the format is part of the act, not a fact about the list.* — `(source: ExportSheet.tsx)`
6. **No Cancel.** — *the sheet commits nothing on the way through; Close already does what Cancel would.* — `(source: ExportSheet.tsx)`
7. **Plain sections, not cards; the date is one line under its heading.** — *a box around one control is a third edge inside a bordered sheet.* — `(source: ExportSheet.tsx)`
8. **Clear appears only on a row with several values.** — *on one value it duplicates the chip's ✕.* — `(source: ExportSheet.tsx)`
9. **Width 510 (`compact`).** — *controls, not a two-column form.* — `(source: RecordSheet.tsx)`
10. **The toast is one line.** — `(source: memory feedback_toasts; docs/unified-view/3-interactions.md)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Pass the screen's own `ListFilters` (`fullWidth`) and date control.
- ✅ Include search and column search as narrowing rows.
- ✅ Title it "Export {Noun}".
- ❌ Lay out a flat calendar or a flat facet list in the sheet.
- ❌ Add a Cancel button or a second toast line ("The filtered list…").
- ❌ Keep a separate export filter state.

## Checks
- `fpds-units` — header · Date (when dated) · Filters + rows · footer (count left; format + Export right).
- `microcopy` — "Export {Noun}", "Date", "Filters", "Clear", "Export", ".csv / .pdf / .doc", toast "Exporting N {noun} as .{format}".
- By eye: removing a chip in the sheet changes the list behind it; Export disabled at 0.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders (with Date), 3PL › Recurring Orders, 3PL › Directory.
- Sales › Orders, Sales › Quotes, Sales › Customers, Sales › Assets.
- Customer Portal › Orders, › Invoices, › Sites & Assets, › Team & Access; Service Work reference pages (via `unit.standard-list`).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/filter-panel.md ===== -->

# Filter panel

`id: unit.filter-panel` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ListFilters.tsx (ListFilters, ListFilterPanel), prototypes/unified-view/filterMemory.ts` · `registry: fpds-filter-panel`

## Purpose
The faceted filter behind the toolbar's **Filters** button: pick values in one or more categories, see how many rows they leave, then apply. It answers "show me only the rows with these values — and how many is that before I commit?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a list has category columns worth narrowing by (status, type, customer, line of service, business…). Every list's Filters button opens this panel.
- Use the same control with a full-width trigger (`fullWidth`) inside `unit.export-sheet` — it stages until Apply exactly as on the toolbar, and keeps no Recent history. (`ListFilterPanel`, the body alone, exists for a flat layout; no screen uses it today.)
- Don't use for free text (that is the toolbar search or the column search row in `unit.data-table`), for numbers, amounts or dates (date → `unit.date-picker`).
- Don't use for a split flipped many times a session that is exhaustive and short — that is a quick filter in `unit.toolbar` (ui §10c).

## Anatomy
1. **Trigger** — outline `Filters` button with `ListFilter` icon and an applied-count bubble.
2. **Popover** (600px wide), two panes:
   - **Facet menu** (left, 200px): *Recent history* row (`History` icon + count) when any exists, then one row per facet with its ticked count.
   - **Options pane** (right): per-facet **search** field (only facets marked `searchable`), optional **section headings** (uppercase, for a facet with `sections`), **checkbox options** (value, optional second line `description`), "No matches." when search finds nothing.
   - **Recent history pane** (when that row is chosen): one card per applied set — facet label + values per line, then "Just now" / "12 min ago" / "3 h ago".
3. **Footer** — left: live **"N results"**; right: **Clear All** (ghost) then **Apply Filters** (primary).

## Look
- Trigger: `variant="outline" size="sm"` (32px); count bubble `ml-1 rounded-full bg-accent px-1.5 text-xs tabular-nums`.
- Popover: `w-[600px] p-0 overflow-hidden`, panes fixed at 264px tall and scroll inside.
- Facet menu: `border-r border-border p-2 gap-1`; rows `h-8 rounded-md px-2 text-sm`; active row `bg-accent text-accent-foreground`; others `text-muted-foreground`, `hover:bg-accent/60 hover:text-foreground`; counts `text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground`.
- Facet search: `h-8 pl-8` with `Search` glyph; placeholder "Search {facet}…".
- Section heading: `text-xs font-medium tracking-wide uppercase text-muted-foreground`.
- Option rows: `min-h-8 py-1 gap-2`; checkbox nudged `mt-0.5` to center on the first line; value `text-sm`; description `text-xs text-muted-foreground`.
- History cards: `rounded-md border px-3 py-2`; label `text-muted-foreground`, values `text-foreground`; chosen card `border-selected-border bg-selected`; hover `bg-fp-row-hover`.
- Footer: `h-16 border-t border-border px-4`; count `text-sm text-muted-foreground`, number `font-medium text-foreground`; buttons `size="sm"`, `gap-2`.

## States
- **default (closed)** — Filters button; count bubble only when something is applied.
- **open** — first facet selected; ticks start from what is applied (`value`).
- **staged** — ticks changed but not applied: facet-menu counts and the footer count move; the trigger's bubble does not.
- **focus** — `focus-visible:ring-2 ring-ring/50` on menu rows and history cards.
- **selected** — active facet row on `bg-accent`; a history card whose set equals the current ticks is `aria-pressed` on the selection fill.
- **empty** — facet search with no hits: "No matches."; no history: the Recent history row is absent; no `countFor`: footer left is blank.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press **Filters** | Opens the panel; ticks reset to what is applied; Recent history is read (last 24h). |
| Press a facet in the menu | Shows its options; clears the facet search. |
| Type in a facet's search | Filters its options by value AND description (a ship-to is found by its address). |
| Tick / untick an option | Updates the staged set, the facet's count in the menu and the live "N results"; the list does not change yet. |
| Press **Recent history** | Shows the last applied sets, newest first. |
| Press a history card | Puts that set's ticks back (staged); Apply applies it. |
| Press **Apply Filters** | Applies the staged set, records it in Recent history (same set again moves to the top), closes; page → 1; chips appear on `unit.chip-shelf`. |
| Press **Clear All** | Unticks everything AND applies the empty set at once (panel stays open). |
| Esc / click outside | Closes; staged ticks are discarded (next open starts from what is applied). |
| Remove a chip on the shelf, then reopen | The panel shows the chip's value unticked (it re-reads `value` on open). |

## Outcomes
- A list narrowed to rows matching every facet with ticks (AND across facets, OR within one), page 1.
- An emptied filter (Clear All) — every row back, chips gone.
- A history entry (localStorage, per list, 24h, at most 8, newest first, de-duplicated).
- A suggested view name available to `unit.view-row` (the applied ticks are registered with `setActiveFilters`).
- "0 results" shown before applying, when the ticks would leave nothing — the reader can change them before committing.
- Nothing, when closed without Apply.

## Data contract
