- `facets: ListFacet[]` — `{key, label, options? | sections?, searchable?}`; an option is a string or `{value, description}`.
- `value?: ReadonlySet<string>` — applied ids as `facetKey:value` (the caller keeps the truth).
- `onApply?(selected)` — fires on Apply and on Clear All. Without it the panel narrows nothing (demo only — never ship that).
- `countFor?(selected) => number` — what Apply would show, computed with everything else on screen (search, column boxes, date).
- `historyKey?` — which list's history (defaults to the facet keys). `fullWidth` — trigger stretches (`h-8 w-full justify-start`); used in the Export sheet; no history is read or recorded, and it does not register for the suggested view name.
- `ListFilterPanel` (body only): `facets`, `checked`, `onToggle(id)`, `menuWidth`, `height?`, `history?`, `onPickHistory?`.

## Rules
1. **Nothing narrows until Apply.** — *the panel is a draft; a list that jumps on every tick is hard to reason about.* — `(source: ListFilters.tsx; interactions 2-rules §4)`
2. **The trigger's count is what is APPLIED, not what is ticked.** — *the button is read with the panel shut; a number that moved as you ticked would count a narrowing that hasn't happened.* — `(source: ListFilters.tsx)`
3. **Live "N results" on the footer's left; Clear All beside Apply on the right.** — *read the number before committing; Clear All is Apply's quieter sibling.* — `(source: docs/ds/interactions/2-rules.md §4, Pavan 2026-10-05)`
4. **Search is per facet, by an explicit `searchable` flag**, not a length threshold. — *a facet you can take in at a glance doesn't need a field; a flag can't silently appear or vanish as options change.* — `(source: ListFilters.tsx)`
5. **Recent history leads the menu — every set applied on that list in the last 24 hours, by category, newest first; absent when there is none.** A press puts the ticks back; Apply applies. — `(source: ui §10p "Filters remember", Pavan 2026-10-21)`
6. **Line of Service, Business (3PL) and Order Kind (Sales) are facets here**, not tabs or segments. — `(source: ui §10p)`
7. **One facet asking the same question at several grains uses sections** (ship-to name / ZIP / state). — *saves three rows of the menu; selections stay independent.* — `(source: ListFilters.tsx)`
8. **Applying a filter resets to page 1.** — *page 4 of a 2-page result looks empty.* — `(source: docs/unified-view/3-interactions.md §7)`
9. **Every applied filter is said out loud as a chip.** — *a quietly narrowed list is the most common way people conclude data is missing.* — `(source: interactions 2-rules §4c)`
10. **Standard-list facets come from the list's own category columns**: 2–12 shared values, not the first column, not numbers/amounts/dates. — `(source: ui §10p; ServiceListScreen.tsx)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Pass `value`, `onApply` and `countFor` on every list (`countFor` with the same narrowing the list applies).
- ✅ Mark long facets (customers, ship-tos, vendors) `searchable`.
- ✅ Give a ship-to option its address as `description`.
- ❌ Ship a Filters button without `onApply` (parity G6).
- ❌ Apply on tick in the toolbar popover.
- ❌ Add helper text or a toast to the panel.
- ❌ Put dates or amounts in as facets.

## Checks
- `parity-lists` G6 (a Filters panel applies — `value` + `onApply`), G7 (applied filters show as chips).
- `microcopy` — "Filters", "Recent history", "No matches.", "N results"/"1 result", "Clear All", "Apply Filters".
- `fpds-units` — two panes + footer in order.
- By eye: the trigger count does not move while ticking; the footer count does; reopening after removing a chip shows it unticked.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders (Business, Line of Service facets; ship-to sections).
- Sales › Orders (Order Kind facet), Sales › Quotes, Customers, Assets.
- Service Work › Service Orders; Service Work › Parts / Price Book / Agreements / Maintenance / Compliance / Performance / Technicians (auto facets).
- Customer Portal › Orders, › Invoices, › Sites & Assets, › Team & Access.
- Inside any list's Export sheet (full-width trigger, no history).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/lines-table.md ===== -->

# Lines table

`id: unit.lines-table` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/threepl/BrokerOrderSheet.tsx (Delivery Order table), prototypes/customer-portal/OrderRequestSheet.tsx (LinesTable), prototypes/unified-view/ProductTable.tsx (Sales), src/components/form-layout/table-cells.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/HangingAdd.tsx, prototypes/customer-portal/TankLevel.tsx` · `registry: fpds-lines-table`

## Purpose
The order's lines as an editable table: which tank or asset gets which product, and how much. It answers "what is going onto this truck, into which tank" — one row per asset, every cell typed into in place.

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a record holds a repeated line of asset · product · quantity — a fuel order, a recurring rule's ship-to, a portal order. Repeated inputs are a table by default; never a stack of fields.
- Use the Sales variant (`ProductTable`: Product · Quantity, plus priced columns for a quote) where the line has no asset.
- Don't use for documents — use `unit.documents-table`.
- Don't use for a one-off value (one product, one quantity) — that is a field row in the record sheet (`unit.record-sheet`).
- Don't use for several values that belong in ONE cell (work types, categories) — that is `TableCellMultiSelect` inside a row, not rows.

## Anatomy
1. **Section header band** — the `SheetSection` title ("Delivery Order" in 3PL, "Tanks & assets" in the portal); the section is `flush`, so the table's edges are the card's edges.
2. **Header row** — Asset · Product * · Current level · On fulfillment (with an ⓘ breakdown popover, `FulfilmentHead`) · Days to empty (while ordering only) · **Top off** + select-all checkbox · **Qty (gal)** · unnamed bin column.
3. **Rows** — `TableCellSelect`/`TableCellCombobox` (asset, multi-pick from a blank row) · `TableCellSelect` (product, ONE VALUE) · `LevelReading` (gauge + %) · `AfterDelivery` / `FulfilmentPct` · `DaysToEmpty` · Top off checkbox (right-aligned) · `TableCellInput` (numeric, suffix "gal", suggestion placeholder) · `TableRowRemove`.
4. **Total row** (`tfoot`) — "Total" label spanning every column before Qty; the sum under Qty, or "Top-off"/"Top off" when every line tops off.
5. **Hanging add** — `HangingAdd` "Add asset" (3PL) / "Add tank or asset" (portal) off the card's squared bottom-left corner.
6. **Hanging-row flag** (3PL only) — "Mark as winter additive" + switch, at the right end of the hanging row, outside the card.
- Sales variant: Product · Quantity (Gal) · caller's priced columns · bin; Total row under Quantity.

## Look
- Header: `bg-fp-table-header`, `px-3 py-2.5`, label `text-sm font-medium`; required mark = `text-destructive` " *" (`TableRequiredLabel`).
- Rows `h-11`, `border-b border-border`, last row no rule; `group/row` for the bin's hover reveal.
- Editable cell = `td relative p-0`; the control is `absolute inset-0`, `px-3`, hover `bg-accent`, focus `bg-fp-surface-1a` + inset `ring-ring/50`. Read-only cells are plain text with `px-3`; empty readings show "—" in `text-muted-foreground`.
- Qty: `tabular-nums`, unit "gal" as `text-xs text-muted-foreground` suffix inside the cell.
- Column widths are fixed px scaled by `var(--fp-scale)` and match the sheet's `fit`: `CELL.pick` 160 (asset, product), `CELL.number` 110 (readings, qty), `CELL.topOff` 104, `CELL.bin` 52 (portal colgroup 44). Too narrow → the table scrolls sideways (portal `overflow-x-auto` with a min width), never squeezes.
- Hanging add: `h-8`, `rounded-t-none rounded-b-md`, `bg-fp-table-header`, plus icon, label slides out on hover/focus.
- Total row: `h-11 border-t border-border`, label `text-sm text-muted-foreground`, figure `text-sm font-medium tabular-nums text-foreground`.
- Everything left-aligned — headers and cells, numbers included; only the row's action sits at the end.

## States
- **default** — one row always; a blank row shows placeholders "Select asset", "Select product", "0".
- **no ship-to yet** — asset cell disabled, placeholder "Select a ship-to first" (3PL "Select a ShipTo first"); product becomes the first focusable cell.
- **hover** — cell lifts to `bg-accent`; the bin fades in on row hover.
- **focus** — cell washes to `bg-fp-surface-1a` with an inset ring.
- **top off ticked** — Qty cell disabled (`opacity-50`), placeholder "Top-off"; typed number kept for unticking.
- **select-all** — checked when every line tops off, indeterminate when some do.
- **suggestion** — empty Qty shows the suggested gallons as placeholder (monitored tank, while ordering).
- **delete armed** — bin swaps for ✕ (keep) over ✓ (confirm, `bg-destructive`), cursor on ✕.
- **last row** — bin disabled/hidden (an order needs a line).
- **sent / locked** — portal: Days to empty column drops, On fulfillment shows the delivered %; the fieldset is disabled.
- **no total** — `tfoot` hidden until a row carries a value.
- **multi-pick list** — checkboxes, items on other rows ticked and locked, footer "N selected" + Apply (disabled at 0).

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press the hanging add | A blank row is added at the bottom; its first editable cell takes the cursor |
| Open asset cell on a BLANK row | List with checkboxes; assets on other rows show ticked + locked; footer "N selected" · Apply |
| Tick several, press Apply | First pick fills this row; each other pick becomes its own row right under it, in pick order, each with the asset's product filled |
| Open asset cell on a FILLED row | Single pick that swaps this row's asset; list closes on choice |
| Pick an asset | Product fills from the asset (portal: the product last ordered for it, else its own; 3PL: the asset's product and rental flag) |
| Type a new asset name (3PL combobox) | Offered as its own option (`creatable`) |
| Pick a product | Writes that row's product only |
| Tick a row's Top off | Qty cell shuts and reads "Top-off"; total excludes it |
| Untick Top off | Qty cell reopens with the number typed before |
| Tick header select-all | Every line tops off; untick clears all |
| → (ArrowRight) in an empty Qty with a suggestion | The suggested gallons are taken as the value |
| Type in Qty | Digits only kept; total updates |
| Hover row | Bin appears in the trailing column |
| Press bin | Bin swaps to ✕ / ✓ in place, focus on ✕ |
| Press ✓ | Row removed |
| Press ✕, Escape, or focus leaves | Pair closes, row kept |
| Hover ⓘ on "On fulfillment" | Popover explains the reading |

## Outcomes
- Lines added one per asset (several at once from one Apply).
- A line swapped to another asset; a product changed.
- A line set to top off (no number counted) or a quantity typed / taken from the suggestion.
- A line removed after ✓; nothing removed after ✕/Escape.
- Total updated, or reads "Top-off" when every line tops off; no total while nothing is filled.
- Nothing added when the add has nothing left to add (portal: the add is not rendered once every asset of the ship-to has a line).
- Autosaved into the record (the sheet has no Save).

## Data contract
- In: `lines` (each: asset id/name, product, gallons, topOff), the ship-to (its assets), `focusRow` (the row just added), `onChange(nextLines)`; portal also `live` (draft vs sent), `deliveryKey` (planned date, for suggestion / on-fulfillment / days to empty), `request` (sent order).
- Assets carry monitor readings (level, capacity, daily use) when monitored.
- Out: `onChange` with the full next list for every edit, multi-pick apply, top-off toggle and removal.
- Sales `ProductTable`: `lines {name, gal}`, `options`, `onChange`, `focusRow`, `columns` (key, label, width, cell, total, required), `productLabel`, `qtyLabel`.

## Rules
1. **Repeated inputs are an editable table, every cell editable in place** — *Pavan had to ask for a table where three fields were built; consistency is the default* — `(source: ux/2-rules "Forms — like inputs are a table by default"; memory feedback_table_by_default 2026-10-07)`
2. **Same data, same control: a fuel line is the 3PL Delivery Order's line table, Top off checkbox included** — *the portal shipped a "Gal / Fill to full" menu where every module has Top off* — `(source: ux/2-rules "Same data, same control" 2026-10-13)`
3. **Top off sits just before Qty; Qty is the last column; select-all in the Top off header, row ticks right-aligned** — *Top off replaces the number, so the two are never apart, and the figure the row leads to ends the row; Pavan had to ask twice* — `(source: ui §10i; memory feedback_top_off_after_qty 2026-10-20 final)`
4. **The picker that names the row takes several (checkbox + Apply); first pick fills the row, each other its own row; only from a blank row; picks on other rows show ticked + locked** — *the customer had to open the list once per tank; said three times* — `(source: ui §10f "Picking several makes rows"; memory feedback_multi_pick_rows 2026-10-15/16)`
5. **Every other picker in the row is marked ONE VALUE** — *there is no third kind; the eval checks it* — `(source: ui §10f)`
6. **Top off is a named checkbox column, not a unit menu; the typed number is kept but never counted** — *a unit is not a yes/no; a choice that replaces the number is a checkbox* — `(source: ux/2-rules "A unit is not a yes/no"; interactions §4a T3, T5)`
7. **Cells are `TableCell*` filling the `td` (`relative p-0`); never a bordered control inside a cell** — *a control in a cell is a box in a box, and without `relative` it escapes the table* — `(source: ui §10f "The cells")`
8. **The add hangs off the card's squared bottom-left corner, filled with the header tint, plus with words on hover/focus** — *a band reads as an empty extra row; an outline is a second box* — `(source: ui §10f; HangingAdd.tsx)`
9. **New row at the bottom; the cursor goes into its first editable cell; a blank row is never added twice** — *the add should be followed by the answer, not a hunt* — `(source: ui §10f "Rows")`
10. **Delete asks in place (✕ over ✓) via `TableRowRemove`; the bin names its subject; the last line keeps no bin** — *nothing goes on one careless click; an order with no line has no meaning* — `(source: ui §10f "Rows"; memory 2026-09-23)`
11. **The total is a `tfoot` row, shown only once a row carries a value; all top-off says the word** — *a total outside drifts; "0 gal" about nothing says something false* — `(source: interactions §4a T1–T2)`
12. **A property of the whole load (winter additive) rides the hanging row's right end, outside the card** — *it is not a property of a row* — `(source: ui §10f shape item 5)`
13. **The suggestion is the Qty placeholder; → takes it; Qty starts empty; Top off never pre-ticked** — *no separate Suggested column (Pavan, 2026-10-20)* — `(source: memory project_customer_portal 2026-10-20)`
14. **Column widths are `CELL` px, matched by the sheet's `fit`; everything left-aligned** — *the sheet is only as wide as its tables; right-aligned Qty misaligned with its header* — `(source: memory feedback_ui_rules sheet width; ui §10p 2026-10-22)`
15. **One row is always there; never an empty message instead of rows** — `(source: ux/2-rules "Tables — one row is always there" 2026-10-14)`
16. **Tank lines read: Asset · Product · Current level · Days to empty · On fulfillment · Qty · Top off LAST** — the level the quantity lands at sits beside it; Top off's select-all and every row tick share one right edge; the header carries `data-top-off-last` (3PL DO, CP order, 3PL recurring). Other line tables keep Top off · Qty last — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
17. **Every header declares `text-left`** — a bare `<th>` centres — `(source: misses M-005; eval th-align)`
18. **Every column its own width; the table scrolls sideways rather than squeezing; no unit suffix in the Qty cell** (the header says "(gal)") — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
19. **The row delete is a sticky layer at the right edge** — `sticky right-0 z-10 border-l bg-popover`, header cell `bg-fp-table-header`, no heading; the bin shows on row hover; the columns scroll behind it — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
20. **A monitored tank never takes more than its room on the planned day** — the Qty cell holds the figure to the max AS it is typed and a tooltip says why ("<asset> has room for only N gal on the planned day — more would overfill it."); On fulfillment never exceeds 100% — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26; misses M-013)`
21. **The On fulfillment card answers first** — "The tank level post delivery will be X%, based on your daily usage." — then **Show breakdown** opens Current level · Requested · Days to deliver (sub: per-day usage · level on the day of delivery) · On fulfillment total; no formula — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
22. **The asset picker marks the exception and sorts first** — a "No monitor" pill only on assets without a tank monitor; tabs **All · Tank monitor · No tank monitor** above the list (saved-views underline tabs); the list names each asset once — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
23. **Multi-pick from ANY row** — the row's own value opens ticked; kept, the row stays and each other pick adds a row under it; unticked, the first pick takes the row — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26; misses M-015; eval multipick-any-row)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Asset · Product · (readings) · Top off · Qty · bin, in that order, in every module.
- ✅ `multiple` on the asset picker from a blank row; `ONE VALUE` comment on the product picker.
- ✅ Hanging "Add asset" with a singular label; cursor into the new row.
- ✅ Unit "gal" inside the Qty cell as a suffix.
- ❌ A "Gal / Fill to full" menu, a Suggested column, or Top off after Qty.
- ❌ A bordered Select/Combobox dropped into a `td`.
- ❌ A full-width "Add" band inside the card, or an empty-state panel instead of a blank row.
- ❌ A bin that deletes on one click, or right-aligned number columns.

## Checks
- `table-rows` — row pickers without `multiple`/`ONE VALUE`, adds that focus nothing, removes that don't ask or have no subject, Top off not right before Qty.
- `boxed-tables` — table drawing its own box inside a card, add band instead of hanging add.
- `sheets` — a sheet with a table and no `fit`.
- `parity-sheets`, `field-reach` — the same line fields on every create/edit screen of the record.
- By eye: Qty is last, select-all sits at the Top off header's right, ticks line up under it, total sits under Qty, hanging add joins the card corner.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders › Delivery Order sheet — "Delivery Order" section.
- 3PL › Recurring Orders › Create rule — ship-to tab's asset table.
- Customer Portal › Orders › order sheet — "Tanks & assets"; Customer Portal › Recurring Orders › rule sheet.
- Sales › Orders and Sales › Quotes sheets — product table (`ProductTable`, Quote adds priced columns).

> **Sticky delete layer (Pavan, 2026-10-26):** in every editable sheet table the row-remove column is pinned to the right edge (`sticky right-0 z-10 border-l bg-popover`, header `bg-fp-table-header`, no heading); the other columns scroll sideways behind it. The bin still shows on row hover.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/map.md ===== -->

# Map

`id: unit.map` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/OrderMapView.tsx` · `registry: fpds-map`

## Purpose
Shows the list's orders as a list on the left and pins at their ship-to sites on the right, one selection across both. Answers "what else is going out near this one?" — a question no column can sort.

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when the list's records are geographic (orders with a ship-to) and the toolbar offers `mapView`.
- Use in the Customer Portal with live trucks for orders out for delivery.
- Don't use when records have no place (Customers, Directory lists) — `mapView` stays off; no dead icon.
- Don't use for picking or placing one location on a record (ship-to / asset address) — that is the record sheet's own Leaflet map, not this unit.
- Don't use for workflow stage → `unit.board`; for columns → the list table.

## Anatomy
1. **Order list** (left, 340px column) — one row per order: order number + status pill · subtitle (whose order / date and window) · site line with a pin icon.
2. **Plot** (right, fills the rest) — the orders' own geography:
   - **Site pin** — one round pin per ship-to with the count of orders there; site name label under it, shown when lit.
   - **Pin hover card** (optional) — the orders at that site, primary details only.
   - **Roads** (when trucks are given) — dashed line from origin to site, the driven part solid.
   - **Origin mark** — small square at each place trucks left from, named.
   - **Truck** — round button with a truck icon riding its road, pulsing ring.
   - **Unmapped note** — bottom-right "N without a mapped site".

## Look
- Layout `flex gap-4`, full height. List `w-[340px] rounded-md border border-border`, rows `px-3 py-2.5 border-b border-border`; number `font-mono text-sm font-semibold`; status `StatusChip variant="soft"` (the pill, by kind; Sales may pass a chosen `color`); subtitle + site `text-xs text-muted-foreground`, `MapPin` size-3.
- Plot `rounded-md border border-border bg-fp-surface-1a`, no tiles.
- Pin fill by the first order's tone: success `bg-status-success` · processing (caution) `bg-fp-banner-accent-caution` · failed (critical) `bg-status-failed` · info `bg-fp-banner-accent-info` · neutral `bg-muted-foreground`. Size-5 (size-7 + scale-110 lit), `text-[10px] font-semibold` count in white, `ring-2 ring-fp-surface-1a`.
- Site label `text-[10px]`, lit: `bg-selected text-selected-foreground`.
- Road: dashed `stroke-muted-foreground/50`; driven part `stroke-fp-banner-accent-caution`. Truck size-7 `bg-fp-banner-accent-caution`, white icon, `shadow-md`, ping ring `/40` (`motion-safe` only), moves with a 1s linear transition. Origin mark size-3 `bg-foreground`.
- Hover card `w-80 p-0`, opens above the pin (open 80ms, close 120ms).

## States
- **Row default / hover** — hover `bg-accent`.
- **Lit (row or pin hovered)** — the row turns `bg-selected` with `text-selected-foreground` number; every row at that site lights; its pin grows and shows its site name.
- **Focus** — 2px `ring-ring/50` on rows, pins and trucks.
- **No mappable orders** — the plot frames the whole country (fallback bounds), no pins.
- **Some unmapped** — note "N without a mapped site".
- **Trucks moving** — CP re-reads positions every 3s while the map view is open.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Hover a list row | Lights that row's site: the pin grows, shows its name; all rows at that site light. |
| Hover a pin | Lights the site's rows; with `hoverCard`, a card lists the orders there (CP: number, status, ship-to · date and window, lines, truck · driver · ETA). |
| Click a list row | Opens that order's sheet. |
| Click a pin | Opens the sheet of the first order at that site. |
| Click a truck | Opens the order it carries. |
| Tab to a row / pin / truck, Enter | Same as click. |
| Change search, filters, date range | List and pins redraw from the same narrowed set; the frame re-fits to the orders' spread. |

## Outcomes
- A record sheet opens (row, pin or truck).
- The reader sees which orders share a site and how they spread.
- Orders without a site are counted, never silently dropped.
- Empty narrowed set → the list's empty body replaces the view.

## Data contract
- `orders: MapOrder[]` — `id`, `orderNo`, `site` (a ship-to name; must match a known site or carry `coords`), `subtitle`, `status` (its word), `tone` (from the module's kind map), optional `color` (Sales chosen stage color), optional `coords {lat,lng}`.
- `onOpenOrder(id)` — reports which order to open.
- `hoverCard?(rows) → node` — optional; omitted, a pin has no hover card.
- `trucks?: MapTruck[]` — `id`, `orderId`, `label`, `from`, `to`, `progress` 0–1, optional `fromLabel`.
- `minPad` — least margin round the pins in degrees (default 1.2 for a region; CP uses 0.04 for one city).
- The map takes the whole sorted set, never one page.

## Rules
1. **One map for every orders screen, fed by `MapOrder`** — *forking per module would make the same screen behave differently depending on where you came in.* `(source: OrderMapView.tsx)`
2. **The map takes the sorted set, not the page** — *a page boundary is a table's property; half the day's work missing from a map is a map that lies.* `(source: BrokerOrdersScreen.tsx)`
3. **List and plot are one selection** — *hover one lights the other; click either opens the order.* `(source: OrderMapView.tsx)`
4. **Pins group by site** — *one dot per site hiding six orders hides what the map was opened to show.* `(source: OrderMapView.tsx)`
5. **The frame is the orders' own spread** — *a day's work in one valley should not be three pins in a corner of the country.* `(source: OrderMapView.tsx)`
6. **It draws the data's geography, not tiles** — *no network dependency or key in a file meant to open from disk; swap in tiles and pins don't move.* `(source: OrderMapView.tsx)`
7. **Pin color follows the status kind; info is blue like its chip** — *Scheduled and Dispatched had fallen through to gray.* `(source: OrderMapView.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-07)`
8. **A map view only where records are geographic; Map has no view tools** — *a control with nothing behind it is worse than none; a map has no columns to search or hide.* `(source: ui/2-rules.md §10p; ListToolbar mapView)`
9. **CP map: pin hover = primary details, press = sheet; trucks move from the clock** — *live tracking for the customer.* `(source: customer-portal README; memory 2026-10-13)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Pass every order a `tone` from the module's kind map.
- ✅ Count orders that cannot be placed.
- ✅ Keep search, filters and the date scope working on the map.
- ❌ Paginate or feed the map the current page.
- ❌ Build a module-specific map, or add Search-by-column / column tools to it.
- ❌ Animate the truck ping when reduced motion is set.

## Checks
- `status-kinds` (tones come from kinds), `tokens`, `parity-shell` (toolbar/view switch).
- By eye: hover links list ↔ pin, pin count, label on light, unmapped note, map icon only on geographic lists.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders — Map view.
- Sales › Fuel Orders and Asset Orders — Map view (chosen stage colors).
- Customer Portal › Orders — Map view (hover cards, live trucks from the terminal).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/pagination.md ===== -->

# Pagination

`id: unit.pagination` · `layer: unit` · `code: src/components/tables-layout/TablePagination.tsx (TablePagination, JumpToPage), src/components/tables-layout/data.ts (paginationRange), src/components/tables-layout/GroupedListFooter.tsx` · `registry: fpds-pagination`

## Purpose
The footer under a list's table: where you are in the list, a way to the other pages, and how many rows a page holds. While the table is grouped, the grouped footer stands in and reports the list's shape instead. It answers "which slice am I seeing, and how do I see the rest?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use `TablePagination` under every paged list table, in its own `mt-4 shrink-0` block.
- Use `GroupedListFooter` instead while Group by is on (the list scrolls whole).
- Don't use under a board or map display.
- Don't use for an editable table inside a sheet (those show all rows).
- Don't replace it with a count ("24 rules") — a count is not a page position.

## Anatomy
**TablePagination** — three zones:
1. **Range** (left) — "Showing 1-25 of 62".
2. **Pages** (center) — Previous · windowed page numbers (first, current ±1, last) · `…` · Next. Each `…` is a **Jump to page** trigger: popover with heading "Jump to page" and a numeric "Page #" field.
3. **Rows per page** (right) — select: 10 · 25 · 50 · 100.

**GroupedListFooter** — same left/right skeleton:
1. **Shape** (left) — "4 groups · 62 orders" (+ " · 12,400 gal" when the list sums a measure); when a record can sit in several groups, a `Layers` note: "orders appear in every group they match, so the group counts add up to more than 62".
2. **Expand all / Collapse all** (right) — ghost button with `ChevronsUpDown` / `ChevronsDownUp`.

## Look
- Band: `h-9` (36px, exactly its controls), `flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-muted-foreground`; the page owns the spacing (`mt-4` above, the content layer's 16px below).
- Range text: `shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap`, numbers locale-formatted.
- Page links: DS `PaginationLink` (36px); **current page** `border-fp-outline-2 bg-fp-table-header` (the table header's color); Previous/Next at 50% opacity and not clickable at the ends.
- `…`: 36px (`size-9`) `rounded-md text-muted-foreground`, `MoreHorizontal size-4`.
- Jump popover: `rounded-lg bg-fp-surface-1b p-1`; heading `text-xs font-medium text-primary`; field `h-8 w-20 rounded-md border-fp-outline-2 bg-fp-surface-1a`.
- Rows per page: quiet select — 28px tall (`h-7`), 58px wide, `rounded-sm` (6px), `border-fp-outline-2 bg-fp-surface-1b`, `text-xs`, chevron `size-3.5`.
- No shadows on any control.
- Grouped footer: `text-sm text-muted-foreground`, counts `tabular-nums`; note `text-xs` with `Layers size-3.5`; button `variant="ghost" size="sm"`.

## States
- **default** — page 1 of N; Previous disabled.
- **hover / focus** — DS link states; `…` goes to `text-foreground`; ring on focus.
- **active** — current page outlined on the header fill.
- **disabled** — Previous on page 1, Next on the last page.
- **open** — Jump to page popover with the field focused.
- **empty** — 0 rows: "Showing 0-0 of 0", one page.
- **grouped** — the grouped footer replaces pagination; button reads "Collapse all" until every group is collapsed, then "Expand all".

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press a page number | Goes to that page. |
| Press Previous / Next | One page back / forward (no-op at the ends). |
| Press `…` | Opens Jump to page with the field focused. |
| Type digits + Enter in "Page #" | Jumps to that page and closes, if it is between 1 and the last page; otherwise nothing happens. Non-digits are ignored as typed. |
| Pick a rows-per-page value | Changes page size; the list returns to page 1. |
| Apply a filter / search / sort elsewhere | The screen resets to page 1; the range text follows. |
| Grouped: press Collapse all / Expand all | Collapses or expands every group. |

## Outcomes
- A different page of rows; the range text updates.
- A different page size (saved in the active view's state where the view captures `pageSize`), back on page 1.
- Nothing, for an out-of-range jump.
- Grouped: all groups collapsed or expanded; an honest note when group counts over-add.

## Data contract
- `TablePagination`: `page` (1-based), `pageSize`, `total` (filtered row count, all pages), `onPageChange(page)`, `onPageSizeChange(size)`, `pageSizeOptions?` (default `[10, 25, 50, 100]`), `className?` (band height override — `h-[72px]` restores the old Figma band; nothing wants it).
- Window: `paginationRange(current, total, siblings = 1)` → page numbers and `"ellipsis"` slots.
- `GroupedListFooter`: `groupCount`, `rowCount`, `noun`, `total?`, `unit?`, `overCounts`, `allCollapsed`, `onExpandAll()`, `onCollapseAll()`.

## Rules
1. **Use `TablePagination`'s own page sizes and copy.** — *overriding either makes one screen count differently and the reader re-learns the footer per list.* — `(source: docs/ds/interactions/2-rules.md §4c)`
2. **Pagination and grouping are exclusive; while grouped the list scrolls whole and `GroupedListFooter` reports its shape.** — *at 25 a page a group straddles the boundary and its header claims rows not on screen.* — `(source: interactions §4c; GroupedListFooter.tsx)`
3. **Both footers keep the same left/center/right skeleton.** — *the bottom of the screen doesn't jump when grouping turns on.* — `(source: GroupedListFooter.tsx)`
4. **The band is exactly its controls (`h-9`); the page sets the gaps (`mt-4`).** — *a 72px band doubled the gap below and pressed the footer flush to the table.* — `(source: TablePagination.tsx)`
5. **The current page wears the table header's fill.** — `(source: TablePagination.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-14)`
6. **Rows per page is quiet: white, 12px type, 58×28, 6px corners.** — `(source: TablePagination.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-14, tried on Customer Portal › Orders then everywhere)`
7. **Jump to page lives behind the ellipsis** — no separate go-to field on the bar. — *progressive disclosure.* — `(source: TablePagination.tsx, Figma 27728:13260)`
8. **Applying a filter resets to page 1.** — `(source: docs/unified-view/3-interactions.md §7)`
9. **A fact about the whole list that a count can't carry sits beside the pagination.** — `(source: ui §10c)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Pass the FILTERED total.
- ✅ Swap to `GroupedListFooter` whenever the table is grouped.
- ❌ Custom page sizes or "Page 1 of 3" copy.
- ❌ Paginate a grouped table.
- ❌ Put shadows on the page links or select.

## Checks
- `parity-lists` G19 — a paged table has the pagination footer.
- `fpds-units` — three zones in order; grouped footer when grouped.
- By eye: 16px between table and footer; current page on the header fill; rows-per-page 58×28.

## Reference
- Every list: 3PL › Delivery Orders, Sales › Orders, Sales › Quotes, Service Work › Parts, Customer Portal › Orders, › Invoices.
- Grouped footer: 3PL › Delivery Orders, 3PL › Directory, Sales › Orders, Sales › Customers, Sales › Assets, Customer Portal › Orders — each with Group by on.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/record-sheet.md ===== -->

# Record sheet

`id: unit.record-sheet` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/RecordSheet.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/PendingStrip.tsx, src/components/side-sheet/parts.tsx, src/components/side-sheet/form-steps.tsx` · `registry: fpds-record-sheet`

## Purpose
The right-hand side sheet that creates or edits ONE record — an order, a quote, a customer, an asset, a rule. It answers "what is this record, what does it still need, and what is the next thing to do with it?" Create and edit are the same sheet.

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a single record is created, read or edited from a list, board, map or dashboard tile.
- Use `level={2}` when the sheet opens over another sheet (invite partner over an order, a vendor quote, a dispute over an invoice).
- Don't use for a bulk change across many records — use `unit.bulk-bar` (Bulk edit).
- Don't use for a list's filters/export controls as a form — those are a `compact` sheet of controls, not a record.
- Don't build a page or modal for a record's create form — a separate create screen is how two descriptions of one record come to exist.

## Anatomy
1. **Header** — `SheetTitle` (create title or edit title) · `headerAside` beside it (status chip / `DraftStatusChip`) · optional `headerSubtitle` line under the title · on the right `headerActions` (things that OPEN something: history, preview) · `headerMenu` (⋯ — acts ON the record, e.g. delete, duplicate) · Close ✕ last.
2. **Steps strip** (optional, `steps`) — `SheetSteps` / `StageTrack compact` under the header, outside the scroll, on the sheet's own white (`bg-popover`), no rule under it.
3. **Body** — one scroll; sections (`SheetSection`, card by default) 40px apart. Two-column variant: form (fills) + **rail** 340px, sticky (`unit.summary-rail`).
4. **Quiet pattern** — added by the sheet itself when the content is half the visible height or less.
5. **Footer banner** (`footerBanner`, optional) — fixed strip above the footer: what the primary is waiting on (`PendingStrip`) or an exception that stops the work.
6. **Footer** — left: `footerAside` (decisions taken at the moment of committing); right: Cancel (only where `showCancel`) · the primary (`footerPrimary`, the next act).

## Look
- Sheet: `gap-0 p-0`, side `right`, overlay gap `--fp-sheet-gap` = 56px (level 1) / 112px (level 2); width `--fp-sheet-w` = content width × `var(--fp-scale, 1)`, clamped to `var(--fp-vw) − gap`.
- Widths: `form` 600 (default) · `compact` 510 · `data` 1200 min, fields three across (`DATA_FIELDS` = `grid grid-cols-3 gap-4`) · `fit` = widest table's `CELL` widths + 34, + 364 with rail (340 + 24), never under 600 · `full` = viewport less the gap (only while a step column shows).
- `CELL` widths (px): check 44 · flag 72 · topOff 104 · level 150 · battery 100 · name 200 · pick 160 · number 110 · date 140 · status 140 · text 240 · bin 52. A table's own colgroup uses the same numbers.
- Header: `border-b border-border p-4`, items-start, gap-4; title `text-base`; title row gap 2.5; Close `text-muted-foreground`, opacity 70 → 100 on hover.
- Body: `p-4`, `flex-col gap-10` (40px between groups); `plain` section title 16px from its content.
- Footer banner: `border-t border-border px-4 py-2.5`. Footer: `border-t border-border p-4`, gap-3; aside `gap-x-8`.
- Draft note: `text-xs text-muted-foreground`, `Loader2` spinning while saving, `Check` once saved; `aria-live="polite"`.
- Pending strip: neutral = `text-muted-foreground`; caution (raised by a press) = `text-fp-banner-accent-caution`; all-clear = `text-fp-banner-accent-success` with `CircleCheck`. Item label is underlined (`decoration-current/40`, full on hover). Counter `text-xs tabular-nums`.
- Section blink: `.fp-attention` (outline, once); a grid column blinks a fill `.fp-attention-fill` (`data-blink`).
- Rail: `grid items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_340px]`, `aside` `flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-6 lg:sticky lg:top-0`.

## States
- **create** — create title and create action on the primary; no rail (Summary · Associated records · Activity) while creating (Customer Portal's order request keeps its summary on the right while creating).
- **edit** — edit title; primary defaults to "Confirm" or the record's next act.
- **draft idle** — nothing beside the title (no "Saved" before the first change).
- **draft saving** — "Draft saving…" with spinner (500ms after a change settles).
- **draft saved** — "Draft saved" ✓. On a Draft the status chip gives way to this line; on any other status the chip stays and the note sits beside it.
- **not ready** — primary still pressable; pressing raises the caution pending strip and travels to the first gap.
- **ready** — pending strip (if standing) shows "Everything this record needs is filled in" in success color.
- **short content** — quiet pattern fills the rest of the body; disappears once content passes half the height.
- **level 2** — never wider than the sheet beneath less 56px, so a strip of it always shows.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Open from a row, card, pin or tile | Sheet slides in from the right at its content width |
| Type in any field (existing record) | Draft chip: "Draft saving…" → "Draft saved" |
| Press primary, everything filled | `onSubmit` runs and the sheet closes |
| Press primary with `pending` items / `canSubmit` false | Nothing is submitted; the caution `PendingStrip` rises above the footer; the body scrolls (320ms, ease-out) to the first item's section and blinks its outline once |
| Press the pending item's label | Scrolls to that section and blinks it once (800ms); focus stays on the strip |
| Press ‹ / › on the strip | Steps to the previous / next outstanding item; "Pending" stays put; chevron disabled at either end |
| Last pending item filled | Strip says "Everything this record needs is filled in" |
| Press a step in the steps strip | Scrolls to that step's section (one scroll; nothing gated) |
| Press ⋯ (`headerMenu`) | Menu of acts on the record (delete, duplicate, …) |
| Press a header action (e.g. history icon) | Opens that thing (often a level-2 sheet) |
| Press ✕ / overlay / Esc | Sheet closes; a raised pending strip resets |
| Close a NEW order with anything typed | "Discard this order?" — Keep editing / Discard |
| Press Cancel (only where `showCancel`) | Closes the sheet |
| Open a level-2 sheet from inside | It opens 112px from the edge, narrower than the sheet below |
| Stage changes while open | Sheet scrolls to the section that stage's work lives in — on a change, never on open |
| Form grows past half the visible height | Quiet pattern disappears and takes no space |

## Outcomes
- A record created (create action) — it appears in its list/board and the sheet re-opens on it with its rail (3PL orders).
- A record kept as it is typed (autosave) — no Save needed.
- A record moved on one stage by the primary (the ladder: e.g. Create Order → Quoting).
- A Draft parked with "Save as Draft" (outline, left of the commit act; only while it is a draft).
- Nothing submitted, and the reader taken to the first missing field.
- A discarded new order (Discard confirmed) — nothing kept.
- A closed sheet with no change — nothing logged, no toast.

## Data contract
- `mode` create | edit; `createTitle`, `editTitle`; `createAction`, `editAction` (default "Confirm").
- `size` form | compact | data | full, or `fit { tables: number[][] (CELL widths), rail?: boolean }` — per step.
- `level` 1 | 2; `open`, `onOpenChange(open)`.
- `headerAside`, `headerSubtitle`, `headerActions`, `headerMenu`.
- `steps` (SheetSteps / StageTrack node), `stepNav`, `stepColumn` (off everywhere; archived), `stepsAction`.
- `pending: { key, label (an instruction), target? (section id) }[]`, `canSubmit`.
- `footerPrimary` (replaces the primary; `null` removes it), `footerAside`, `footerBanner`, `showCancel`.
- `onSubmit()` — called on a ready press; the sheet then closes.
- `useDraftSaveState(draft)` → idle | saving | saved, fed into `DraftStatusChip { tone, color?, label, saveState, showStatus? }`.
- Exports for builders: `CELL`, `fitWidth`, `FORM_WIDTH` 600, `DATA_WIDTH` 1200, `DATA_FIELDS`; `attend(id)`, `blink(id)`, `blinkGroup(name)`, `BLINK_MS`.

## Rules
1. **A sheet with editable fields autosaves and says so** — *a reader who must remember Save loses work; a silent autosave is not trusted* — `(source: docs/ds/ux/2-rules.md Side-sheet anatomy; docs/unified-view/3-interactions.md §1)`.
2. **Nothing before the first change** — no "Saved" on open — *a sheet that opens saying "Saved" claims to have done something it has not* — `(source: RecordSheet.tsx useDraftSaveState; 3-interactions §1)`.
3. **No Save, no Update, no Cancel** — Close is the exit; the only "Save" allowed is "Save as Draft" on a draft — *Cancel promises an undo an autosaving sheet can't keep* — `(source: ux/2-rules Side-sheet anatomy, Forms 2026-09-25)`.
4. **The primary is the next act, one only, a ladder** — the label is the act, never the state — *what's not happened yet belongs in the banner, not on a button* — `(source: ux/2-rules; unified-view/2-layouts §8)`.
5. **A primary that isn't ready stays pressable and says why on the press** (pending strip, travel, one blink) — *a dead button with no reason is the commonest broken thing in a form* — `(source: G25, Pavan 2026-10-07; ui/7-variants §1 footer)`.
6. **Pending items are instructions** ("Choose a customer"), one at a time, under a fixed word "Pending" — *three missing fields cost one line, and the subject never changes* — `(source: ux/2-rules Side-sheet anatomy)`.
7. **Blink once, leave no trace** — *two flashes ask to be watched; a ring that stays reads as a state* — `(source: PendingStrip.tsx, Pavan 2026-09-22)`.
8. **Create and edit are the same sheet** — *one layout to learn, no create form to drift* — `(source: ux/2-rules, promoted 2026-09-22)`.
9. **Width follows content**: form 600, `fit` from `CELL` widths (+rail), data 1200, per step; clamp to viewport − 56 — *a sheet sized for more than it holds reads as empty* — `(source: ui/7-variants §1, Pavan 2026-10-15)`.
10. **Every order sheet fits its tables plus its rail**; no form uses `full` — `(source: ui/7-variants §1, Pavan 2026-10-15)`.
11. **A sheet over a sheet takes the gap twice** and is never wider than the one beneath less 56 — *otherwise it reads as the same sheet having changed* — `(source: ux/2-rules, Pavan 2026-09-22; RecordSheet ParentSheetWidth 2026-10-15)`.
12. **Editable left, derived right** — the rail is read-only and sticky — *once an editable and a computed number share a column they look identical* — `(source: ux/2-rules two-column sheet)`.
13. **No rail while creating** — *the record doesn't exist yet* — `(source: feedback_ui_rules; ux/2-rules)`.
14. **A section is a card, 40px apart** — *the border says where a subject ends at any length* — `(source: ux/2-rules Section rhythm)`.
15. **A stepped create form is ONE vertical scroll**, steps drawn as `StageTrack` rings, pressing a step scrolls, nothing gated — `(source: ux/2-rules, Pavan 2026-10-16)`.
16. **Step column is off in every module** — `(source: RecordSheet stepColumn, Pavan 2026-10-07; docs/ds/ui/archive/step-column.md)`.
17. **Quiet pattern only when content ≤ 50% of the visible body; the sheet places it, never by hand** — `(source: ui/2-rules §11d, Pavan 2026-10-16)`.
18. **Side-by-side fields fill their row** (fr / flex-1, 24px gap) — re-check every row after a width change — `(source: ui/2-rules §10e, Pavan 2026-10-16)`.
19. **Removing an optional section is a minus, never a bin**; confirm only when it has content — `(source: ux/2-rules SheetSectionRemove)`.
20. **No helper text that explains a control** — state and consequence only — `(source: ux/2-rules helper text; feedback_no_unrequested_ui)`.
21. **Closing a new order with anything typed asks "Discard this order?"** — `(source: ui/7-variants §1 stepped form, Pavan 2026-10-06)`.
22. **A read stage speaks quietly** (3PL from Assigned on): every unit heading 14px medium muted — the rail cards' voice — units 24px apart, heading → box 10px; facts, not disabled fields — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
23. **Order facts in three groups side by side, ruled top to bottom** — what kind (job type · order type · PO · urgent) | who and where (customer · hub · ship-to) | tags — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
24. **A full-width unit under the form + rail (Pricing) is preceded by an edge-to-edge rule** through the sheet padding — 40px from the form's last unit to the rule, 40px from the rule to the unit — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
25. **Several partners priced → saved-views underline tabs on the column header's bottom rule**, the label level with the sibling headings; Finalize takes the tab on show — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
26. **A total row draws `border-t` only** — `border-y` stacks on the card rim — `(source: misses M-017; eval double-stroke)`
27. **A field that IS a value is read until adjusted** — a pencil BEFORE the figure (tooltip "Make adjustments" + why), the figure on the same right edge as its siblings; pressed, the box replaces it, right-aligned; never the box and the same figure side by side — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26; misses M-018)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Pass `fit` with `CELL` widths whenever the sheet holds a table; pass the current step's tables.
- ✅ Put `DraftStatusChip` in `headerAside`; `showStatus={false}` when a stage track already shows the status.
- ✅ Pass `pending` with instruction labels and section `target` ids.
- ✅ `showCancel={false}` on every autosaving sheet.
- ✅ Use `level={2}` for any sheet opened over another.
- ❌ A Save / Update / Save Changes / Cancel button in the footer.
- ❌ A fixed px width or `size="full"` for an order sheet.
- ❌ A disabled primary with no reason.
- ❌ Hand-placing `QuietPattern` in a sheet.
- ❌ A bespoke summary or activity block in the rail (use `unit.summary-rail`).

## Checks
- `sheets` — a RecordSheet carrying a table (`<table`, `ProductTable`, `DocumentsTable`, `ListTable`) without `fit`; census of sizes.
- `parity-sheets` — G22 draft chip shown, G23 no Cancel / Save / Update, G25 dead primary says why, G27 no step column, G28 no retired `fill`, G29/G30 shared cards.
- `fields-fill` — fixed-width field containers.
- `field-reach` — a new field reaches every create/edit/list screen of its record.
- `microcopy` — helper text.
- `tokens`, `wcag-contrast`.
- By eye: after any width change, screenshot the whole sheet and check every field row reaches its right edge; the level-2 strip shows; the pending travel lands with 12px air above the section.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders — order sheet (`threepl/BrokerOrderSheet.tsx`): fit + rail, ⋯ menu, Quotation summary, Documents, Discard dialog.
- 3PL › Asset Orders (`threepl/AssetMovementSheet.tsx`); 3PL › Recurring rules (`threepl/RecurringRuleSheet.tsx`, `RecurringProgramCreate.tsx` with steps).
- 3PL › Directory — customer / vendor records (`threepl/DirectoryRecordSheet.tsx`); level 2: Invite partner (`InvitePartnerSheet.tsx`), Vendor quote (`VendorQuoteSheet.tsx`).
- Sales › Orders and Quotes (`OrderSheet.tsx`, `QuoteSheet.tsx`); Sales › Customers / Assets (`CustomerSheet.tsx`, `AssetSheet.tsx` — `data` when existing, `form` when creating); Export (`ExportSheet.tsx`, `compact`).
- Service Work › Service Orders (`service/ServiceOrderSheet.tsx`); dispatch at level 2 (`service/DispatchFlow.tsx`).
- Customer Portal › Orders — order sheet (`customer-portal/OrderRequestSheet.tsx`); Invoices (`InvoiceSheet.tsx`, dispute at level 2 `DisputeSheet.tsx`); Sites & Assets — two-step ship-to and asset sheets (`ShipToSheet.tsx`, `AssetSheet.tsx`); Recurring rule (`RuleSheet.tsx`, steps).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/recurring-section.md ===== -->

# Recurring section

`id: unit.recurring-section` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/RecurringOrderSection.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/threepl/RuleFrequency.tsx (RuleFrequencyPicker, NextDeliveries, scheduleSummary, encodeFrequency), src/components/side-sheet/parts.tsx (SheetSection, SheetSectionRemove)` · `registry: fpds-recurring-section`

## Purpose
An optional section on any order that can repeat: a dashed "Add Recurring Order" row until it is asked for, then the recurring rule's own frequency unit in a box. It answers "does this order repeat, on which days or dates, how often, until when" — set the same way wherever a repeat is set.

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use on every order sheet whose order can repeat (3PL Delivery Order, Customer Portal order).
- Use the frequency unit alone (`RuleFrequencyPicker`, in a "Frequency" section) inside a recurring RULE form, where repeating is the whole point.
- Don't use on Service Work service orders (they don't repeat — Agreements/Maintenance do) or Sales orders.
- Don't use on 3PL movement (asset) orders, or on a portal order made by a rule (the rule owns the repeating).
- Don't use a checkbox + frequency dropdown or a list of cadences.

## Anatomy
1. **Section** — `SheetSection` titled "Recurring order" (3PL passes "Recurring Order"); header action = `SheetSectionRemove` (minus icon, label "Remove recurring order" slides out on hover/focus) once on.
2. **Off: dashed add row** — "Add Recurring Order" left · outline "+ Add" button right.
3. **On: bordered box** (`fieldset`) holding **`RuleFrequencyPicker`**:
   - **Days | Date** — DS segment control.
   - **Days basis** — left column: "Select day/s" + seven day badges S M T W T F S (or, in a custom cycle, one badge row per "Week N"); under them "Daily" + switch and "Custom cycle" + switch side by side. Right side, two equal fields: **Repeat** (Weekly · Monthly · Yearly) and its follow-up — **Times a day** (1–3; >1 shows "Drop N" time inputs), **Which part of the month** (checkbox dropdown: Every week · First–Fourth week + "of the month"), or **Which part of the year** (First month of every year · Specific months → month chips). Cycle on: **Cycle length** (2/3/4 weeks) + **Week 1 starts**, and a **Next 4 weeks** preview below.
   - **Date basis** — "Select dates" + day numbers 1–31 filling the row; **Repeat** at the row's right edge (Every month · Every 2/3/6 months · Every year · Does not repeat).
   - **End date** — ghost "+ Add an end date", then a date field + bin "Remove the end date".
4. (Rule forms only) **Next 4 deliveries** — `NextDeliveries` strip pinned above the form's Create button.

## Look
- Add row: `rounded-md border border-dashed border-fp-outline-1 px-4 py-3`, label `text-sm text-foreground`, button `variant="outline" size="sm"`.
- On box: `rounded-md border border-fp-outline-1 p-4`.
- Segment: DS `ToggleGroup` outline `sm`, `h-7`, items `text-xs`; selected is greyscale (the segment control's own selection), never brand or violet.
- Day badges / date numbers / month chips: picked = `bg-accent-violet text-accent-violet-foreground`; unpicked = `bg-fp-card-1 text-muted-foreground` (dates `text-foreground`), hover to `text-foreground` / `bg-accent`. Badges `h-8 rounded-md text-sm font-medium`, stretching to fill a 360px days column; dates `h-9`, auto-fill grid of ≥2.5rem.
- Field labels `text-sm font-medium text-foreground`; "Drop N" `text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground`.
- Days column 360px, then `gap-15`, then Repeat + follow-up as a `grid-cols-2 gap-6` that fills the rest. Date basis: dates `flex-1`, Repeat `w-62`, `gap-12`. End date field `w-62` (as wide as the badges).
- Validation lines `text-xs text-status-failed`.
- Preview / next-deliveries tiles: `rounded-md border border-fp-outline-1`, four across.

## States
- **off** — dashed "Add Recurring Order" row.
- **on** — box with the picker; minus in the section header.
- **locked, off** — section not rendered.
- **locked, on** — picker shown disabled (`fieldset disabled`), no minus.
- **daily** — all seven badges lit; Daily switch on.
- **custom cycle** — week rows replace the badges; Daily switch disabled (faint); Cycle length + Week 1 starts replace Repeat; Next 4 weeks preview below ("No delivery" for an empty week).
- **weekly × 2–3** — Drop time inputs under Repeat.
- **yearly, specific months** — month chips row.
- **end date set** — date field + bin instead of "+ Add an end date".
- **error** — "Pick at least one day." / "Pick at least one date." / "Pick at least one week."

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press "+ Add" | Section turns on with a weekly frequency on the planned date's weekday (today's if no date), or the previously stored schedule |
| Press the section minus | Section turns off (no confirm); recurring flag cleared |
| Press Days / Date | Switches the basis |
| Press a day badge | Toggles that day; all seven = daily |
| Turn Daily on | All seven lit; the previous pick is remembered |
| Turn Daily off | The previous pick comes back (or none) |
| Turn Custom cycle on | Current days become Week 1, Week 2 empty; cycle settings and preview appear |
| Turn Custom cycle off | Back to weekly |
| Change Cycle length | Week rows added/removed, existing picks kept |
| Choose Repeat | Shows its follow-up field (Times a day / Which part of the month / Which part of the year) |
| Choose Times a day > 1 | A time input per drop |
| Tick weeks in the month dropdown | Several weeks, menu stays open; "Every week" clears single weeks and vice versa |
| Press a date number | Toggles that date (stored as its next occurrence) |
| Choose date Repeat | Every N months, every year, or once |
| Press "+ Add an end date" | End date set one year ahead, editable |
| Press the end date's bin | End date cleared (no end) |

## Outcomes
- The order carries `recurring: true` and a schedule (the frequency unit), plus its one-line summary ("Weekly · Tue, Fri", "Every month · 5th, 20th", "Every 2 weeks", "Every day") and the end date.
- Turning it off clears the recurring flag.
- Validation lines when nothing is picked; nothing blocks the sheet.
- Rule forms: the next four delivery dates update live; "—" when none.
- Autosaved with the record.

## Data contract
- `RecurringOrderSection` in: `on`, `schedule` (RuleFrequency or null), `plannedDay` (weekday 0–6), `locked`, `title`, `id`. Out: `onChange(frequency)` on turn-on and every edit; `onRemove()`.
- `RuleFrequency`: basis (days|date), dates, dateRepeat, dayMode (daily|specific), days, repeat (weekly|monthly|yearly|cycle), times, monthWeeks, yearlyMode, months, endKey (null = no end), cycleWeeks, cycleStartKey.
- Helpers: `blankFrequency()`, `scheduleSummary(f)`, `encodeFrequency(f)` (3PL stores it as text), `nextHits(f, 4)` → `NextDeliveries`.

## Rules
1. **Every order that can repeat uses this section: dashed "Add Recurring Order" → the frequency unit in a box; the minus removes it** — *Pavan, 2026-10-20: apply in all modules; the Load Order's optional-section shape* — `(source: ui §10m; memory feedback_delivery_timeline)`
2. **The frequency unit is the recurring rule's own `RuleFrequencyPicker`, never a frequency dropdown** — *one unit everywhere, so a repeat is set the same way wherever it is set* — `(source: RecurringOrderSection.tsx; ui §10m)`
3. **Two questions — which days, how it repeats — never one list of phrases** — *two lit badges and "monthly" are understood before they are read* — `(source: RuleFrequency.tsx header)`
4. **Picked days, dates and months are solid accent violet with white text** — *violet highlights; brand orange means "you chose this" for selection/focus* — `(source: ui §10c; memory feedback_accent_violet 2026-10-16)`
5. **The Days|Date segment is greyscale** — *segment controls are greyscale everywhere; the accent trial was "too much"* — `(source: memory feedback_ui_rules 2026-10-16; project_3pl_recurring)`
6. **Date basis = day numbers 1–31, no month to step; Repeat at the row's right edge** — *dates repeat every month or every N* — `(source: memory project_3pl_recurring 2026-10-20)`
7. **Days column wide, Repeat fields share the rest equally** — *the fixed 340px Repeat column left a third of the card empty* — `(source: ui §10e; memory feedback_fields_fill 2026-10-16)`
8. **No end date is the default; the offer to add one is the whole control** — `(source: RuleFrequency.tsx)`
9. **A new repeat starts on the planned date's weekday** — `(source: memory project_customer_portal 2026-10-20)`
10. **Off and locked → the section is not shown** — *a repeat that can't be added is not an option* — `(source: RecurringOrderSection.tsx)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Place it after the schedule/delivery section of the order sheet.
- ✅ Store the frequency itself and its `scheduleSummary` for lists.
- ✅ Use violet only for picks, the greyscale segment for Days|Date.
- ❌ A "Recurring order" checkbox with a Frequency dropdown beside it (the old pattern).
- ❌ A second violet, brand orange, or black for picked days.
- ❌ Fixed px widths on the Repeat fields.
- ❌ Showing it on service orders, Sales orders, movement orders, or portal orders made by a rule.

## Checks
- `fields-fill` — fixed-width containers holding the Repeat fields.
- `tokens`, `wcag-contrast` — accent-violet pair.
- `parity-sheets` — same section on create and edit.
- By eye: dashed add row when off, bordered box + minus when on, badges stretch across the days column, picks violet.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders › Delivery Order sheet — "Recurring Order" section.
- Customer Portal › Orders › order sheet — "Recurring order" section.
- Frequency unit alone: 3PL › Recurring Orders › Create rule; Customer Portal › Recurring Orders › rule sheet ("Frequency", with Next 4 deliveries).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/standard-list.md ===== -->

# Standard list

`id: unit.standard-list` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/StandardList.tsx (StandardList; re-exported as PortalList in prototypes/customer-portal/PortalList.tsx), prototypes/unified-view/service/ServiceListScreen.tsx (ServiceListScreen, printedText)` · `registry: fpds-standard-list`

## Purpose
A template-level composition written as a unit card — the whole list page in one piece, built from `unit.view-row`, `unit.toolbar`, `unit.filter-panel`, `unit.chip-shelf`, `unit.view-bar`, `unit.data-table`, `unit.export-sheet` and `unit.pagination`. Every table page, assembled the same way: the view row, the toolbar, the chip shelf, the view bar on the table, pagination, and an export that asks first. A screen hands it rows, columns, what each column prints, its facets and what Create and opening a row do. It answers "show me this kind of record as a list I can search, filter, sort, save and export" — identically on every page.

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use for any page whose content is one table of one kind of record with no board or map: Customer Portal lists, Service Work reference pages.
- Use `ServiceListScreen` (a thin wrapper) for a reference page whose filters should come from its own columns automatically.
- Don't use when the list needs a board, a map, a date window, Group by or bulk selection — those lists compose the units directly (3PL › Delivery Orders, Sales › Orders, Service Work › Service Orders, Customer Portal › Orders).
- Don't use inside a side sheet or for an editable table (boxed table unit, ui §10f).

## Anatomy
Top to bottom, with its fixed vertical rhythm:
1. *(optional)* **Above** — overview cards (`above`), 32px (`mb-8`) above the view row.
2. **View row** (`unit.view-row`) — All · saved views · + View · [segment] · [Create CTA] · Export.
3. **Toolbar shelf** (`mt-4`) — `unit.toolbar`: search · Filters [· afterFilters]; no Group by, no date, no display switch (one view). Then `unit.chip-shelf` with the column-search chip inside it.
4. *(optional)* **Banner** (`mt-4`) — the alert banner, under the toolbar shelf, right above the table.
5. **Table area** (`mt-4`, fills the height) — `unit.data-table` with `unit.view-bar` as its top bar ("62 invoices"; Search by column · Show/hide columns). OR the **empty state** in its place.
6. **Pagination** (`mt-4`) — `unit.pagination`, 25 rows a page by default.
7. **Export sheet** — `unit.export-sheet`, titled "Export {Noun}".

## Look
Inherits every unit's look. Its own values:
- Page column: `flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col`; spacing 16px (`mt-4`) between view row → toolbar shelf → [banner] → table → pagination; 32px under `above`.
- Empty state: DS `Empty` filling the table area — icon tile (`EmptyMedia variant="icon"`), title, optional description, one outline `size="sm"` button.

## States
- **default** — All, first page of 25, unsorted, every non-default-hidden column shown.
- **narrowed** — search, column search, column boxes or facets on; chips show; statement count drops.
- **sorted** — by a column's `sortValue` (numbers, lifecycle position) or its printed text, case-insensitive.
- **empty — none yet** — no rows at all: the page's own icon, "No {noun} yet", and the CTA (when the list has one).
- **empty — nothing matches** — rows exist but the narrowing leaves none: `SearchX` icon, "No matching {noun}", "{N} {noun} in all. Clear the search or a filter to see them.", button "Clear all".
- **empty with column boxes open** — the table stays (header + boxes, no rows) instead of the empty state.
- **dirty** — any change from the active view; update icon / dot on the view row.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Type in search | Rows whose printed text in any labeled column contains it; page 1. |
| Filters → Apply | Facets AND across, OR within one; page 1; chips appear. |
| Search by column → type in a box | That column's printed text must contain it; AND across boxes; page 1. |
| Close the column search row | Clears every box. |
| Sort a header | asc → desc → unsorted; page 1. |
| Hide / show / reorder / resize columns | Applied at once; view becomes dirty. |
| Click a row / Enter | `onOpen(row)` — the record's sheet (no-op when the list opens nothing yet). |
| Press Create | `onCreate()`. |
| Press Export → Export | Toast "Exporting {N} {noun} as .{format}" (one line), sheet closes. |
| Press "Clear all" in the empty state | Clears search, column search, column boxes and facets; page 1. |
| Press the CTA in the "none yet" state | `onCreate()`. |
| Banner title press (via `applyFacets`) | Sets the facet filter from outside; page 1. |
| Save / switch / update a view | Restores display, search, sort, hidden columns, page size, column search and boxes (row reopens if any box has text), column order and widths, facets. |
| Leave and come back | Same state (per `storageKey`, this session). |

## Outcomes
- A searched, filtered, sorted, paged list; matching text marked in cells.
- Saved views per list; state remembered for the session.
- A record's sheet or create flow opening.
- An export toast; nothing exported at 0 rows.
- One of two empty states, each with its one next action.
- `onFacetsChange(ids)` reported on every facet change (a banner knows its filter is on).

## Data contract
- Rows & columns: `rows`, `columns` (must include the `actions` key, which is dropped), `defaultHidden?`, `rowId`, `renderCell`, `text(row, key)` (what a column PRINTS — searched, boxed, sorted), `sortValue?(row, key)`.
- Facets: `facets`, `facetValues(row, facetKey)` (several for a many-valued field).
- Words: `noun` (plural), `nounOne`, `searchPlaceholder`, `ctaLabel?` + `onCreate?` (omit both for a list the user doesn't create in, e.g. Invoices), `openLabel(row)`, `emptyIcon`.
- Persistence: `storageKey` (one per list).
- Slots: `above?`, `banner?`, `afterFilters?`; `segments?` / `segmentValue?` / `onSegmentChange?`.
- Outside control: `applyFacets?` `{ids, n}` (n changes per press), `onFacetsChange?(ids)`. `onOpen?(row)`.
- **ServiceListScreen**: `rows`, `columns`, `rowId`, `renderCell`, `sortValue`, `searchPlaceholder`, `noun`, `icon`, `above?`, `valuesOf?(row, key)` (several values in one cell, e.g. skills). It derives `text` from what `renderCell` prints (`printedText`), `nounOne` from `noun`, `storageKey` `service-{noun}`, `openLabel` "Open {id}", and the facets (Rules 3).

## Rules
1. **Every table page is the standard list**: the view row (All, saved views, Export), search + Filters, the chip shelf, the view bar, the table, pagination. — *one list in this app, not a second thing to learn.* — `(source: ui §10p "Every table page is the standard list", Pavan 2026-10-22; ui §10c)`
2. **Take the composition, not just the components** — the `mt-4` shelf for toolbar + chips, `mt-4` for the table and for pagination. — *bare parts sat flush and read as a crowded band.* — `(source: ui §10c "The composition is part of the component")`
3. **Reference-page filters come from their own category columns**: 2–12 distinct values shared by several rows; not the first column (the record's name/number); not numbers, amounts or dates. — *no page is left without a filter; numbers and dates are what search and the column boxes are for.* — `(source: ui §10p; ServiceListScreen.tsx, 2026-10-22)`
4. **A column box and search match what the cell PRINTS**, not the sort value. — *a date sorts by 2026-09-24 and reads "Sep 24, 2026"; the reader types what they read.* — `(source: ServiceListScreen.tsx printedText)`
5. **One view, so no view switch; no Group by.** — `(source: ui §10h; StandardList.tsx)`
6. **Two empty states with different next actions**: none yet → the CTA; nothing matches → Clear all, with how many exist in all. — *one shared sentence fits neither situation.* — `(source: interactions 2-rules §4c; StandardList.tsx)`
7. **Every change to the narrowing resets to page 1.** — `(source: docs/unified-view/3-interactions.md §7)`
8. **No CTA where the user doesn't create** (Customer Portal › Invoices). — `(source: StandardList.tsx; ui §10c)`
9. **A saved view stores the display it opens in** (`mode`), with everything else. — `(source: ui §10p)`
10. **Banner under the toolbar shelf, right above the table — every module** (Pavan, 2026-10-25).

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Hand it `text` that returns exactly what the cell shows.
- ✅ Give each list its own `storageKey`.
- ✅ Use `ServiceListScreen` for Service reference pages so facets stay automatic.
- ❌ Rebuild a table page from parts when it is table-only — drift starts there.
- ❌ Add a board, map or Group by to it — compose the units directly instead.
- ❌ Facets on the first column, on amounts or on dates.

## Checks
- `parity-lists` G1–G8, G19 (StandardList satisfies them once for every page that uses it).
- `fields-fill` / `tokens` on any `above` content.
- `fpds-units` — the seven parts in order with `mt-4` rhythm.
- By eye: open a reference page beside 3PL › Delivery Orders — same rows, same order, same spacing.

## Reference
- Customer Portal › Invoices (segment Invoices · Statement, no CTA), › Sites & Assets (Ship-tos "Create Ship-to", Assets "Create Asset"), › Team & Access ("Invite user"), › Recurring Orders ("Create Rule", Recommendations in `afterFilters`).
- Service Work › Price Book (jobs), › Parts, › Agreements, › Maintenance (plans), › Compliance (items), › Performance, › Technicians — via `ServiceListScreen`.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/status-and-awaiting.md ===== -->

# Status & awaiting pills

`id: unit.status-and-awaiting` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/statusKind.ts, src/components/ui/status-chip.tsx, src/components/ui/pill.tsx, prototypes/customer-portal/cells.tsx (AwaitingPill, FlagsCell, RulePill)` · `registry: fpds-status-and-awaiting`

## Purpose
Says what state a record is in, as its word in a pill coloured by what the reader should do about it. The Awaiting pill says a customer's ask (change or cancellation) is waiting on the tenant, without inventing a status. Flags say what is wrong or about to be.

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use the **status pill** (`StatusChip`, default variant) in every status column, sheet header, board card badge and map list row.
- Use a **badge pill** (`Pill kind=…`) for flags and tags on a card: Urgent, Over SLA, Needs approval, Recurring, "Best price", "+N".
- Use the **Awaiting pill** beside the status when a Customer Portal order carries a `changeRequest`.
- Use **Flags as plain coloured text** in the Customer Portal Orders Flags column.
- Don't use `variant="dot"` unless a dense grid is asked for it by name.
- Don't use a pill for button / nav counters (`BadgeNumber`), the board lane count, or type initials (`CodeDisc`).
- Don't make "Change requested" / "Cancellation requested" a status or a lane — use the Awaiting pill.

## Anatomy
1. **Status pill** — the status word, truncating, inside the pill shape.
2. **Awaiting pill** — "Awaiting confirmation" or "Awaiting cancellation", a caution status pill that is a press target; opens a breakdown card:
   - Title "Change requested" / "Cancellation requested".
   - Row 1: what was asked (sub-line: the customer's note).
   - Row 2: "Sent to <tenant>" (sub-line: date · waiting for their confirmation).
3. **Flags cell** (CP) — most serious flag's word in its level color + a neutral "+N" pill; press opens "Flags (N)" with each flag and its detail.
4. **Rule pill** (CP) — repeat icon + rule number (R-xxxx) on an order a rule made.

## Look
- Pill shape (`PILL_SHAPE`): `inline-flex h-7 rounded-full px-3 text-xs font-normal whitespace-nowrap`, no border.
- Kind colors (`KIND_PILL`): neutral `bg-muted text-muted-foreground` · info `bg-fp-banner-accent-info/10 text-fp-banner-accent-info` · caution `bg-fp-banner-accent-caution/10 text-fp-banner-accent-caution` · success `bg-status-success/10 text-status-success` · critical `bg-status-failed/10 text-status-failed`.
- Chosen color (Sales stages / order types only): DS lane palette over the kind — `bg-fp-lane-<hue>/12` (yellow /18, orange /15) with text mixed from the hue and `--foreground`; violet = `bg-accent-violet-soft text-accent-violet-ink`.
- Dot variant: `size-1.5` dot (`bg-status-success` / `-processing` / `-failed` / `bg-muted-foreground` / `bg-fp-banner-accent-info`) beside a `text-sm` neutral word.
- Flags text: critical `text-status-failed` · warning `text-status-processing` · info `text-foreground`.
- Rule pill: `Pill color="purple"`, `Repeat` icon size-3.
- Awaiting breakdown card: 320px wide.

## States
- **Kinds** — caution (waiting on somebody, amber) · info (simply true, blue) · success (done, green) · critical (a warning, red) · neutral (neither; Draft, Canceled — gray).
- **Awaiting pill** — present only while the ask is open; hover = press target, focus ring from `BreakdownPopover`; open = card above the cell.
- **No flags** — the cell shows "—" in muted text.
- **Truncated** — long words truncate inside the pill (`max-w-full`).

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press the Awaiting pill | Opens the card: what was asked, the note, when it was sent and that it waits for the tenant. Row is not opened. |
| Press the Flags cell | Opens "Flags (N)" with every flag (most serious first) and its detail. |
| Hover a "+N" pill in a `PillList` | Hover card lists every value as pills. |
| Customer sends "Request change" / "Cancel order" on a sent order | Order keeps its status (Unassigned / Scheduled) and gains the Awaiting pill in the table, a pill + caution strip ("View") on the board card, and a "… requested · awaiting confirmation" section in the sheet. |
| Status changes | The pill's word and kind color change with it; nothing else is added. |

## Outcomes
- The reader sees the state and whether it asks something of them, the same color in every module.
- An open customer ask is visible everywhere the order shows, without a new status.
- Late / at-risk / credit-hold / past-due facts show as flags (CP) or badges (board), not as statuses.
- Running late (CP) = planned date passed and not delivered (Unassigned, Scheduled, Out for delivery); it surfaces on the Dashboard as an Attention required tile, not as a status.

## Data contract
- `StatusChip` — `tone` (a DS channel: success · processing · failed · neutral · info; supplied from the module's kind map, never typed by hand), `variant` (`text` default = pill, `soft` = pill, `dot`), optional `color` (chosen hue, Sales).
- Modules declare `Record<Status, StatusKind>` through `statusTones()` (or `kindTone()`); `KIND_TONE` maps caution→processing, info→info, success→success, critical→failed, neutral→neutral. `ACTIVE_KIND(active)` → info / neutral.
- `Pill` — `kind` (neutral · info · caution · success · critical), optional `color`.
- `AwaitingPill({ r })` — needs `r.changeRequest {kind: change | cancel, what, note, at}` and `r.requestNo`; renders nothing without an ask. `awaitingLabel(r)` gives the label for board badges and strips.
- `FlagsCell({ r })` — `r.flags[] {kind, detail, level: critical | warning | info}`.

## Rules
1. **A status is coloured by its KIND — what the reader should do** — *a column is a place, not a state; every badge gray made a job waiting on parts look like a new job.* `(source: docs/ds/ui/2-rules.md §10b, 2026-09-23)`
2. **Modules declare kinds, never tones** — *a map written in tones is a map written in colors; that is how "Assigned" came out green, amber and blue in three modules.* `(source: ui §10b; statusKind.ts)`
3. **Being active is info, not success** — *nothing has been achieved by a customer existing; green stays for done.* `(source: ui §10b)`
4. **Canceled is not red; a decision is not a fault** — *Canceled is a terminal non-event; Paused is caution, Skipped neutral.* `(source: ui §10b)`
5. **Red only for a warning** — *types, categories, lanes and Canceled never red.* `(source: ui §11b, Pavan 2026-10-16)`
6. **A status is its word — no dot beside it** — *the dot repeats the tone and spends 12px of a control meant to be small; nothing is added to a status it was not asked for.* `(source: ui §11)`
7. **Every chip and badge is the one pill; every status column shows a pill** — *a status reads the same in a cell, a sheet header and a board card.* `(source: ui §11b, Pavan 2026-10-09)`
8. **Color is by kind except where the module lets the user choose** (Sales stages, order types) — *the chosen color is kept wherever the value shows.* `(source: ui §11b)`
9. **Render the absence** — *a chip every row carries ("Normal", a status repeating its lane) says nothing.* `(source: ui §10b; 1-components §5)`
10. **A change or cancellation ask is not a status** — *the order stays Unassigned / Scheduled and wears "Awaiting confirmation" / "Awaiting cancellation" until the tenant answers.* `(source: cells.tsx; memory project_customer_portal 2026-10-22)`
11. **CP flags are plain text coloured by level** (red critical · amber warning · plain note) — *Pavan's choice for the Flags column.* `(source: cells.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-13)`
12. **A new module adds its row to the binding table before it ships a badge** — *one table so a hue cannot mean two things.* `(source: docs/unified-view/1-components.md §5)`
13. **Don't style plain text as pills** (products, driver names) — *Pavan rejected that; apply the style only where chips already exist.* `(source: memory feedback_pills_default)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ `<StatusChip tone={MODULE_STATUS_TONE[status]}>{status}</StatusChip>` with the map built by `statusTones()`.
- ✅ Put the Awaiting pill right after the status pill in the same cell.
- ✅ Keep the label spelling the status out — color is never the only carrier.
- ❌ `tone="processing"` or a ternary of tones written at a call site in Unified View.
- ❌ A dot + coloured word, or coloured text alone, in a status column.
- ❌ A red Canceled, a red type, a red lane that is just a place.
- ❌ A "Change requested" status, lane or filter value.

## Checks
- `status-kinds` — fails a Unified View chip or map that names a tone (scans `prototypes/unified-view`; the Customer Portal is not scanned, and `AwaitingPill` passes `tone="processing"` directly).
- `wcag-contrast` — pill text on its tint; `tokens`.
- By eye: same status, same color across 3PL, Sales, Service, CP; no dots; Awaiting pill opens its card; flags most-serious first.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders — Status column, sheet header, board badges.
- Sales › Fuel Orders — chosen stage colors (Sales Settings → Workflow).
- Service Work › Service Orders — Status column, board.
- Customer Portal › Orders — Status column with Awaiting pill, Flags column, Repeats rule pill, board caution strip.


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/summary-rail.md ===== -->

# Summary rail & activity

`id: unit.summary-rail` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/SheetCards.tsx` · `registry: fpds-summary-rail`

## Purpose
The read-only right column of a record sheet: the **Summary card** shows what the form computes (totals, counts, the headline figure), the **Activity card** shows the record's history. It answers "what has this record become?" and "what happened to it, when, by whom?".

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a record sheet has a derived side — figures computed from what is typed, or a history.
- Use `RailCard` + `RailRow` for other read-only facts in the rail (e.g. Service Work "Record", "Payment", "Visits").
- Don't use while CREATING a record — create sheets have no rail (exception: the Customer Portal order sheet keeps its summary on the right while creating).
- Don't put editable values in the rail — they belong in the form (`unit.record-sheet`).
- Don't build a bespoke summary block or activity list — always these two cards.

## Anatomy
1. **Rail** — `aside`, 340px, sticky at the top of the sheet body, cards stacked 24px apart.
2. **SummaryCard** — tinted frame · title on the frame (e.g. "Quotation summary", "Quote summary", "Order summary", "Summary") · one inner card holding label · value rows (`RailRow` `line`), a dashed divider, then the headline figure last (`RailRow` `total`).
3. **RailRow** — label (`dt`) · value (`dd`); with a `breakdown`, the value is dotted-underlined and a tooltip on the left lists each contribution.
4. **RailCard** — bordered block with a header band (icon + title, optional action) and a flush body of rows.
5. **ActivityCard** — a `RailCard` titled "Activity" with the history icon; a timeline: mark (round icon) · what changed · "date, time · who"; a line joins the marks.

## Look
- SummaryCard frame: `rounded-xl p-1`, gradient from `--pastel-violet` and `--pastel-rose` into `--fp-surface-1a` (dark: the `-foreground` pastels); title `text-base font-semibold text-foreground`, `px-3 pt-2 pb-2.5`; inner card `rounded-lg border border-border bg-fp-surface-1b p-3`, `gap-3`.
- `fill` SummaryCard: grows to the column's height (`min-h-64 flex-1`), inner card scrolls; what should sit at the bottom takes `mt-auto`.
