- Divider between line rows and total: `border-t border-dashed border-border`.
- RailRow `rail`: `px-4 py-2.5`, label `text-xs text-muted-foreground`, value `text-sm text-foreground`. `line`: `py-1`, label `text-sm text-foreground`, value `text-sm text-muted-foreground`. `total`: label `text-base font-medium`, value `text-base font-semibold`, wraps rather than cutting the label (figure drops under it). Values `tabular-nums`.
- Breakdown affordance: `underline decoration-dotted decoration-muted-foreground underline-offset-4`, `cursor-help`; tooltip `side="left"`, `max-w-xs`.
- RailCard: `rounded-lg border border-border`; header band `border-b border-border bg-fp-surface-1a px-4 py-2.5`, title `text-sm font-semibold text-muted-foreground`, icon 14px.
- Activity marks: 28px round (`size-7`), border + 10% fill by kind — money/quote and created: `status-success`; sent/invited/email: `fp-banner-accent-info`; cancel/remove/delete/block: `status-failed`; everything else: `border-border bg-card text-muted-foreground`. Connector `w-px bg-border`. Change `text-sm text-foreground`; stamp `text-xs text-muted-foreground`.

## States
- **summary, empty** — rows show "—"; a caller may put a one-line state note inside (3PL: no quotes yet).
- **summary, filled** — rows + total.
- **summary, fill** — stretches to the rail's height (Customer Portal order sheet, every state).
- **row with breakdown** — dotted underline; hover/focus shows the tooltip.
- **activity, empty** — "Nothing has changed yet."
- **activity, entries** — the six latest saves, most recent first, one line per change.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Hover / focus a dotted-underlined figure | Tooltip to the left lists how the figure adds up (label · value per line) |
| Hover a figure with no breakdown | Nothing (no underline, no cursor change) |
| Scroll the form | Rail stays in place (sticky) |
| Sheet narrower than `lg` | Rail folds under the form, one column |
| Caller's tabs inside the summary (3PL quotes) | Figures switch to the chosen quote |
| Press a link value (e.g. Invoice number) | Opens that record (caller-supplied) |

## Outcomes
- A reader sees the computed figures and how each adds up without leaving the sheet.
- A reader sees who changed what and when (six latest saves).
- Nothing changes on the record — the rail is read-only.

## Data contract
- `SummaryCard { title, children, fill? }`.
- `RailRow { label, value, breakdown?: {label, value}[], variant?: "rail" | "line" | "total" }`.
- `RailCard { title, icon?, action?, children }`.
- `ActivityCard { entries?: { at (ISO), by, changes: string[] }[], timeZone? }` — stamps printed "Mon D, YYYY, h:mm"; `timeZone` from Sales Preferences.
- No callbacks; read-only.

## Rules
1. **One summary card, one activity card, in every module's record sheet** — never a bespoke version — *consistency is the default; Quote's price and activity were once rebuilt as new blocks* — `(source: docs/ds/ux/2-rules.md; Pavan 2026-10-07; feedback_shared_sheet_cards)`.
2. **Editable left, derived right** — *an editable number and a computed one in one column look identical* — `(source: ux/2-rules two-column sheet)`.
3. **The rail is sticky** — *it answers questions the reader has while looking at any part of the form* — `(source: ux/2-rules; unified-view/2-layouts §7)`.
4. **The headline figure is last, and a figure has a name** (Total, Estimated, Margin…) — `(source: ux/2-rules "A figure has a name")`.
5. **The dotted underline appears only where a breakdown exists** — *an affordance is a promise* — `(source: SheetCards RailRow; unified-view/3-interactions §4)`.
6. **A total never cuts its label** — the figure drops under it — `(source: SheetCards.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-20)`.
7. **Activity = meaningful events only, latest first; draft edits not logged; a note change is logged, not its text** — *an entry per pause buries the ones that matter* — `(source: feedback_ui_rules; 3-interactions §2)`.
8. **What changed leads, who and when follow** — *the reader is looking for the event* — `(source: SheetCards ActivityCard)`.
9. **No rail while creating** — `(source: feedback_ui_rules)`; the Customer Portal order sheet keeps its summary on the right while creating — `(source: project_customer_portal 2026-10-13)`.
10. **Customer Portal order summary fills the rail's full height in every state**; trip + activity keep their own height below — `(source: project_customer_portal 2026-10-20)`.
11. **One highlighted card per sheet** — the tinted SummaryCard goes to the single most important read-out (the order/quote summary, or what needs attention); every other rail card is a plain RailCard — `(source: UX-161, Pavan 2026-10-26; eval sheets)`
12. **RailCard headers are quiet** — no fill band; title 14px medium muted with its icon; a hairline under it — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
13. **Notes above Activity; Activity is the last card** — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`
14. **The rail ends level with the form's last unit** — pinned, capped to the scroller, its foot aligned with the last unit on the left (the form's 40px foot room below both); what doesn't fit scrolls inside the rail — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Give every computed figure a `breakdown` when it is made of parts.
- ✅ Show the same figure the form shows — one number for one thing.
- ✅ Use `RailCard` for other read-only fact groups in the rail.
- ❌ A local summary box or a `RailCard` titled as a summary.
- ❌ An activity list drawn by hand.
- ❌ Inputs in the rail.
- ❌ A dotted underline on a plain figure.

## Checks
- `parity-sheets` — G29 summary must be `SummaryCard`, not a `RailCard`; G30 history must be `ActivityCard`.
- `tokens`, `wcag-contrast`.
- By eye: rail stays put while the form scrolls; total is last; tooltips line up with the form's tables.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders — order sheet "Quotation summary" + Activity (`threepl/BrokerOrderSheet.tsx`) — the source shell.
- Sales › Quotes — "Quote summary" (`QuoteSheet.tsx`); Sales › Orders (`OrderSheet.tsx`).
- Service Work › Service Orders — "Summary", Payment, Record, Assessment, Visits, Activity (`service/ServiceOrderSheet.tsx`).
- Customer Portal › Orders — "Order summary" (`fill`), "Trip", Activity (`customer-portal/OrderRequestSheet.tsx`); dashboard action sheet (`customer-portal/ActionSheet.tsx`).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/toolbar.md ===== -->

# Toolbar

`id: unit.toolbar` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ListToolbar.tsx (ListToolbar, IconTool), src/components/tables-layout/GroupByMenu.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/DateFilter.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/threepl/DateRangeFilter.tsx` · `registry: fpds-toolbar`

## Purpose
The list's control row (Pavan's original "Control unit"): narrow the list on the left (search, Filters, Group by), scope and present it on the right (date, display switch). It answers "how do I find the rows I want, and how do I want to see them?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a page shows a list — always directly under `unit.view-row`, inside one `mt-4` shelf together with `unit.chip-shelf`.
- Use the date control only on a list whose rows sit on a date (3PL › Delivery Orders, Sales › Orders, Service Work › Service Orders, Customer Portal › Orders). Omit it on lists with no date (Customers, Parts…).
- Use the display switch only when a second display (board or map) exists.
- Don't use for the view's own tools (Search by column, Show/hide columns, Card configuration) — they live in `unit.view-bar`.
- Don't use inside a side sheet — a sheet's tables use the boxed table unit (ui §10f).

## Anatomy
LEFT cluster (`gap-3`), in this order:
1. *(optional)* **Quick filters** (`quickFilters`) — a joined segmented control, before search.
2. **Search** — 320px field, leading `Search` glyph, trailing clear `X` while it has text; `aria-label` "Search all columns".
3. **Filters** — the `unit.filter-panel` trigger (outline button, `ListFilter` icon, applied count bubble).
4. *(optional)* **After filters** slot (`afterFilters`) — e.g. Recommendations.
5. **Group by** — `GroupByMenu`, only when the screen passes real dimensions and the view is not a board.

RIGHT cluster (`gap-1`):
6. **Date control** — `dateControl` (3PL-style `DateRangeFilter`: ‹ range ›) or `dateScope` (`DateFilter`: ‹ one day ›); then a 12px spacer.
7. **Display switch** — `IconTool`s: `Table2` "Table view", `KanbanSquare` "Board view", `Map` "Map view" — only those the list has.
8. *(legacy, only when no view bar is given)* the active view's tools routed under its icon by an L-shaped line; for a one-view list, the table tools inline in the row.

## Look
- Row: `flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3` — **top-aligned**, so the search row stays level with the view icons.
- Every control is 32px (`h-8` / `size="sm"` / `icon-sm`); quick filters match (`h-8`).
- Search: fixed `w-[320px]`, `pl-8`, glyph `size-4 text-muted-foreground`; clear `X size-3.5`.
- Filters button: `variant="outline" size="sm"`; count bubble `rounded-full bg-accent px-1.5 text-xs tabular-nums`.
- Display icons: `variant="ghost" size="icon-sm"` with tooltip; active icon on the chip fill (`bg-chip text-foreground`) — `--chip`, not `--accent`.
- Space between date and display switch: 12px spacer + the row's 4px gaps = 20px — space, not a rule.
- Date stepper: arrows `variant="outline" size="icon-sm"`, label `variant="outline" size="sm"`.
- Routed tools (legacy): line `h-4 w-3 rounded-bl-md border-b border-l border-muted-foreground/40` dropping from the active icon's center.

## States
- **default** — empty search, Filters with no count, active display icon on chip fill.
- **hover / focus** — DS button states; tooltips on display icons; `focus-visible:ring-2 ring-ring/50` on the clear button.
- **active** — search has text → clear `X` appears; Filters shows the applied count; Group by shows its active stack (from `GroupByMenu`).
- **selected** — the current display icon.
- **disabled** — date stepper arrows are disabled while the window is "All dates".
- **hidden (not disabled)** — Group by on a board; display switch on a one-view list; date control on a dateless list.

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Type in search | Narrows rows whose printed columns contain the text (case-insensitive); page resets to 1; matches are marked in cells (`Hit`). |
| Press search's `X` | Clears the search. |
| Press a quick-filter segment | Switches the set being searched/filtered inside. |
| Press **Filters** | Opens `unit.filter-panel`. |
| Press **Group by** → pick a dimension | Groups rows into headed sections (ordered stack, max 2 levels); pagination gives way to `GroupedListFooter`. |
| Press ‹ / › on the date stepper | Moves the window back/forward by its own length (a day steps a day, a week a week). |
| Press the date label | Opens `unit.date-picker` (range form for `DateRangeFilter`, one-date form for `DateFilter`). |
| Press **Table view / Board view / Map view** | Switches the display; the view bar's tools change with it (column tools on table, Card configuration on board, none on map); Group by hides on board. |

## Outcomes
- A narrowed list (search), with page reset to 1 and matched text marked.
- An open filter panel or date picker.
- A grouped table with group headers and the grouped footer.
- A different date window — the list, the view statement and counts follow.
- A different display (table / board / map).
- Nothing changes on a dateless list (no date control) or a one-view list (no switch) — those controls are not drawn.

## Data contract
- Search: `searchValue`, `onSearchChange`, `searchPlaceholder` (names what is searched — "Search orders, customers, partners…").
- `filters` — the screen's `ListFilters` node. `quickFilters?`, `afterFilters?`.
- `groupBy?` `{dimensions, value, onChange}`; `showGroupBy` (false on board).
- `view`, `onViewChange`; `boardView` (default false), `mapView` (default false).
- `dateControl?` (node; wins) or `dateScope?` `{date, onDateChange}`.
- Column/card tool props (`columns`, `hiddenColumns`, `onToggleColumn`, `onShowAllColumns`, `columnOrder`, `onReorderColumns`, `searchFields`, `colScope`, `colQuery`, `columnFiltersOpen`, `onToggleColumnFilters`, `columnFilterCount`, `cardFields`) are passed here but render in `unit.view-bar` when `viewToolsInto` + `viewStatement` are given.

## Rules
1. **Left narrows, right scopes and presents.** Quick filters lead the left, before search — *they decide WHICH SET you then search inside.* — `(source: ui §10c "Quick filters", 2026-09-21)`
2. **Search is the DS's 320px on every list.** — *it is not a row of fields; the same width everywhere.* — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx)`
3. **Group by only when the screen hands it real dimensions; off on the board.** — *a board is already grouped by lane; a menu with nothing behind it is a dead control.* — `(source: ui §10; parity G8, Pavan 2026-10-07)`
4. **One view, no view switch.** A table-only list shows no Table icon. — *a switch with one position switches nothing.* — `(source: ui §10h, 2026-10-01)`
5. **Board and map are switched on by flag, never offered by default.** — *a dead third option teaches people to ignore the switch.* — `(source: ui §10c; ListToolbar `mapView`/`boardView`)`
6. **Display icons are `Table2`, `KanbanSquare`, `Map`, the active one on the chip fill.** — *the pre-round design, restored 2026-10-22; the next design comes from Pavan.* — `(source: ui §10p; memory project_feedback_round_2026_10_21 point 4)`
7. **Top-aligned row.** — *the search row stays level with the view icons when tools hang beneath.* — `(source: ui §10p)`
8. **The date control is a stepper whose arrows move by the window's length.** — *stepping never silently changes how much you are looking at.* — `(source: DateRangeFilter.tsx)`
9. **20px of space, not a rule, between the date and the display switch.** — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-05)`
10. **A control the current view can't act on is hidden, not disabled.** — *a control on screen that does nothing teaches people to stop reaching for it.* — `(source: ui §10)`
11. **One gap, 12px, between every control group** — search ↔ Filters ↔ Group by, and date ↔ view switch — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-25; misses M-012)`
12. **The view switch is one bordered segmented control** — list · kanban (two upright columns side by side, a gap between) · map icons; the selected segment a grey fill — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-23)`
13. **Export is an outlined square as tall as the Create CTA** (24 × 24, the icon centred, the CTA's corner radius) — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-26)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Wrap toolbar + chip shelf in one `mt-4 shrink-0` shelf (ui §10c composition).
- ✅ Pass `viewToolsInto` + `viewStatement` so the column tools sit in the view bar.
- ❌ Show a lone Table icon on a table-only list.
- ❌ Leave Group by on with the demo dimensions (it groups nothing).
- ❌ Put Search by column / Show-hide columns on a board or map.
- ❌ Add a divider line between the date and the display switch.

## Checks
- `parity-lists` G2 (search by column is the filter row), G6 (Filters applies), G8 (Group by fed or off), G11 (board only where rendered).
- `fpds-units` — left/right order as in Anatomy.
- By eye: all controls 32px on one baseline; search 320px; active display icon on chip fill; nothing hanging under the icons when a view bar is used.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders (table · board, range date stepper, Group by) — the reference list.
- Sales › Orders (table · board · map, range date stepper).
- Service Work › Service Orders (board, range date).
- Customer Portal › Orders (table · board · map, Group by).
- 3PL › Recurring Orders, Customer Portal › Recurring Orders (Recommendations in `afterFilters`).
- Sales › Customers / › Assets (table only, no date); 3PL › Directory (no date; Vendors adds a map).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/view-bar.md ===== -->

# View bar

`id: unit.view-bar` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/ListToolbar.tsx (useViewBar, ViewStatement, BoardWithBar, BAR_TOOL, Reveal, viewToolsInto), prototypes/unified-view/ListTable.tsx (topBar)` · `registry: fpds-view-bar`

## Purpose
A strip ON the view — above the table header or above the board's lanes — that says what is shown ("62 orders · Thu, Oct 22, 2026") and carries that view's own tools. It answers "how many records am I looking at, over what window, and how do I adjust this view?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use on every list with a table or a board (ui §10p — every list).
- Don't use on a map view — the map has no view tools and the bar is not rendered for it.
- Don't use for filters, search or the date — those are `unit.toolbar`.
- Don't use inside a side sheet table.

## Anatomy
Left → right, in one 44px row:
1. **Statement** (`ViewStatement`) — the count + noun in medium ink, then the rest in muted: table "62 orders · Thu, Oct 22, 2026"; board "62 orders across 9 of 9 lanes · …"; a dateless list "150 customers".
2. **View tools** (right, 4px apart):
   - Table: **Search by column** (`TextSearch`) · **Show/hide columns** (`Settings2`).
   - Board: **Card configuration** (`LayoutList`).
   Each is icon-only; its name slides out on hover/focus (`Reveal`).
3. **Show/hide columns menu** — header "Columns" + shown/total, a list of every column in table order (grip + checkbox; child entries indented), footer **Show all**.
4. **Card configuration menu** — header "Card fields" + shown/total, optional lane scope picker ("All lanes" or one lane), one switch per category with its fields as indented checkboxes while the category is on, footer **Show all**.

Hosts: table → `ListTable topBar={bar.slot}` (inside the table frame, outside its scroll); board → `BoardWithBar barRef={bar.ref}` (the top strip INSIDE the board's box, like the table — 2026-10-25).

## Look
- Bar: `flex h-11 items-center justify-between gap-3 px-3`.
- In a table: the bar sits in the table's rounded frame, `border-b border-fp-outline-1 bg-fp-surface-1b`, above the sticky header.
- On a board: its own strip `rounded-md border border-border bg-fp-surface-1b`, 12px (`gap-3`) above the lanes.
- Statement: `text-sm text-muted-foreground truncate`; count part `font-medium text-foreground`, locale-formatted.
- Tool (`BAR_TOOL`): ghost `size="sm"` (32px), `px-2`, `text-xs font-normal text-muted-foreground`; **no fill and no outline in any state** (`hover:bg-transparent`, dark too); label gap animates `gap-0 → gap-1.5`, label width `max-w-0 → max-w-48`, opacity 0 → 1, 200ms. Tooltips are hidden in the bar (the label is the tooltip).
- Columns menu: `w-56 p-1`; count `rounded bg-fp-card-1 px-1.5 text-[11px] tabular-nums`; rows `py-1.5 text-sm`, hover `bg-accent`; grip `GripVertical size-3.5`, 20px column; child rows `pl-5 text-[13px] text-muted-foreground`; drop target tinted `bg-accent`, dragged row `opacity-40`.
- Card fields menu: `w-60 p-1`; categories are `Switch size="sm"`; fields are `Checkbox`, indented `pl-5 text-[13px]`.

## States
- **default** — icons only.
- **hover / focus-visible** — the tool's name slides out beside its icon, 12px muted gray (never black); still no fill.
- **on (stays revealed)** — Search by column while its row is open (`aria-pressed`); a tool while its menu is open (`aria-expanded`).
- **disabled** — *Show all* when nothing is hidden.
- **empty** — statement reads "0 orders …" when nothing matches (the bar stays; the table shows no rows while the column search row is open).

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Hover / focus a tool | Its name slides out. |
| Press **Search by column** | Opens the filter row under the table header (one "Filter…" box per column); the label stays out while open. |
| Press **Search by column** again | Closes the row and clears every box. |
| Press **Show/hide columns** | Opens the columns menu (aligned to the end). |
| Tick / untick a column | Shows / hides it in the table at once. |
| Drag a column's row by its grip, drop on another row | Moves the column to that place in the table. |
| ↑ / ↓ on a focused column row | Moves it one step. |
| Press **Show all** | Shows every hidden column. |
| Press **Card configuration** (board) | Opens the card fields menu. |
| Pick a lane in the scope picker | The ticks below now edit that lane's cards ("All lanes" = every lane). |
| Toggle a category switch | Shows / hides that block on the cards; its fields list appears/disappears. |
| Tick / untick a field | Shows / hides that line inside its block. |
| Change the list (search, filter, date, display) | The statement's count and window update. |

## Outcomes
- A statement that always matches what is on screen (filtered count, window, lanes with cards).
- The column search row open or closed (closing clears its boxes).
- Columns hidden, shown or reordered — the active view becomes dirty.
- Board cards showing more or fewer fields, per lane or for all lanes.
- Nothing hidden by *Hide all* — there is no Hide all.

## Data contract
- `const bar = useViewBar()` → `{into, ref, slot}`.
- Toolbar: `viewToolsInto={bar.into}` and `viewStatement={<ViewStatement count one many rest? />}`; the toolbar portals the table tools or board tools into the bar for `view === "table" | "board"`.
- Table: `ListTable topBar={bar.slot}`. Board: `<BoardWithBar barRef={bar.ref}>…lanes…</BoardWithBar>`.
- Table tools read toolbar props: `onToggleColumnFilters`, `columnFiltersOpen`, `columnFilterCount`, `columns` `{key,label,group?}`, `hiddenColumns`, `onToggleColumn`, `onShowAllColumns`, `columnOrder` + `onReorderColumns` (both → grips on).
- Board tool reads `cardFields` `{units, hidden, onToggle, onShowAll, scope?, scopes?, onScopeChange?}`.

## Rules
1. **Every list with a table or a board has a view bar; the view's tools live there, not under the view icons.** — *a control belongs to the view it acts on.* — `(source: ui §10p "THE VIEW BAR — every list", Pavan 2026-10-22, after a trial on 3PL › Delivery Orders)`
2. **The statement leads with the count, then the window**: "62 orders · Thu, Oct 22, 2026"; the board says "across N of M lanes" (it replaces the board's footer line); a dateless list is just "150 customers". — `(source: ui §10p)`
3. **Tools are icon-only; the name slides out on hover/focus and STAYS out while the tool is on; 12px muted gray, never black; no fill or outline, ever.** — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx BAR_TOOL, ui §10p)`
4. **Search by column and Show/hide columns sit 4px apart, like the view icons.** — `(source: ui §10p)`
5. **Column tools on the table only; Card configuration on the board only; nothing on the map.** — *one icon that changes its subject with the view is one icon nobody can read.* — `(source: ui §10)`
6. **Show all, and nothing facing it — no Hide all.** — *Hide all's only outcome is a screen with no data, one slip from the button people use.* — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx)`
7. **The columns menu follows the table's order and can reorder it.** — *the column you want is usually the one scrolled off the right edge.* — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx; parity G3)`
8. **Card fields: a switch per category, a tick per field, per lane or all lanes; no ordering.** — *the category is the decision; a card's order is its design.* — `(source: ListToolbar.tsx, Pavan 2026-09-23; ui §10)`
9. **Closing the column search row clears every box.** — *a narrowing with nothing on screen is not allowed.* — `(source: ui §10g)`
10. **On a board the view bar is the top strip of the board's own box** (`BoardWithBar`), a line under it — attached like the table's, never a separate box — `(source: Pavan 2026-10-25; misses M-011)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Wire all three: `useViewBar()`, `viewToolsInto` + `viewStatement`, `topBar` / `BoardWithBar`.
- ✅ Count the FILTERED rows in the statement.
- ❌ Give the tools a hover fill, an outline, or black text.
- ❌ Show tooltips on bar tools (the sliding name is the label).
- ❌ Hang the tools under the display icons with the routing line (pre-view-bar design).
- ❌ Add a Hide all.

## Checks
- `parity-lists` G2 (search by column is the filter row), G3 (column menu can reorder), G13 (card fields menu per lane / all).
- `fpds-units` — statement left, tools right, correct tools per view.
- `microcopy` — "Search by column", "Show/hide columns", "Card configuration", "Columns", "Card fields", "All lanes", "Show all".
- By eye: bar 44px; tools have no fill on hover; label stays while the row/menu is open; statement count equals the rows matched.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders (table and board; the trial screen).
- Sales › Orders (table, board "across N of M lanes · window").
- Service Work › Service Orders (board), Customer Portal › Orders (table, board).
- Sales › Customers ("150 customers"-style, no date), Service Work › Parts and the other reference pages, Customer Portal › Invoices (via `unit.standard-list`).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/units/view-row.md ===== -->

# View row

`id: unit.view-row` · `layer: unit` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/FuelOrdersViewBar.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/useSavedViews.ts, prototypes/unified-view/filterMemory.ts` · `registry: fpds-view-row`

## Purpose
The first row of every list page: which saved narrowing you are on (All or a view you made), a way to save the current one, and the list's own actions (Create, Export). It answers "which version of this list am I looking at, and how do I add to it or take it out?"

## Use when / Don't use when
- Use when a page shows a list of records (table, board or map) — every list page has one, at the top of the content layer.
- Use the Create CTA as a **menu** (`createMenu`) only when Create means several different records that take different forms (Sales › Orders: Fuel Order / Asset Order / Quote; 3PL › Delivery Orders: Delivery Order / Asset Order).
- Don't use for page-level actions that no list control scopes — those stay in the page header (ux "Action placement").
- Don't use the row for business / line-of-service presets — those are filters in the `unit.filter-panel` (ui §10p).
- Don't build a whole list from parts by hand when it is a plain table page — use `unit.standard-list`, which already contains this row.

## Anatomy
1. **All** tab — the resting state; holds the underline when no saved view is on.
2. **Saved view tabs** — one per view, in creation order. Each wrapper (`group/tab`) holds:
   - the view name button;
   - **update icon** (`RefreshCw`) — only on the active view while it has unsaved changes;
   - **⋯ menu** (`MoreHorizontal`) — revealed on hover: *Update view* (only when changed), *Rename*, *Delete*.
3. **DirtyDot** — on All only, when All has unsaved changes.
4. **+ View** — opens the *Save current view* dialog.
5. *(optional)* **Segment** (`segments`) — a joined toggle group for two KINDS of record (Customer Portal › Invoices: Invoices · Statement), then a 12px spacer.
6. **Create CTA** — `Button size="xs"` with `Plus` + label; or the same button with `ChevronDown` opening a `w-80` menu (entries: label + optional `detail` line; an entry with `choices` becomes a heading over its own entries).
7. **Export** — ghost `icon-xs` button with the `Upload` icon and an "Export" tooltip; always the rightmost item.
8. **Dialog** — title *Save current view* / *Rename view*; field *View name*; footer *Cancel* (outline) + *Save view* / *Rename*.

Code-supported but switched off by rule: `presets` (business tabs before a vertical rule) — see Rules 2.

## Look
- Row: full panel width (`-mx-4 -mt-4` cancels the content layer's 16px inset, re-padded `px-3`, `pt-0.5`), `border-b border-border` hairline, no bottom padding, `gap-1`.
- Tabs: `text-xs`, `px-3 py-2`, plain buttons with `border-b-2`. Active = `font-medium text-foreground` + `border-foreground` underline that lands ON the hairline (`self-end`). Inactive = `text-muted-foreground`, `hover:text-foreground`, transparent border.
- Update icon: 24px (`size-6`) square, `rounded-md`, icon `size-3.5`, `text-muted-foreground` (gray — never brand), `hover:bg-accent hover:text-foreground`; tooltip "Update changes".
- ⋯: `size-3.5` icon, `text-muted-foreground`, `opacity-0` until the tab is hovered, focused or its menu is open.
- DirtyDot: 6px (`size-1.5`) round, `bg-brand`.
- + View: `text-xs text-muted-foreground`, `Plus size-4`, `rounded-md px-2 py-1`, `hover:bg-accent`, 4px off the hairline (`mb-1`).
- Create CTA: `size="xs"` (24px tall), primary fill, radius a literal 4px (`rounded-[4px]` — the one value off the radius scale; flagged in code), 12px above the hairline (`self-end mb-3`).
- Export: `variant="ghost" size="icon-xs"` (24px), same baseline as the CTA, `ml-1.5` after the CTA, `mr-1` so its right edge lines up with the toolbar below.
- Segment (when used): `ToggleGroup variant="outline" size="sm"`, 28px (`h-7`), items `text-xs`, active `font-medium`.
- Dialog: `sm:max-w-sm`.

## States
- **default** — All underlined, no views.
- **hover** — inactive tab text goes to `text-foreground`; a saved tab reveals its ⋯.
- **focus** — `focus-visible:ring-2 ring-ring/50` on every button; ⋯ becomes visible on focus.
- **selected (active view)** — underline + medium weight on that tab only; only one tab is ever underlined.
- **dirty** — active saved view: gray update icon beside its name, *Update view* appears in its ⋯ menu. All: DirtyDot.
- **open** — ⋯ menu stays visible while open; Create menu open under the CTA, aligned to its end.
- **disabled** — dialog *Save view* is disabled when the box is empty AND there is no suggested name; *Rename* is disabled when the box is empty.
- **empty** — no saved views: All + "+ View" only. No CTA when the list has no create flow (no handler → no button).

## Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press **All** while on a saved view | Parks the view's unsaved changes, restores All's own working state (or the default), All takes the underline. |
| Press **All** while already on All | Drops All's unsaved changes — the list returns to its default state. |
| Press a **saved view** tab (another one) | Parks the current tab's changes, applies that view's working copy (or its saved state), view takes the underline. |
| Press the **saved view you are on** | Puts it back to its saved state — the one way to drop its changes. |
| Press the **update icon** (RefreshCw) | Overwrites the view with what is on screen; icon disappears (view is clean). |
| Hover / focus a saved tab | ⋯ fades in. |
| ⋯ → **Update view** | Same as the update icon. Only listed when the active view has changes. |
| ⋯ → **Rename** | Opens the dialog in rename mode, box filled with the current name. |
| ⋯ → **Delete** | Removes the view at once (destructive item). If it was active, All takes over. |
| Press **+ View** | Opens *Save current view*; the box is empty with a **suggested name** as placeholder (from the filters on), or "e.g. Unassigned today" when nothing is filtered. |
| → or Tab in the empty name box (when a suggestion exists) | Takes the suggestion into the box, followed by a space, to type on from. |
| Enter / *Save view* with an empty box and a suggestion | Saves the view under the suggested name. |
| Enter / *Save view* with a typed name | Creates the view from what is on screen and makes it active; the tab you left becomes clean (its changes moved into the new view). |
| *Cancel*, Esc or outside click in the dialog | Closes; nothing saved. |
| Press **Create** (button) | Calls the screen's create handler — usually opens the record's create sheet. |
| Press **Create ▾** (menu) → an entry | Starts that record's create flow in one click (no selection is held). |
| Press **Export** | Opens `unit.export-sheet` (never exports silently). |
| Change a **segment** | Switches the record kind shown; pressing the active segment again does nothing (never empty). |

## Outcomes
- A list restored to All / default, or to a saved view's state (filters, search, sort, hidden columns, column order and widths, page size, column search and boxes, group-by, date, display mode — whatever that screen's `ViewState` captures).
- A new named view, active, with the screen's current state.
- A renamed view; an updated view; a deleted view (All becomes active if it was the one deleted).
- Unsaved changes kept per tab for the session — leaving a page and coming back finds the same tab, dirty or not (`storageKey`); a page reload starts clean; a dashboard drill arriving with its own narrowing wins (`restore=false`).
- Nothing, when the dialog is canceled or the name and suggestion are both empty.
- A create sheet or flow opening; the export sheet opening.

## Data contract
- `views` — `{id, name}[]` (state is opaque to the row). `activeId` — `null` = All. `dirty` — current state differs from the active view (or from default).
- Callbacks: `onApply(id)`, `onResetToDefault()`, `onCreate(name)`, `onRename(id, name)`, `onUpdate(id)`, `onDelete(id)` — all supplied by `useSavedViews`.
- `useSavedViews({current, defaultState, keyOf, apply, storageKey?, restore?})` — `keyOf` serialises state deterministically (sets sorted, group-by order kept) so drift is not fooled by key order.
- CTA: `onCreateOrder` + `ctaLabel` (default "Create Order"), or `createMenu` `{key, label, detail?, choices?}[]` + `onCreateSelect(key)`.
- `onExport()`. Optional `segments` / `segmentValue` / `onSegmentChange`.
- Suggested name: `suggestViewName()` reads the filters the toolbar's `ListFilters` registered via `setActiveFilters` — no wiring between the two on the screen.

## Rules
1. **The first tab is "All", never "Default".** — *it says what you see: everything, nothing applied.* — `(source: docs/ds/ui/2-rules.md §10p, 2026-10-21)`
2. **No preset view tabs and no line-of-service segment in any module.** Line of Service, Business (3PL) and Order Kind (Sales) are facets in the Filters panel. — *users create their own views; a scope you can't see on the chip shelf is a narrowing you forget.* — `(source: ui §10p; memory project_feedback_round_2026_10_21 point 3)`
3. **A saved view stores its filters AND its arrangement AND its display (table / board / map), and opens in it.** — *a view that restored the narrowing but not the columns rebuilds half the thing.* — `(source: ui §10p; docs/unified-view/3-interactions.md §7)`
4. **A changed view shows a gray `RefreshCw` update icon beside its name, tooltip "Update changes"** — one press saves. — *the update is one press beside the name, not a dot that sends you to ⋯.* — `(source: FuelOrdersViewBar.tsx comment, Pavan 2026-10-21/22; ui §10p)`
5. **Save view suggests a name from the filters on**: business (or line of service when no business), then other facets in panel order; one value = the value, two = "A & B", more = "3 Status"; at most three parts joined " · ". → or Tab takes it; Save with the box empty saves it. — *naming is the friction that stops people saving views.* — `(source: ui §10p "Filters remember; views name themselves", filterMemory.ts, 2026-10-21)`
6. **Each tab keeps its own unsaved changes; pressing the tab you are on is the reset.** — *leaving a view and coming back finds it as it was left.* — `(source: useSavedViews.ts, Pavan 2026-09-24)`
7. **A list remembers where it was left for the session** — filters, search, sort, columns, date, active view, dirty state; only Clear all (or the view's reset) removes them. — `(source: docs/unified-view/3-interactions.md "A list remembers where it was left", 2026-10-09)`
8. **No create handler → no CTA.** — *a button that does nothing when pressed teaches people to ignore it.* — `(source: FuelOrdersViewBar.tsx; ui §10c)`
9. **Create is a menu only when it means different records with different forms**; entries carry names (a `detail` line only where names alone don't separate them — Sales' menu is names only). — `(source: FuelOrdersViewBar.tsx, Pavan 2026-10-05 / 2026-10-07)`
10. **CTA and Export sit at the right end of this row**, Export rightmost carrying `mr-1`. — *the CTA belongs with the scope that decides what it creates; the 4px corrects the row's inset so right edges line up with the toolbar.* — `(source: docs/ds/ux/2-rules.md "Action placement")`
11. **Export always goes through the Export sheet.** — *the moment data leaves is the moment the narrowing must be visible.* — `(source: ExportSheet.tsx; ui §10c)`

## Do / Don't
- ✅ Wire the row to `useSavedViews` with a `storageKey` per list.
- ✅ Let the active tab's 2px underline land on the hairline (`self-end`, no bottom padding).
- ✅ Keep the update icon gray (`text-muted-foreground`).
- ❌ Label the first tab "Default", or add business/LOS preset tabs (the `presets` prop still exists in code — don't pass it).
- ❌ Stub views with no-op handlers or unnamed "View 1, View 2" (parity G1).
- ❌ Put a dot instead of the update icon on a saved view, or color the icon brand.
- ❌ Make the CTA `sm` (32px) — it sits heavy on a row of text tabs.
- ❌ Add a toast on view save beyond one line (toasts rule).

## Checks
- `parity-lists` G1 — the saved-views row is real (handlers wired, not stubbed).
- `fpds-units` — anatomy present in order: All · views · + View · [segment] · CTA · Export.
- `microcopy` — "All", "+ View", "Save current view", "Rename view", "View name", "Save view", "Update changes", "Export".
- By eye: underline sits on the hairline; CTA and Export right edges line up with the toolbar's right edge below; only one tab underlined; update icon appears only on a changed active view.

## Reference
- 3PL › Delivery Orders (create menu with details), 3PL › Recurring Orders ("Create Rule"), 3PL › Directory (Customers, Vendors, Products, Rental Assets — Vendors has no CTA).
- Sales › Orders (create menu, names only), Sales › Quotes, Sales › Customers, Sales › Assets.
- Service Work › Service Orders ("New service order").
- Customer Portal › Orders, › Invoices (segment Invoices · Statement), › Sites & Assets, › Team & Access ("Invite user").
- Service Work › Price Book / Parts / Agreements / Maintenance / Compliance / Performance / Technicians (via `unit.standard-list`).


<!-- ===== docs/fpds/05-templates.md ===== -->

# FPDS — Templates

A template is a whole page or sheet: which units sit where, in what order, and what the
page as a whole does. Pick one in step **L2** of `00-protocol.md`; every region inside
it is a unit from `units/`. Each template names a **reference page** — the real screen
to copy when in doubt.

---

## List page

`id: template.list-page` · `layer: template` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/threepl/BrokerOrdersScreen.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/StandardList.tsx` · `registry: fpds-list-page`

### Purpose
Show many records of one kind, let the reader narrow, sort, group, save the narrowing as
a view, switch between table, board and map, open a record and act on many at once.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use when the job is "find / scan / work through records of one kind" — orders, quotes,
  customers, vendors, parts, invoices, agreements, technicians…
- Use the **table-only** variant (`StandardList`) when the records have no lifecycle
  lanes or places (reference lists: Price Book, Parts, Invoices, Team & Access).
- Don't use for a single record (→ Record sheet) or for figures over time (→ Dashboard).

### Anatomy
Top to bottom, always in this order:
1. **Page title** (`h1`) — the page's nav name.
2. **View row** (`unit.view-row`) — All · saved views · + View … Create · Export.
3. **Toolbar** (`unit.toolbar`) — search · Filters · Group by (where grouping exists) …
   date control (date-scoped lists) · display switch (Table / Board / Map).
4. **Chip shelf** (`unit.chip-shelf`) — only while something narrows the list.
5. **Alert banner** (`unit.alert-banner`) — only while something needs attention.
6. **The view**, with its **View bar** (`unit.view-bar`) on top:
   table (`unit.data-table`) · board (`unit.board`) · map (`unit.map`).
7. **Pagination** (`unit.pagination`) — tables only; boards state their total in the view bar.
8. **Bulk bar** (`unit.bulk-bar`) — floats in while rows are selected.

### Look
Content layer white (`bg-fp-surface-1b`) on the app ground; 16px page inset; units
stacked with 12–16px between them; the view takes the remaining height and scrolls inside
itself — the page itself never scrolls.

### States
default · narrowed (chips shown) · empty (no records on the date / no match — the
Empty state names why and offers the way out) · loading (skeleton rows/cards) ·
selecting (bulk bar) · saved view active (its tab underlined; the update icon when changed).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Type in search | List narrows as you type; page resets to 1 |
| Filters → tick → Apply | List narrows; chips appear; Recent history records the set |
| Display switch | Same records, other view; the view's own tools move into the view bar |
| Row / card press | The record opens in its Record sheet |
| + View → Save | The current filters, sort, columns and display become a named tab |
| Export | Export sheet with the current narrowing listed |

### Outcomes
A narrowed list; a saved view; an opened record; an export; a bulk change with a
one-line toast; an empty state that says why and how to undo it.

### Data contract
Records; columns (key, label, width from content); facets; what each column prints
(search and column boxes match it); sort values; the date window (if date-scoped);
lanes (if it has a board); places (if it has a map).

### Rules
1. **The order of the regions never changes** — *every list reads the same way, so a
   user who learnt one has learnt them all* (UX rules, Lists & views).
2. **No preset view tabs; All comes first** — *views are the user's, not the product's*
   (Pavan, 2026-10-21).
3. **A saved view stores its display** — *a board view opens as a board* (2026-10-21).
4. **The table always fills, everything left-aligned** — (DS ui §10p, 2026-10-22).

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Copy the reference page's spacing and order exactly.
- ❌ Don't add a second toolbar row, a tab strip of presets, or filters outside the panel.

### Checks
`parity-lists`, `parity-tables`, `fpds-units`, `table-rows`, `tokens`.

### Reference
3PL › Delivery Orders (table, board, map) · Sales › Orders · Service Work › Service Orders ·
Customer Portal › Orders · table-only: Service Work › Parts, Customer Portal › Invoices.

---

## Record sheet

`id: template.record-sheet` · `layer: template` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/RecordSheet.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/threepl/BrokerOrderSheet.tsx` · `registry: fpds-record-sheet-page`

### Purpose
Create, edit or read ONE record in a side sheet over its list, without leaving the list.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for every create and every edit — orders, quotes, customers, assets, service
  orders, rules, users — even for a record the product has never had before.
- Don't use for a dashboard drill (→ List page narrowed) or a bulk change (→ Bulk edit).

### Anatomy
1. **Header** — title (`Order #: …` / `Create …`), status or draft chip, record pills,
   ⋯ menu, close.
2. **Body** — sections in a fixed order (who/where → what → when → documents → notes),
   each a titled section; tables for anything repeated (`unit.lines-table`,
   `unit.documents-table`); the delivery timeline and recurring section where the
   record has a window or repeats.
3. **Rail** (340px, right) — `unit.summary-rail`: the summary card and the activity card.
4. **Footer** — pending strip (what is still missing), Save as draft, the primary act.

### Look
Width from its widest table (`fit`), else `form`; never wider than the window less 56px;
a sheet over a sheet takes the gap twice. Fields fill their row (24px gap).

### States
create · draft (autosaving) · sent/locked (read-only) · with an ask awaiting the other
side · loading · error on submit (pending strip names the missing fields).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Edit a field | Autosaves; the draft chip says so |
| Press the primary act with fields missing | The pending strip opens and names them; nothing is lost |
| ⋯ menu | Duplicate / order again, request change, cancel — whichever the record allows |
| Close | Closes; a new untouched draft is discarded, a touched one stays a draft |

### Outcomes
A record created or updated (one-line toast), a draft kept, an ask sent, a cancel.

### Data contract
The record; its sections; which tables it carries (`fit`); what is required; the acts
its status allows.

### Rules
1. **Every record sheet carries the summary card and the activity card** — *one shape
   for every record* (shared sheet cards, Pavan).
2. **Repeated inputs are a table, units inside the cell** — (table by default).
3. **A new field appears on every create, edit and list screen of that record** — (field reach).
4. **Lines tables end with Top off, then Qty** — (top off then qty).

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Open over the list it came from.
- ❌ Don't build a full-page form, a modal form, or a stepper outside the sheet.

### Checks
`sheets`, `parity-sheets`, `field-reach`, `fields-fill`, `boxed-tables`, `fpds-units`.

### Reference
3PL › Create Delivery Order · Service Work › Service order · Customer Portal › Order.

---

## Directory record

`id: template.directory-record` · `layer: template` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/threepl/DirectoryRecordSheet.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/threepl/CustomerRecord.tsx` · `registry: fpds-directory-record`

### Purpose
Read and keep one master-data record — a customer, vendor, product or rental asset —
with its properties, related records and activity.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for master data (customers, vendors, products, assets, ship-tos).
- Don't use for transactional records like orders (→ Record sheet).

### Anatomy
Header with name and status; property sections; related records (orders, ship-tos,
assets) as tables; the summary and activity cards in the rail.

### Look
As the Record sheet.

### States
read · editing a section · inactive record.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Edit a section | Autosaves |
| Press a related record | Opens that record's sheet over this one (level 2) |

### Outcomes
An updated master record; navigation to a related record.

### Data contract
The record, its sections, its related lists.

### Rules
1. **Related records open over, not instead** — *the record you came from stays in reach.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Reuse the Record sheet's header, rail and sections.
- ❌ Don't add charts or metric widgets (V1 decision, call 2026-10-22).

### Checks
`sheets`, `parity-sheets`, `fpds-units`.

### Reference
3PL › Directory › Customers / Vendors / Products / Rental Assets.

---

## Dashboard

`id: template.dashboard` · `layer: template` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/threepl/ThreePLDashboard.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/DashboardParts.tsx, prototypes/customer-portal/DashboardScreen.tsx` · `registry: fpds-dashboard-page`

### Purpose
Answer "how are we doing today, and what needs me" for one module, and send the reader
to the records behind every number.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for a module's landing figures.
- Don't use for one record's figures (→ Directory record) or for analytics (later phase).

### Anatomy
Date control top right; a row of stat tiles (`unit.dashboard`); "Attention required"
(`unit.alert-banner` / action tiles); cards with tables or charts below.

### Look
White cards on the dashboard ground; metric icon tiles; trend chips in the DS colors.

### States
today · another date · nothing needing attention (the section says so).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Press a tile, a bar, a row | The list behind it opens, already narrowed (drill-through) |

### Outcomes
A narrowed list; an action sheet for the item that needs attention.

### Data contract
Figures with their previous period; the drill (list + narrowing) behind each.

### Rules
1. **Every number opens the records behind it** — *a number you can't check is not trusted.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Use MetricIcon tiles and trend chips.
- ❌ Don't add custom chart colors.

### Checks
`tokens`, `wcag-contrast`, `fpds-units`.

### Reference
3PL › Dashboard · Sales › Dashboard · Customer Portal › Dashboard.

---

## Settings page

`id: template.settings` · `layer: template` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/SettingsPage.tsx, prototypes/unified-view/SalesSettingsScreen.tsx` · `registry: fpds-settings`

### Purpose
Set how a module behaves for the whole tenant.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for module-wide configuration.
- Don't use for personal preferences (→ Accessibility panel) or one record (→ Record sheet).

### Anatomy
Section list; each section a titled group of fields or an editable table.

### Look
As record-sheet sections, full page width.

### States
default · edited (autosaved).

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Change a setting | Autosaves; one-line toast where the change has a consequence |

### Outcomes
A changed setting.

### Data contract
The settings and their allowed values.

### Rules
1. **Settings autosave** — *there is no "Save settings" to forget.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Use editable tables for repeated settings.
- ❌ Don't hide settings behind modals.

### Checks
`fields-fill`, `boxed-tables`, `fpds-units`.

### Reference
Sales › Sales Settings · Service Work › Service Settings.

---

## App frame

`id: template.app-frame` · `layer: template` · `code: prototypes/unified-view/UnifiedView.tsx, prototypes/customer-portal/CustomerPortal.tsx` · `registry: fpds-app-frame`

### Purpose
The frame every page sits in: top bar, navigation, content layer.

### Use when / Don't use when
- Use for every page.
- Don't build a page without it.

### Anatomy
Top bar (logo, global search, notifications, profile) · navigation (`unit.app-shell`:
module, pages, Accessibility at the foot) · the content layer.

### Look
App ground behind; the content layer is the white rounded panel; one-panel sidebar.

### States
module loading (skeleton) · collapsed navigation.

### Interactions
| Trigger | Result |
|---|---|
| Choose a module or page | The content layer changes; the list keeps where it was left |
| Accessibility | The panel opens pinned at the navigation's foot |

### Outcomes
Navigation; personal display settings.

### Data contract
Modules and their pages.

### Rules
1. **The navigation order and the shell are locked** — *people find things by place.*

### Do / Don't
- ✅ Keep Accessibility at the foot of the navigation.
- ❌ Don't add a second navigation level outside the sidebar.

### Checks
`parity-shell`, `fpds-units`.

### Reference
Unified View (any page) · Customer Portal (any page).
